# gameplay: ancient_relics_events

Stellaris Cygnus v4.5.2 (9776). Extracted 2026-10-12. 1238 entries. Original raw strings retained.

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:2`

### English

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:3`

## crisis.250.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acrisis.250.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

虫后已经孕育出了一支新的索林原虫舰队，该舰队将环绕[Root.Capital.GetName]的轨道。当女王处于我们的控制下时，这些生物是绝对忠诚的，并会服从我们的全部命令。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:3`

### English

The Brood-Queen has spawned a new Prethoryn fleet which has assumed orbit over [Root.Capital.GetName]. With the Queen under our control, the creatures are utterly loyal and will obey our every command.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:4`

## crisis.252.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acrisis.252.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在最近于[battle_system.GetName]星系中与索林原虫的舰队交火中，我们的部队设法夺取了它们的一只虫后运送的某种卵。卵不久后就孵化了，显露出其中的§H虫后§!。\n\n我们的科学家使用神经递质已经安抚了这只虫后。它现在仿佛服从索林原虫蜂巢思维本体一样服从我们下达的命令。我们或许可以利用这只虫后培育一个属于我们自己的小型索林原虫虫群。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:5`

### English

In the recent fleet engagement against the Prethoryn in the [battle_system.GetName] system, our forces managed to capture some sort of egg that was being transported by one of their Queens. The egg hatched shortly afterwards, and a §HBrood-Queen§! emerged.\n\nThrough the use of neural transmitters, our scientists have managed to pacify the creature. It will now obey our commands as if they had been issued by the Prethoryn hive mind itself. We could potentially use this Queen to grow a small Prethoryn brood of our own.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:6`

## crisis.1250.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acrisis.1250.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

当最后一个维度传送门被摧毁后留下的能量旋涡最终消散时，我们的部队发现附近有一个虚弱的异次元体在飘荡。我们捕获了这个生物并进行了审讯。\n\n这显然是它们的一个“§H术士§!”，即便是以它们的标准来评判，它也是一位强大的异次元体。它负责维持我们和他们的现实之间的传送门。如果我们能够研究或者引用它的灵能，我们应该能够极大的提高我们的亚光速航速和跃迁引擎的跃迁范围。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:7`

### English

When the swirling energies left behind after the destruction of the last dimensional portal finally dissipated, our forces found a weakened extradimensional drifting nearby. The creature was captured and interrogated.\n\nIt is apparently one of their "§HWarlocks§!" - a powerful extradimensional even by their standards, responsible for maintaining the portal's link between our reality and theirs. If we can study or even channel its psionic power, we should be able to greatly improve our sublight speeds and jump drive range.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:8`

### 中文

寻回主机核心

### English

Mainframe Core Salvaged

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:9`

## crisis.2480.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acrisis.2480.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的部队已经从肃正协议的最后一个机械星球的残骸中寻回了一个部分完好的§H肃正协议主机核心§!。这台功能极其强大的计算机被用于计划和维护肃正协议的建筑程序，它将极大的提升我们的[GetContingencyCoreEffect]。\n\n当然，必须设置多层次的防护措施，以防止任何残留的肃正协议代码再次运作，那将会带来……很不幸的副作用。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:9`

### English

Our forces have salvaged a partially intact §HContingency Mainframe Core§! from the wreckage of their last Machine World. This incredibly powerful computer was used to plan and maintain the Contingency's building programs, and it will greatly increase our [GetContingencyCoreEffect].\n\nSeveral layers of safeguards will have to be put in place, of course, to prevent the resurfacing of any remaining Contingency code, which could result in... unfortunate side effects.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:10`

## crisis.2481.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acrisis.2481.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的部队在屏蔽几近完成时捕获到了一个部分完好的§H肃正协议主机核心§!，彼时它正试图逃离最后一个机械星球。这台功能极其强大的计算机被用于计划和维护肃正协议的建筑程序，它将极大的提升我们的[GetContingencyCoreEffect]。\n\n当然，必须设置多层次的防护措施，以防止任何残留的肃正协议代码再次运作，那将会带来……很不幸的副作用。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:10`

### English

Our forces have captured a partially intact §HContingency Mainframe Core§! attempting to flee their last Machine World shortly before the shielding completed. This incredibly powerful computer was used to plan and maintain the Contingency's building programs, and it will greatly increase our [GetContingencyCoreEffect].\n\nSeveral layers of safeguards will have to be put in place, of course, to prevent the resurfacing of any remaining Contingency code, which could result in... unfortunate side effects.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:11`

## marauder.710.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amarauder.710.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在仔细搜索了大可汗旗舰的残骸后，我们的部队发现一把沾满[FROM.GetBloodType]的大椅子漂浮在太空中。我们有充足的理由推定，这件家具实际上是大可汗的王座。\n\n这件遗珍目前已被放在一个安全的保险库里。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:14`

### English

After sifting through the wreckage of the Great Khan's flagship, our forces found a large [FROM.GetBloodType]-stained chair floating in space. We are reasonably certain that this piece of furniture is, in fact, the Great Khan's throne.\n\nThe relic has been placed inside a secure vault.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:15`

## story.335.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astory.335.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们已经用勘探器遗珍的强力扫描光束扫描了我们的领土，并且在§H[chosen_planet.GetName]§!上发现了一个以前不为人知的资源储藏！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:16`

## precursor.105.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aprecursor.105.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

有那么一瞬间，宇宙的构造似乎被扭曲了，然后在古老的沃陶姆遗珍周围伸展。正如我们所知道的那样，现实再次慢慢地展现自身，就在这时，一个短暂的时机出现了，一些规则在此刻可以被巧妙地改写。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:18`

### English

For an instant, the fabric of the universe seems to contort and then expand around the ancient Vultaum relic. As reality as we know it slowly reasserts itself, a brief window appears where some of the rules can be subtly rewritten.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:19`

## site_command_center_intro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asite_command_center_intro&lang=en)

### 中文

与一个较小小行星的碰撞在[From.Planet.GetName]表面露出一个略微伸出的类似小型防爆门或者气闸室的东西。它似乎有着数千年的历史，先前一直被岩石和尘土所掩盖。\n\n由于未知原因，我们的扫描无法穿透到小行星的内部，并且常规切割工具也无法对防爆门造成影响。为了了解这扇门的背后有什么，我们的团队或许不得不从另一个方向向小行星的中心挖掘。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:23`

### English

A collision with a smaller asteroid has revealed a small blast door or airlock extending slightly from the surface of [From.Planet.GetName]. It appears to be thousands of years old, and had previously been concealed by rocks and dust.\n\nFor unknown reasons, scans cannot penetrate the asteroid's interior and the blast door is impervious to conventional cutting tools. To learn what is behind this door, a team would likely have to dig their way towards the center of the asteroid from another direction.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:24`

## ancrel.6005.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.6005.name&lang=en)

### 中文

钻出一个洞

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:24`

### English

Drilling a Hole

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:25`

## ancrel.6005.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.6005.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

某种非常巨大的建筑物占据了[From.Planet.GetName]小行星的绝大部分内部空间。作为这个建筑物唯一入口的防爆门至今仍无法被打开，但是一条从小行星另一侧的通道已经被挖掘出来。\n\n我们的钻头目前仍无法钻透这个建筑物厚实的外壁，但这个方法似乎仍然提供了进入的最佳机会。尽管它们已经过千年风沙，但是防爆门仍然被证实是坚不可摧的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:25`

### English

A very large structure of some kind occupies most of the interior of asteroid [From.Planet.GetName]. The blast door which once served as this structure's only entrance still can't be opened, but a passage has been dug from the other side of the asteroid.\n\nDrills have so far been unable to penetrate the thick outer walls of the structure, but this method still seems to afford the best chance of gaining access. Despite their age, the blast doors have proven nearly invulnerable.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:26`

## ancrel.6010.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.6010.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们最终还是在[From.Planet.GetName]内建筑物的外墙上钻出了一个洞。它的内部是一个宽广的指挥中心，或者是某种地堡，其中零散的散布着几千个腐朽的外星人遗骸。\n\n仔细研究这些，将会让我们得到一张关于当年这颗小行星内部所发生事件更为清晰的图片，这也将花费相当长的时间。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:27`

### English

A hole has finally been drilled through the outer walls of structure inside [From.Planet.GetName]. The interior is a vast command center or bunker of some sort, littered with the decayed remains of thousands of dead aliens.\n\nSifting through all this to gain a clearer picture of what exactly was going on inside this asteroid will take considerable time.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:28`

### English

Command Center

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:29`

## ancrel.6015.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.6015.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这颗小行星曾经作为一个相对较大的星际帝国的隐匿首都，该帝国在数千年前曾经统治着这片星区。有了这样堡垒化的指挥中心，帝国的统治精英们可以与数以千计的军事或民政官员一道安全无虞地发号施令，不受其苛政引起的暴乱所威胁。\n\n小行星与帝国的其余部分的联系只依靠加密的亚空间通讯和一艘定期来访以补给基地物资和运送人员的货船来进行。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:29`

### English

This asteroid once served as the hidden capital of a relatively large interstellar empire that dominated this section of the galaxy several thousand years ago. From this fortified command center, the ruling elite along with thousands of military and civilian officials administered the empire while safe from the violent reprisals their harsh policies often inspired.\n\nContact with the rest of their empire seems to have been handled exclusively through encrypted subspace transmissions and a single freighter which made periodic visits to resupply the base and transfer personnel.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:30`

## ancrel.6020.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.6020.name&lang=en)

### 中文

最后时日

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:30`

### English

The Final Days

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:31`

## ancrel.6020.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.6020.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

从拼凑出的数据来看，这艘补给货船似乎是由于推进器故障而与小行星相撞的。碰撞产生的爆炸破坏了防爆门，并摧毁了小行星的亚空间发射器，永久地切断了它与外界的所有联系。\n\n陷入了困境，更没有得救的希望，小行星的居民慢慢地死于饥饿或自杀。他们唯一残存的子空间接收器的最后日志证实，他们的帝国突然失去了首都，随后陷入内战和混乱中。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:31`

### English

From what can be pieced together, it appears that the resupply freighter collided with the asteroid due to a thruster malfunction. The resulting explosion damaged the blast doors and destroyed the asteroid's subspace transmitter, permanently severing all contact with the outside world.\n\nTrapped and with no hope of rescue, the asteroid's inhabitants slowly succumbed to starvation or suicide. The last logs of their single surviving subspace receiver confirm that their empire, suddenly bereft of its capital, descended into civil war and anarchy.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:32`

## site_hunting_ground_intro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asite_hunting_ground_intro&lang=en)

### 中文

在[From.Planet.GetName]上的一个大而与世隔绝的山谷中一些不同物种的化石被发现了，这些物种似乎不是来自该行星的。它们大体上都来自同一个时期，大约是360万年前，跨度为几个世纪的那段时间内。\n\n这些物种中的每一个个体似乎都是在其所属的独特的生物圈中进化而来的，而且它们似乎都具有智慧。奇怪的是，到目前为止发现的每一个化石都显示出遭到暴力终结的迹象。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:33`

### English

Fossilized remains from several different species that do not appear to have been indigenous to [From.Planet.GetName] have been found in a large and secluded valley on the planet. They all date from roughly the same time period, within the span of a few centuries some 3.6 million years ago.\n\nEach of these species seems to have evolved in its own unique biosphere, and all appear to have been sapient. Strangely, every fossilized individual found so far shows signs of having met a violent end.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:35`

## ancrel.6050.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.6050.name&lang=en)

### 中文

更多遗骸

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:34`

### English

More Remains

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:36`

## ancrel.6050.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.6050.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

考古学家们在山谷中发掘出了越来越多的化石残骸，到目前为止已经有26种不同的智慧物种了，它们都不是[From.Planet.GetName]的本土物种。那些保存程度较高，能用于确定死亡原因的化石都表明这些物种死于刀刃、矛或箭造成的物理创伤。似乎没有任何样本是被现代炮弹或能量武器等类似的装备所击倒的。\n\n在山谷入口附近发现了某种小型结构的废墟。它似乎与这些化石来源于同一时期。也许这会使事情变得明朗一些。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:35`

### English

Archaeologists have unearthed more and more fossilized remains in the valley, and the count is now up to 26 distinct sapient species, none of them native to [From.Planet.GetName]. Those fossils that have been sufficiently preserved to determine a cause of death all point towards physical trauma inflicted by blades, spears, or arrows. Not a single specimen appears to have been felled by the equivalent of modern projectile or energy weapons.\n\nThe ruins of a small structure of some sort have been found near the entrance of the valley. It appears to originate from the same time period as the fossils. Perhaps it will shed some light on things.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:37`

## ancrel.6055.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.6055.name&lang=en)

### 中文

小屋

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:36`

### English

The Lodge

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:38`

## ancrel.6055.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.6055.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

对峡谷入口周边发现的古代建筑结构的发掘工作正按照计划稳步进行中。许多文物被发现，大多数文物似乎是各种狩猎武器和战利品。一个被部分翻译的标志似乎是该结构的名字，意为：\n\n§H马克维尔之谷—第一狩猎屋§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:37`

### English

The excavation of the ancient structure found near the valley's entrance is proceeding according to plan. Many artifacts have been discovered, most of which seem to be variations of hunting weapons and trophies. A partially translated sign, which appears to denote the name of the structure, reads:\n\n§H"VALLEY OF MAK'VAEHL - HUNTING LODGE PRIMUS"§!

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:39`

## ancrel.6060.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.6060.name&lang=en)

### 中文

雷塔拉克斯领域

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:38`

### English

The Retarax Domain

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:40`

## ancrel.6060.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.6060.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

对狩猎屋周边的持续发掘提供了更多的细节信息。[From.Planet.GetName]和马克维尔之谷是作为一个被称为§H雷塔拉克斯领域§!的星际政体的武士同志阶级的狩猎保留地。\n\n雷塔拉克斯是掠夺性的禽类物种，周期性地纳集他们统治下的外星附庸文明的随机个体。这些外星物种按照统治雷塔拉克斯社会的仪式在马克维尔的荒野被狩猎并杀害。这个传统持续了数个世纪直到领域最终被某种未知的外来威胁毁灭。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:39`

### English

Continued excavations near the hunting lodge have provided more details. [From.Planet.GetName] - and the Valley of Mak'vaehl, specifically - served as a hunting reserve for the ruling warrior class of an interstellar polity known as the §HRetarax Domain§!.\n\nThe Retarax, a predatory avian species, periodically collected random individuals from the many subjugated alien civilizations under their rule. These aliens were then hunted down and killed in the wilderness of Mak'vaehl, according to the rituals that governed Retarax society. This tradition lasted several centuries, until the Domain eventually succumbed to an undefined outside threat.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:41`

## ancrel.6065.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.6065.name&lang=en)

### 中文

女猎手

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:40`

### English

The Huntress

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:42`

## ancrel.6065.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.6065.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

发掘狩猎屋的考古学家偶然发现了一些参考线索，这些参考线索指向了一位传奇雷塔拉克斯战士，其仅仅被称为§H“女猎手”§!。她显然是最具效率的杀手，因亲手在马克维尔屠戮了数千外星人而闻名整个领域。\n\n现在还不明白这是基于事实还是文学幻想，但是按照雷塔拉克斯的传说，女猎手在一次狩猎时被她追逐的猎物智取而死在了峡谷内部的某处。在传说中她当时正挥舞着§H“女猎手之刃”§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:41`

### English

Archaeologists excavating the hunting lodge have stumbled upon several references to a legendary Retarax warrior known only as §H"The Huntress."§! She was apparently their most efficient killer, renowned throughout the Domain for having personally butchered thousands of aliens at Mak'vaehl.\n\nIt is unclear whether this is based on fact or fiction, but according to Retarax legend the Huntress was killed somewhere inside the valley when she was outwitted by the alien prey she was pursuing. She was supposedly wielding the fabled §H"Blade of the Huntress"§! at the time.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:43`

## ancrel.6070.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.6070.name&lang=en)

### 中文

女猎手之刃

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:42`

### English

The Blade of the Huntress

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:44`

## ancrel.6070.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.6070.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

经过精心梳理马克维尔谷任何与女猎手相关的痕迹，考古学家最终查明了她最后休息的地方。她的化石骨骼被发现锁定在大型带角节肢动物的残骸中，她的爪子依旧牢抓着保存完好的锐剑之柄。\n\n这一定就是§H女猎手之刃§!了。这把武器是由一种未知的材料打造而成的，锋利到能够切断我们最强韧的盔甲。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:43`

### English

After painstakingly combing through the Valley of Mak'vaehl for any trace of the legendary Huntress, archaeologists were finally able to pinpoint her last resting place. Her fossilized skeleton was found locked in battle with the remains of a large horned arthropoid, with her claws still clasping the hilt of a perfectly preserved sword.\n\nThis must be the §HBlade of the Huntress§!. The weapon is composed of an unknown material and is sharp enough to cut straight through our strongest armor.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:45`

## ancrel.6070.b.evopred

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.6070.b.evopred&lang=en)

### 中文

女猎手把这件珍宝留给了我们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:44`

### English

The Huntress has left us a great treasure.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:46`

## ancrel.6099.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.6099.name&lang=en)

### 中文

隐形场现形

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:45`

### English

Chameleon Field Exposed

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:48`

## ancrel.6099.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.6099.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在§H[tomb_planet.GetName]§!——我们领土内一颗没有生命存在的[tomb_planet.GetPlanetMoon]地表上，一些古老的建筑物隐藏在某种隐形能量场之下。偶然有一颗小行星在撞击[tomb_planet.GetPlanetMoon]时发生了偏转并被旁经的货船监控到，我们这才发现了它的存在。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:46`

### English

A chameleon energy field of some sort concealing an ancient structure was recently exposed on the surface of §H[tomb_planet.GetName]§!, a lifeless [tomb_planet.GetPlanetMoon] inside our space. Only a chance asteroid impact which was deflected off the field while the [tomb_planet.GetPlanetMoon] was being monitored by a passing freighter revealed its existence.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:49`

## ancrel.6099.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.6099.a&lang=en)

### 中文

应该对其进行调查。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:47`

### English

This should be investigated.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:50`

## site_zarqlan

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asite_zarqlan&lang=en)

### 中文

古墓

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:48`

### English

Ancient Tomb

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:51`

## site_zarqlan_intro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asite_zarqlan_intro&lang=en)

### 中文

这颗行星的地表上，一座古老的建筑物被隐藏在某种迷彩能量场之下。只是偶然观测到，一颗小行星在撞击时才揭示了它的存在，该小行星化为流星偏转了它的能量场。\n\n在该能量场被关闭后，考古学家们现在可以去探索原本隐藏其下的，令人生畏的巨大建筑群了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:49`

### English

A chameleon energy field was concealing an ancient structure on the surface of this planet. Only a chance observation of an asteroid impact revealed its existence as the meteor deflected off the field.\n\nWith the field deactivated, archaeologists will now have access to the large and forbidding building complex hidden beneath it.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:52`

## ancrel.6100.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.6100.name&lang=en)

### 中文

陵墓

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:50`

### English

The Mausoleum

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:53`

## ancrel.6100.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.6100.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这座建筑看起来似乎是一座巨大的陵墓或者坟场，其建造时间可以追溯到几十万年前。这里没有曾遭劫掠的迹象，所以这座建筑或许在这处隐形场启动后就一直保持着原状。\n\n建筑的前厅是开放的，但一扇紧闭的大门阻挡了我们进一步的考古进程。在门上方的墙壁上以大字刻着尚未破译的外星符号。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:51`

### English

The structure appears to be a gigantic mausoleum or necropolis, dating back around a hundred thousand years. There are no signs of previous looters, so the structure may have been untouched since the chameleon field was erected.\n\nThe structure's First Chamber is accessible, but an impenetrable door blocks any further progress. Large alien glyphs, untranslated, have been carved into the wall above the door.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:54`

## ancrel.6105.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.6105.name&lang=en)

### 中文

前厅

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:52`

### English

The First Chamber

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:55`

## ancrel.6105.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.6105.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

刻在大门上方的外星符号翻译如下：\n\n§H“我是扎克兰，MOZ-TARB的守护者，ZVA的大先知，塞塔娜的终结者。我放弃了一切来拯救这个充满怨恨和仇恨的星系。如果你们有这个资格，那就继续前进吧。”§!\n\n在细细梳理过前厅以搜寻有价值的东西后，考古学家们在墙壁上发现了一块被隐藏起来的微小嵌板。当按下嵌板时，那扇大门缓缓打开，次厅显露在我们眼前。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:53`

### English

The alien glyphs above the door have been translated as follows:\n\n§H"I am ZARQLAN, the Guardian of MOZ-TARB, the Prophet of ZVA, he who felled CETANA. I gave up everything to save a galaxy awash with spite and hatred. Proceed, if ye be worthy."§!\n\nAfter combing through the First Chamber for anything of interest, archaeologists discovered a tiny concealed wall panel. When pressed, the ancient door rumbled open. The Second Chamber can now be accessed.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:56`

## ancrel.6110.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.6110.name&lang=en)

### 中文

进院

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:54`

### English

The Second Chamber

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:57`

## ancrel.6110.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.6110.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

次厅看上去十分普通，除了在又一扇紧闭的门上方刻着的符号，上面写着：\n\n§H“渺小的国王以我的名义宣布敕令；帝国为我的荣光发动战争；愚民们用歌唱表达对我的颂扬。而我为他们而牺牲。”§!\n\n这次我们没有找到什么秘密嵌板来打开通往中堂的通道。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:55`

### English

The Second Chamber is unremarkable, aside from another set of alien glyphs above a closed door. They read:\n\n§H"Petty kings issued edicts in my name. Empires waged war in my honor. Fools sang my praises. And yet I died for them."§!\n\nNo secret wall panel to access the Third Chamber has yet been found.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:58`

## ancrel.6115.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.6115.name&lang=en)

### 中文

中堂

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:56`

### English

The Third Chamber

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:59`

## ancrel.6115.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.6115.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

经过长时间痛苦疲乏的勘察，我们发现了一根藏在地板下面的秘密控制杆，用于打开通往后室的通道。像之前一样，外星符号被刻在另一扇门的上方：\n\n§H“所有想要利用扎克兰的人必须先找到属于他们的道路。在每个墓室中都有一把隐藏的钥匙，只有找到钥匙才能继续前进。这些挑战的意义很快就会显现。”§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:57`

### English

After a long and tiring search, a secret lever hidden under the floor was found, which provided access to the Fourth Chamber. As before, alien glyphs adorned the wall above yet another door:\n\n§H"Those who wish to parlay with ZARQLAN must first find their way. Each chamber has a hidden key that only the deserving could find. The purpose of these challenges will soon be revealed."§!

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:60`

## ancrel.6120.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.6120.name&lang=en)

### 中文

后室

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:58`

### English

The Fourth Chamber

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:61`

## ancrel.6120.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.6120.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这一次，后室里的控制杆被藏在屋顶。而在下一个房间中有更多的外星符号：\n\n§H“这里没什么值得学习的。这些挑战也像这些语句一样毫无意义。这是我留下的教训。我是扎克兰，一个无名小卒，不情愿地被历史的潮流裹挟着前行。继续前进吧，你将获得一名忠实的听众，假如你依然认为值得的话。”§!\n\n看上去再下一个房间就是最后一个墓室了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:59`

### English

This time, the lever was concealed in the roof. The Fifth Chamber has more alien writing:\n\n§H"There is nothing to learn here. The challenges are as meaningless as these words. That is my lesson. I am ZARQLAN, the pawn. Swept along by the currents of history. Proceed, and an audience shall be yours, should one still be worthy."§!\n\nThe next chamber is likely to be the last.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:62`

## ancrel.6125.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.6125.name&lang=en)

### 中文

尾室

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:60`

### English

The Final Chamber

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:63`

## ancrel.6125.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.6125.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

经过紧张的勘察，最后一把“钥匙”被发现是藏在外星符号表面上的一个微小按钮。在尾室中，一个巨大的王座支撑着一个弯曲而静止的人形物体。一阵刺鼻的气味飘来，告诉了我们这具尸体腐烂的状况。几个外星符号写在王座底部：§H“扎克兰，我们愿为您效劳”§!\n\n整具尸体由于过度腐烂而无法移动，但考虑到尸体放置于此的年月，其头部被保存的出奇得好。考古学家们不想空手而归，于是便用携带的激光切割器将头部切下带走。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:61`

### English

After a grueling search, the last "key" was discovered as a minuscule button hidden in one of the carved alien glyphs. In this final chamber, a gigantic throne holds a sizeable humanoid figure's slumped and motionless form. A strong odor hints at the decayed state of the being's body. Some glyphs at the bottom of the throne read: §H"ZARQLAN, AT YOUR SERVICE"§!\n\nThe body is too far gone to be moved, but the head is surprisingly well-preserved, given its age. Not wanting to leave empty-handed, some archaeologists use a laser cutter to remove the head.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:64`

## ancrel.6125.desc.b.evopred

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.6125.desc.b.evopred&lang=en)

### 中文

别管什么渎不渎神，我们就要它的基因！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:62`

### English

Sacrilege or not, we must have its DNA!

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:65`

## ancrel.6130.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.6130.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这……居然是真的。你们找到了传说中扎克兰的墓室。当预言官们告诉我这件事时，我并不相信他们，但现在证据已无可辩驳。我明白，你们已经拿到了大先知的头颅，对吗？\n\n那么，按照预言所说，伟大的扎克兰的选民终于揭晓。如扎克兰所愿，在我们领土附近的任何未定居的圣地都随你们处置。在[spiritualist_fallen_empire.Capital.GetName]上另有许多虔信的朝圣者，他们会因能在你的旗帜下作战而感到极大的荣耀。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:63`

### English

It... is true, then. You have found the legendary tomb of Zarqlan. When the Augurs told me, I did not believe them, but the evidence is irrefutable. I understand that you have the Prophet's head in your possession?\n\nThen, as was foretold, great Zarqlan's chosen has finally been revealed. As would have been Zarqlan's wish, any unsettled holy worlds near our space are yours to do with as you wish. There are also many devout pilgrims on [spiritualist_fallen_empire.Capital.GetName] who would be greatly honored to fight under your banner.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:66`

## ancrel.6130.machine.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.6130.machine.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这的确是真的；区区一台机器发现了传说中的扎克兰之墓。当虚境灵使把这个消息刚刚传到时，我觉得很难相信。但现在我已经完全接受了你拥有先知头颅的事实。\n\n我们不会质疑伟大的扎克兰的意志。你们是这份庇佑的合法持有者。根据古代经文，在我们的领土附近所有未受侵染的圣地星球都由你们来管理。[spiritualist_fallen_empire.Capital.GetName]上许多虔诚的朝圣者都会乐意为这一神圣的事业而战并以此为荣。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:64`

### English

It is indeed true; a mere machine has discovered the legendary tomb of Zarqlan. When the Augurs first relayed this news, I found it hard to believe. But now I understand that you possess the Prophet's head.\n\nWe will not question the will of the great Zarqlan. Your kind is the rightful holder of this blessing. According to the ancient scriptures, any unsettled holy worlds near our territory are yours to manage as you see fit. Many devout pilgrims on [spiritualist_fallen_empire.Capital.GetName] would be greatly honored to fight for this sacred cause.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:67`

## ancrel.6130.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.6130.a&lang=en)

通讯终止。

### English

End transmission.

## opinion_zarqlans_chosen

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aopinion_zarqlans_chosen&lang=en)

### 中文

扎克兰的选民

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:66`

### English

Zarqlan's Chosen

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:69`

## ancrel.6132.a.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.6132.a.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

扎克兰的忠仆们现在听您差遣，[Root.GetSpeciesNamePlural]。告诉我们是谁应当遭受天罚吧！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:67`

### English

The loyal servants of Zarqlan are at your command, [Root.GetSpeciesNamePlural]. Let us know who to smite!

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:70`

## ancrel.6132.b.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.6132.b.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们应召而来。世上再无比侍奉扎克兰的选民更大的荣耀了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:68`

### English

We are summoned. There is no greater honor than to serve the Chosen of Zarqlan.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:71`

## ancrel.6132.c.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.6132.c.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们是您的人。告诉我们您的敌人是谁吧。\n\n§H遵循扎克兰的意旨！§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:69`

### English

We are yours. Point us towards your enemies.\n\n§HZARQLAN WILLS IT!§!

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:72`

## ancrel.6132.d.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.6132.d.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

您召唤我们，我们应召而来！\n\n愿扎克兰的敌人生出的婴孩无比丑陋！愿他们被自己的营养膏堵住喉咙，愿他们的衣服在不合时宜又极度尴尬的时刻撕出裂口，愿他们的感光器官自动燃起火焰，愿他们……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:70`

### English

You called and we are here!\n\nMay the enemies of Zarqlan breed ugly children! May they choke on their nutrients, may their clothing disintegrate at inopportune and embarrassing moments, may their ocular organs spontaneously combust, may their...

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:73`

## ancrel.6132.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.6132.a&lang=en)

### 中文

遵循扎克兰的意旨！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:71`

### English

Zarqlan wills it!

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:74`

## ancrel.6133.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.6133.name&lang=en)

### 中文

朝圣者到来

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:72`

### English

Pilgrims Arrive

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:75`

## ancrel.6133.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.6133.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

如今已很少有活着的扎克兰的遵奉者了，但一小支配备着虔信而缺乏训练的船员的护航舰队还是一路来到了[Root.Capital.GetName]并发誓听从[Root.GetName]调配。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:73`

### English

There are few surviving worshipers of Zarqlan these days, but a small fleet of escorts with devout but untrained crews has made the journey to [Root.Capital.GetName] and pledged itself to the [Root.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:76`

## relic_summon_pilgrims

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Arelic_summon_pilgrims&lang=en)

### 中文

召唤一小支失落帝国舰队。每次激活都会增加舰队的规模。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:74`

### English

Summon a small Fallen Empire fleet. Every activation will increase the size of the fleet.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:77`

## ancrel.6150.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.6150.name&lang=en)

### 中文

屏障星球

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:75`

### English

Shielded Planet

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:79`

## ancrel.6150.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.6150.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

最近的调查显示，§H[From.GetName]§!被包围在了一个拥有难以置信的强度并反射所有探测手段的护盾之中。这个星球护盾似乎是从这颗星球附近的卫星中产生的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:76`

### English

As confirmed by its recent survey, §H[From.GetName]§! is surrounded by an incredibly strong energy field that deflects all attempts to scan it. The field appears to be generated from the planet's nearby moon.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:80`

## ancrel.6151.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.6151.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§H[From.GetName]§!散发出一道围绕着他所运行的行星的无法穿透的能量屏障。这道能量屏障的来源很难确定，但它似乎是来自于卫星贫瘠表面下深处的某个地方。\n\n在卫星上最大的一个陨石坑里，可以发现一些古代遗址，可能是出于某种军事用途的大型基地。那里可能会存在着一些关于能量屏障的答案。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:77`

### English

§H[From.GetName]§! is emitting an impenetrable energy barrier that surrounds the planet it orbits. The exact source of the barrier is difficult to pinpoint, but it appears to be coming from somewhere deep beneath the moon's barren surface.\n\nAncient ruins of what must have been a large base of some sort, possibly of a military nature, can be found inside one of the moon's largest craters. It may hold some answers.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:81`

## site_minigalaxy_1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asite_minigalaxy_1&lang=en)

### 中文

卫星基地

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:78`

### English

Moon Base

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:83`

## site_minigalaxy_1_intro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asite_minigalaxy_1_intro&lang=en)

### 中文

在这颗卫星上的一个大陨石坑内，可以找到一座大型基地建筑群的遗迹。它已经被遗弃了几千年之久。围绕着附近行星的能量屏障似乎是源于这个古老的基地正下方的一个能量源。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:79`

### English

Ruins of a large base complex of some kind can be found inside a large crater on this moon. It must have been abandoned thousands of years ago. The energy barrier that encompasses the nearby planet appears to originate from a source directly beneath this ancient base.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:84`

## ancrel.6155.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.6155.name&lang=en)

### 中文

停战倡议组织

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:80`

### English

The Armistice Initiative

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:85`

## ancrel.6155.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.6155.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

对§H[From.Planet.GetName]§!的进一步挖掘的结果显示，这个废弃的基地曾经是由“§H停战倡议组织§!”建造的大型军事设施。这是一个由至少五个疆域广大并且非常先进的星际力量组成的半永久联盟，于大约75000年前活跃于星系区域。\n\n这个联盟的确切性质尚不清楚，但它似乎致力于立即结束某些长期的星系级战争。每个停战倡议成员都派遣了大量军事分遣队驻扎在这个卫星基地上。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:81`

### English

Continued excavations of the ruins on §H[From.Planet.GetName]§! have revealed that the abandoned base was once a massive military installation built by the "§HArmistice Initiative§!." They were a semi-permanent alliance of at least five large and very advanced interstellar powers active in this galactic region some 75,000 years ago.\n\nThe exact nature of this alliance is unknown, but it appears to have been committed to bringing an immediate end to several long-running galactic wars. Large military contingents from each Initiative member were stationed at the base on this moon.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:86`

## ancrel.6160.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.6160.name&lang=en)

### 中文

交战方丙

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:82`

### English

Belligerent Party C

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:87`

## ancrel.6160.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.6160.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在这个星系中被屏蔽的行星是一个正在交战的帝国的首都，停战倡议组织是正试图遏止这场战争。这个帝国的名字仍然不为人知——在迄今为止已恢复的倡议组织记录中，他们被简单地称为“§H交战方丙§!”。\n\n显然，停战倡议组织希望以交战方丙作为一个示例。在侵入他们的空间并赢得了几场艰苦战斗之后，倡议组织将丙方的母星永久地囚禁在一个能源护盾之中。他们还在在附近的卫星上建立了一座军事基地，以维持星球护盾的运行，并且提供预防遏制行动失败的临时反应部队。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:83`

### English

The shielded planet in this system was the capital of one of the warring empires the Armistice Initiative was formed to stop. This empire's name is still unknown - they are referred to simply as "§HBelligerent Party C§!" in all Initiative records recovered so far.\n\nApparently, the Armistice Initiative sought to make an example of Belligerent Party C. After invading their space and winning several hard-fought battles, the Initiative permanently imprisoned the homeworld of Party C behind an energy shield. A military base was established on the nearby moon to maintain the shield and act as a response force in the unlikely event of containment failure.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:88`

## ancrel.6165.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.6165.name&lang=en)

### 中文

护盾生成器

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:84`

### English

The Shield Generator

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:89`

## ancrel.6165.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.6165.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

起初卫星基地维持了几个世纪的运行，由于没有遏制行动失败的迹象，基地的军事力量逐渐缩减。后来随着不同倡议组织成员之间的转移联盟和内讧，最终卫星基地完全荒废。\n\n自动化系统被保留在适当位置，以维护丙方的家园周围的防护罩。令人难以置信的是，直到现在，它们仍可正常工作。可惜的是，当我们的考古学家在向地下的护盾生成室挖洞时，一条关键的能量电缆被切断了。经过75000年的连续运行，防护罩已不复存在。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:85`

### English

The Initiative moon base was kept operational for several centuries, but with no signs of containment failure the forces were gradually scaled back. Shifting alliances and infighting between the various Initiative members eventually left the base completely deserted.\n\nAutomated systems were left in place to maintain the shield surrounding Party C's homeworld, and they have, incredibly, remained functional up until now. Alas, a critical power cable was severed when our archaeologists dug their way into the subterranean generator chamber. After 75,000 years of continuous operation, the shield is no more.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:90`

## site_minigalaxy_2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asite_minigalaxy_2&lang=en)

### 中文

被遗弃的都市星球

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:86`

### English

Abandoned Ecumenopolis

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:92`

## site_minigalaxy_2_intro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asite_minigalaxy_2_intro&lang=en)

### 中文

这个古老的遗落星球已经被囚禁在一道无法穿透的能量屏障里大约有75000年之久。它曾经是一颗充满活力的都市星球，居住着数以亿计的居民，但现在街道和建筑物都被遗弃了，星球上的原初居民也都销声匿迹了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:87`

### English

This ancient Relic World was imprisoned behind an impenetrable energy barrier for roughly 75,000 years. It must once have been a vibrant ecumenopolis home to hundreds of billions of individuals, but the streets and buildings are deserted now. None of the planet's original inhabitants remain.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:93`

## ancrel.6170.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.6170.name&lang=en)

### 中文

失踪

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:88`

### English

Disappearance

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:94`

## ancrel.6170.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.6170.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这颗星球原初人口的消失仍然是个谜。当护盾升起的时候，他们与星际帝国的其他部分隔绝，他们很可能面临大规模饥荒和国内动乱，因为他们需要竭尽全力用有限的资源来供养庞大的首都人口。\n\n然而，没有迹象表明，他们清理了城市星球的部分土地来耕种。没有任何迹象表明，在护盾升起后发生了任何重大的战斗，居民们没有遗骸、化石或其他遗留物。仿佛他们仅仅是消散在了稀薄的空气中。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:89`

### English

The disappearance of this planet's original population remains a mystery. Once the shield went up and they were isolated from the rest of their interstellar empire, they likely faced mass famine and civil unrest as they struggled to support their capital's massive population with what limited resources they had.\n\nYet there are no signs that they demolished parts of their city planet to cultivate the land. Nothing suggests that any significant fighting took place after the shield went up, and there are no remains, fossilized or otherwise, of the inhabitants. It's like they simply vanished into thin air.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:95`

## ancrel.6175.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.6175.name&lang=en)

### 中文

撤离日

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:90`

### English

Evacuation Day

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:96`

## ancrel.6175.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.6175.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这座被遗弃的星球城市中怪诞空旷的街道仍然没有暴露出原住民当初遭遇了什么。已经发现的一些海报和小册子提到了某种形式的§H“撤离日”§!，这些册子里面画着一些我们天文学家不认识的星系的图片：\n\n§H“撤离日——已经到来。尽快到离你们最近的定居中心报到，开启新的生活吧。”§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:91`

### English

The eerie and empty streets of this abandoned planet-wide city still do not betray what happened to the original inhabitants. Posters and pamphlets have been discovered that mention some sort of "§HEvacuation Day§!." They are typically accompanied by pictures of a galaxy that our astronomers do not recognize:\n\n§H"EVACUATION DAY - TODAY IS THE DAY. REPORT TO YOUR NEAREST RELOCATION CENTER AS SOON AS POSSIBLE TO BEGIN YOUR NEW LIFE."§!

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:97`

## ancrel.6180.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.6180.name&lang=en)

### 中文

最终计划

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:92`

### English

The Project

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:98`

## ancrel.6180.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.6180.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在“撤离日”之后整个行星被彻底废弃了，但这究竟是怎么做到的？没有任何的证据表明行星的能量屏障曾经减弱过。\n\n已经发现的线索表明，在护盾出现之前，行星上的原住民们正在进行某种大规模的研究项目。越来越多的资源和科研人员被转移到这个项目中，并且这种行为似乎在护盾产生后大大地加快了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:93`

### English

The planet was clearly abandoned after this "Evacuation Day" concluded, but how? There is no evidence that the planet's energy barrier ever went down.\n\nClues have been discovered that indicate the pursuit of some sort of massive research project that predates the shield. Resources and science staff were diverted to this project in increasing numbers, and efforts appear to have greatly intensified after the shield went up.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:99`

## ancrel.6185.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.6185.name&lang=en)

### 中文

微型星系

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:94`

### English

Miniaturized Galaxy

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:100`

## ancrel.6185.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.6185.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

经过高强度的搜索之后，我们发现了[From.Planet.GetName]原住民们的去向。在这个行星北极点附近一个高墙环绕的建筑深处，我们发现了一个遗珍，我们的考古学家确信这里面装有一个微型星系。\n\n通过某种手段，在创造了这个星系之后，遗落星球的原住民们都迁移到了里面，然后用某种不可渗透的亚空间场把星系封闭在了这个容器中。虽然我们无法干扰这个星系本身，但我们可以对它进行研究。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:95`

### English

After intense search efforts, we have discovered where the inhabitants of [From.Planet.GetName] went. Deep inside a tall fortified structure near the planet's northern pole, we found a relic that our archaeologists are convinced holds an actual miniaturized galaxy.\n\nSomehow, after creating this galaxy, the inhabitants of the Relic World all relocated there and then sealed it in this container with some kind of impervious subspace field. While we can't interfere with the galaxy itself, our scientists assure us that we can study it.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:101`

## site_star_petal

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asite_star_petal&lang=en)

### 中文

气态巨行星中的建筑

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:96`

### English

Gas Giant Structure

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:103`

## site_star_petal_intro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asite_star_petal_intro&lang=en)

### 中文

一座大型废弃空间站被困在这颗气态巨行星的大气层内部，向着巨行星内部缓慢下坠。其自重很可能在下一个千年里彻底毁坏这个空间站。\n\n好在该空间站飘入大气层还不太深，我们仍有可能对其开展考古行动。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:97`

### English

A large derelict space station is trapped in a slow terminal orbit inside the atmosphere of this gas giant. The gravitational stresses will likely destroy it completely within the next thousand years.\n\nIt has not yet drifted deep enough into the atmosphere to make archaeological expeditions to the station impossible.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:104`

## ancrel.6200.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.6200.name&lang=en)

### 中文

享乐之宫

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:98`

### English

Pleasure Palace

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:105`

## ancrel.6200.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.6200.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

空间站内部黑暗无光、了无生命的现状几乎成功掩盖了它曾经被设计为某种奢华的享乐宫殿的事实。这里有看起来数不清的一列列生锈的老虎机，数百间破旧不堪的酒馆和餐厅，其中到处横陈着共计数千具尸体。\n\n这些随处可见的部分木乃伊化的外星人尸体属于至少十几个不同的物种，但大部分尸体都聚集在几个空的逃生舱停放港附近。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:99`

### English

The present dark and lifeless interior of the space station almost succeeds in hiding the fact that it must have been designed as some kind of luxurious pleasure palace. There are seemingly endless rows of rusty slot machines, hundreds of decrepit bars and restaurants, and amidst it all, thousands of bodies.\n\nDead, partially mummified aliens of at least a dozen different species can be found everywhere, but most of the bodies are clustered around the empty escape pod bays.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:106`

## ancrel.6205.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.6205.name&lang=en)

### 中文

星辰花瓣

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:100`

### English

The Star Petal

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:107`

## ancrel.6205.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.6205.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在将近一千年以前，这个废弃的空间站曾经被称作“§H星辰花瓣§!”并似乎是闻名于星系一角的奢华度假胜地。每年都会有一小队拖船将此空间站牵引到未曾去过的异国他乡，而[From.Planet.GetName]看起来是其最后停留的位置。\n\n由于尚不清楚的原因，这个空间站被拖进了这个气态巨行星的大气层并在此遭受了严重损坏。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:101`

### English

This derelict station was once known as the "§HStar Petal§!" and it was apparently a celebrated luxury resort in this corner of the galaxy nearly a thousand years ago. A small fleet of tugs would drag the station to a new exotic location each year, but [From.Planet.GetName] appears to have been its last.\n\nFor unclear reasons, the station was pulled into the gas giant's atmosphere where it suffered critical damage.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:108`

## ancrel.6210.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.6210.name&lang=en)

### 中文

最后时日

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:102`

### English

The Final Days

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:109`

## ancrel.6210.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.6210.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在“星辰花瓣”工作的考古学家收集到了足够的线索和证据，可以为这个空间站的最后时日描述出一副相当清晰的图景。一颗离群小行星撞上了这个空间站，使其航线偏离，很快就会沉入[From.Planet.GetName]的大气层。\n\n空间站的轨道控制推进器无法使其回归正确航线，指挥人员立即下令进行疏散。不幸的是，空间站上的逃生舱仅够很小一部分乘员撤离，由此引发了集体恐慌。那些没有被踩踏至死者在空间站坠入气态巨行星深处时由于环境维持系统失效而丧生。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:103`

### English

The archaeologists working on the Star Petal have gathered sufficient clues and evidence to paint a reasonably clear picture of the station's final days. A stray asteroid impacted the station, knocking it into an orbit that would soon plunge it into [From.Planet.GetName]'s atmosphere.\n\nStation-keeping thrusters could not compensate, and the command staff soon issued an evacuation order. Unfortunately, the station only had enough escape pods to evacuate a fraction of its passengers and mass panic soon erupted. Those who were not trampled to death perished when the station's environmental systems failed as it drifted deeper into the gas giant.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:110`

## ancrel.6215.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.6215.name&lang=en)

### 中文

脉动之心

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:104`

### English

The Heart of the Pulsar

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:111`

## ancrel.6215.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.6215.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在“星辰花瓣”最富有的主顾们存储其个人物品的设有安保的咖啡厅里搜索时，我们找到了一座装有奇异文物和珠宝的宝库。在找到的物品中最值得注意的或许是“脉动之心”——一串独一无二的钻石项链，在当年极为有名，至少被五个国家的首脑戴过。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:105`

### English

Rummaging through the secure safes where the Star Petal's wealthiest clientèle stored their personal belongings has revealed a treasure trove of strange artifacts and jewelry. Perhaps the most noteworthy among the found items is the "Heart of the Pulsar" - a unique diamond necklace quite famous in its day for having been worn by five different heads of state.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:112`

## ancrel.8000.intro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.8000.intro&lang=en)

### 中文

在[From.Planet.GetName]上考古遗址疏散的过程中，一个古老但仍可工作的激光网在无意间被触发。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:109`

### English

During the excavations of the archaeology site on [From.Planet.GetName], an ancient but still functional laser grid was inadvertently triggered.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:118`

## ancrel.8000.critical

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.8000.critical&lang=en)

### 中文

激光网迅速移动，将§H[dead_scientist.GetName]§!和另外几名[Root.Owner.GetArchaeologistPlural]切成了碎片。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:110`

### English

The rapidly moving laser beams sliced §H[dead_scientist.GetName]§! and several other [Root.Owner.GetArchaeologistPlural] into tiny pieces.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:119`

## ancrel.8000.failure

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.8000.failure&lang=en)

### 中文

几名[Root.Owner.GetArchaeologistPlural]被快速移动的网格切成了碎片。尽管§H[Root.GetLeaderName]§!安然无恙，这些损失还是对项目产生了重大的不利影响。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:111`

### English

Several [Root.Owner.GetArchaeologistPlural] were sliced into tiny pieces by the fast moving beams. Although §H[Root.GetLeaderName]§! survived unscathed, these losses have had a negative impact on the project.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:120`

## ancrel.8000.success

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.8000.success&lang=en)

### 中文

很幸运，多亏了§H[Root.GetLeaderName]§!的快速行动，激光在造成任何损失之前被无效化了。项目进度向前推进，超出了预期。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:112`

### English

Fortunately, thanks to the quick actions of §H[Root.GetLeaderName]§! the lasers were disabled before they could cause any harm. The project is moving forwards, ahead of schedule.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:121`

## ancrel.8000.pay

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.8000.pay&lang=en)

### 中文

激光挡住了前进的唯一道路。无效化它们的代价很昂贵，但要找到另一条绕过激光网的路则会耗费很多时间。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:113`

### English

The lasers are blocking the only path forward. Disabling them will be expensive, but finding an alternative route around the laser grid would take a lot of time.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:122`

## ancrel.8010.intro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.8010.intro&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在[From.Planet.GetName]工作的[Root.Owner.GetArchaeologistPlural]偶然间触发了一个年代久远但仍能运作的哨戒炮阵列。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:121`

### English

Our [Root.Owner.GetArchaeologistPlural] working on [From.Planet.GetName] accidentally triggered an array of ancient but still functional sentry guns.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:131`

## ancrel.8010.critical

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.8010.critical&lang=en)

### 中文

自动炮台在被关闭之前迅速地射杀了§H[dead_scientist.GetName]§!和其他几个[Root.Owner.GetArchaeologistPlural]。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:122`

### English

The automated guns rapidly mowed down §H[dead_scientist.GetName]§! and several other [Root.Owner.GetArchaeologistPlural] before they could be disabled.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:132`

## ancrel.8010.failure

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.8010.failure&lang=en)

### 中文

几个[Root.Owner.GetArchaeologistPlural]被困于他们的交火区域。§H[Root.GetLeaderName]§!最终设法关闭了那些炮台，但死伤不可避免。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:123`

### English

Several [Root.Owner.GetArchaeologistPlural] were caught in their firing zones. §H[Root.GetLeaderName]§! eventually managed to disable the guns, but there were several fatalities.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:133`

很幸运，§H[Root.GetLeaderName]§!设法在有人受伤之前解除了那些自动炮台。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:124`

### English

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:125`

### English

## ancrel.8020.intro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.8020.intro&lang=en)

### 中文

一台四处徘徊的，被设置用来埋伏粗心探险家们的碾轧机器人，被我们在[From.Planet.GetName]的[Root.Owner.GetArchaeologistPlural]撞上了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:132`

### English

A roving crusher-droid, set up to ambush unwary explorers, was encountered by our [Root.Owner.GetArchaeologistPlural] on [From.Planet.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:143`

## ancrel.8020.critical

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.8020.critical&lang=en)

### 中文

在暴走停下来之前，古老的半感性机器人将§H[dead_scientist.GetName]§!碾成了肉末。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:133`

### English

The ancient and semi-sentient robot ground §H[dead_scientist.GetName]§! into tiny pieces before its rampage could be stopped.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:144`

## ancrel.8020.failure

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.8020.failure&lang=en)

### 中文

§H[Root.GetLeaderName]§!设法从暴走的机器人手上捡回一条小命，但其他的[Root.Owner.GetArchaeologistPlural]就没那么幸运了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:134`

### English

§H[Root.GetLeaderName]§! managed to flee its rampage, but several other [Root.Owner.GetArchaeologistPlural] were not so lucky.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:145`

## ancrel.8020.success

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.8020.success&lang=en)

### 中文

凭借着经验和一点点运气，§H[Root.GetLeaderName]§!设法在任何人受伤之前平息了机器人的暴走。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:135`

### English

With skill and a fair bit of luck, §H[Root.GetLeaderName]§! managed to halt the rampaging robot before anyone was injured.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:146`

## ancrel.8020.pay

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.8020.pay&lang=en)

### 中文

每个人都想避开暴走的机器人，但它昼夜不停的在我们前进的唯一道路上巡逻。如果不能关掉它，我们的[Root.Owner.GetArchaeologistPlural]只能去寻找其他的路。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:136`

### English

Everyone managed to escape the rampaging robot, but it is now tirelessly patrolling the only way forward. Unless it can be disabled, our [Root.Owner.GetArchaeologistPlural] will have to look for another path.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:147`

## ancrel.8030.intro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.8030.intro&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的[Root.Owner.GetArchaeologistPlural]在[From.Planet.GetName]上进行考古发掘的时候莫名其妙地激活了一些远古智能人。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:143`

### English

The excavations carried out by our [Root.Owner.GetArchaeologistPlural] on [From.Planet.GetName] have somehow activated several ancient sentinel droids.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:155`

## ancrel.8030.critical

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.8030.critical&lang=en)

### 中文

这些敌对智能人在被武器火力干掉之前迅速解决了§H[dead_scientist.GetName]§!。 

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:144`

### English

The hostile robots made short work of §H[dead_scientist.GetName]§! before they could be permanently deactivated with weapons fire. 

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:156`

## ancrel.8030.failure

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.8030.failure&lang=en)

### 中文

敌对智能人在被§H[Root.GetLeaderName]§!阻止之前成功干掉了一些[Root.Owner.GetArchaeologistPlural]。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:145`

### English

The hostile robots managed to kill several [Root.Owner.GetArchaeologistPlural] before they could be stopped by §H[Root.GetLeaderName]§!.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:157`

## ancrel.8030.success

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.8030.success&lang=en)

### 中文

万幸的是§H[Root.GetLeaderName]§!与附近的安保人员在这些敌对智能人严重伤人之前就关闭了它们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:146`

### English

Luckily, §H[Root.GetLeaderName]§! and the nearby security escort managed to neutralize the hostile robots before they could seriously injure anyone.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:158`

## ancrel.8030.pay

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.8030.pay&lang=en)

### 中文

那些敌对智能人暂时被牵制住了，但我们仍然无法通过它们。除非让这些机器人彻底停摆，否则我们就必须寻找另一条路线。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:147`

### English

The hostile robots have been contained for now, but there is no way past them. Unless they can be neutralized, an alternate route will have to be found.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:159`

## asteroid_vault_1_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aasteroid_vault_1_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

虽然乍看上去平淡无奇，但对[From.Planet.GetName]进一步的检查表明它实际上是一座伪装成自然星体的人工结构。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:155`

### English

Seemingly unremarkable at first, closer examination of [From.Planet.GetName] has revealed that it is actually an artificial construct designed to mimic a natural asteroid.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:168`

## ancrel.101.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.101.name&lang=en)

## ancrel.102.name

### English

Strange Door

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:172`

## ancrel.102.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.102.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

随着科研船靠近[From.Planet.GetName]，一道巨大的，饱经风沙的门显露了出来。到底是谁修了这个大东西，又是为了什么？或许答案就在里面。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:160`

### English

As the science ship approaches [From.Planet.GetName], a vast and time-worn door is revealed. Who would have built such a thing, and why? Perhaps the answers are inside.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:173`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.102.a&lang=en)

### 中文
