### English

Open the door!

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:174`

## ancrel.103.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.103.name&lang=en)

### 中文

小行星保险库

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:162`

### English

Asteroid Vault

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:175`

## ancrel.103.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.103.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

打开保险库所带来的问题比它解决的要多得多。放置在里面的是各种各样稀奇古怪的东西，从垃圾到可能来自被遗忘帝国的文物。整理这些物品需要一些时间，但我们确实找到了一些有用的东西。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:163`

### English

Opening the vault has raised more questions than it has answered. Contained within is an esoteric collection of items ranging from junk to what are possibly artifacts from a forgotten empire. Sorting through it all took some time, but we did find some useful items.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:176`

## ancrel.103.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.103.a&lang=en)

### 中文

一个惊喜！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:164`

### English

A welcome surprise!

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:177`

## it_was_aliens_site

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ait_was_aliens_site&lang=en)

### 中文

废墟

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:165`

### English

Ruins

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:178`

## it_was_aliens_site_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ait_was_aliens_site_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

废墟遍布[From.Planet.GetName]，但奇怪的是，从它们的爆炸模式来看，似乎是大量的地下核爆引发了这一切，而不是正常的大气层核爆或是轨道轰炸。这需要进一步调查，或许需要一次考古挖掘来揭露这里到底发生了什么。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:166`

### English

Ruins cover [From.Planet.GetName], but oddly the blast patterns seem to indicate that vast nuclear explosions occurred underneath the surface rather than from atmospheric or orbital bombardment. This warrants further investigation, but it will require an archaeological dig to reveal what happened here.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:179`

## ancrel.104.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.104.name&lang=en)

### 中文

穿墟而行

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:167`

### English

Sifting Through the Wreckage

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:180`

## ancrel.104.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.104.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这场灾难几乎是彻底的，但仍有一些零散的记录保存了下来。似乎[From.Planet.GetName]的人们因为外星人正在观察他们的想法而感到困扰。不明飞行物出现、动物残害和绑架的频繁报道，使得这颗星球陷入了疯狂。作为他们对观察的回应，无数的资源被投入到巨型的探测阵列、反宇宙飞船防御系统和地下庇护所上，以保护人民。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:168`

### English

The devastation is almost total, but a few scattered records have survived. It seems that the people of [From.Planet.GetName] were obsessed with the idea that aliens were observing them. Frequent reports of Unidentified Flying Objects, animal mutilation, and abductions drove the planet into a frenzy. In reaction countless resources were poured into vast detection arrays, anti-spacecraft defenses, and underground shelters to protect the population.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:181`

## ancrel.105.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.105.name&lang=en)

### 中文

发现记录

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:169`

### English

Records Discovered

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:182`

## ancrel.105.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.105.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

从零散的记录中我们拼凑出了更多的细节。随着星球上越来越多的资源被投入到毫无成果的反不明飞行物措施中，他们的经济和社会开始崩溃。很多地区出现了电力不足与粮食短缺的现象，大多数领域的科技发展停滞不前，一股深深的绝望感弥漫在社会各阶层之中。在散乱的信息中我们发现了某种\"终极护盾\"，这种护盾随着[From.Planet.GetName]上情况局势恶化也变得越来越普及。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:170`

### English

More details have been assembled from the scattered records we have recovered. As an ever-increasing percentage of planetary resources were poured into anti-UFO measures without any noticeable success their economy and society began to collapse. Brownouts and food shortages were endemic, most fields of technology stagnated, and a deep sense of hopelessness prevailed at every level of society. Scattered references have been found to some kind of "ultimate shield", which become increasingly common as reports about the general situation on [From.Planet.GetName] get worse.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:183`

## ancrel.106.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.106.name&lang=en)

### 中文

揭示命运

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:171`

### English

Fate Uncovered

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:184`

## ancrel.106.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.106.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

从一座坚固的军事基地发现的残破的数据库中我们得知了[From.Planet.GetName]的悲惨命运。由于经济全面崩盘，他们的反不明飞行物措施完全停摆，[From.Planet.GetName]的统治者们启动了他们的\"终极护盾\"。然而这并不是一个用以保护[From.Planet.GetName]的护罩，相反它是对人口与基础设施的一次彻底的核毁灭，来确保这两者都不会被外来入侵者占领。他们做出这一决定显然是受到了来自[arc_anomaly_planet.GetName]的“外星人信号”的刺激。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:172`

### English

The discovery of a partially preserved databank in a hardened military bunker has revealed the sad fate of [From.Planet.GetName]. Faced with a general economic collapse which would have lead to the end of their anti-UFO measures, the rulers of [From.Planet.GetName] activated their "ultimate shield". However, this was not a shield to protect [From.Planet.GetName], rather it was the complete nuclear annihilation of the population and infrastructure to ensure neither could be taken by alien invaders. This decision was apparently spurred on by the discovery of an "alien signal" coming from [arc_anomaly_planet.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:185`

## ancrel.107.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.107.name&lang=en)

### 中文

发现真相

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:173`

### English

Finding the Truth

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:186`

## ancrel.107.nothing.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.107.nothing.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

对[From.Planet.GetName]的彻查表明这里与外星生命没有任何联系。要么这里从来没有什么外星人的信号发出，要么就是某种原因使得这里没能留下任何痕迹。不管事实究竟如何，对[From.Planet.GetName]的调查还显示这里蕴含有一些矿藏。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:174`

### English

A thorough investigation of [From.Planet.GetName] has revealed absolutely nothing related to aliens. Either there was never any alien signal originating here, or whatever caused it left without leaving any traces. However, the close inspection of [From.Planet.GetName] has revealed some minerals.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:187`

## ancrel.107.junk.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.107.junk.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

“外星信号”的源头找到了，然而这并不是什么潜伏者的外星入侵，实际上信号来自于一个远古的勘探标记，这应该是由某个自动探测装置在[From.Planet.GetName]探测出一条有价值的小矿脉后所设置的。这个标记已经受损，只能发出断断续续的微弱信号。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:175`

### English

The source of the "alien signal" has been discovered, but rather than being any sinister alien invasion force the signal originates from an ancient survey marker which was apparently set by an automated probe which had discovered a minor vein of valuable resources on [From.Planet.GetName]. The marker is damaged and transmits only intermittently and very faintly.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:188`

## ancrel.107.aliens1.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.107.aliens1.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.Planet.GetName]上外星信号的源头是一座未知设计的废弃哨站。从其中遗留的文件能够看出，他们的计划并不是要策划一次入侵，而是试图与[arc_site_planet.GetName]建立联系并将其引荐给星系中的其他文明。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:176`

### English

[From.Planet.GetName] contains an abandoned outpost of an unknown design, the source of the alien signal. However, rather than planning an invasion, abandoned files reveal their plan to make contact with [arc_site_planet.GetName] and introduce them to the galactic community.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:189`

## ancrel.107.aliens2.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.107.aliens2.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.Planet.GetName]上外星信号的源头是一座未知设计的废弃哨站。其中被遗弃的数据库记录着详尽的入侵计划，并打算在入侵[arc_site_planet.GetName]之后利用\"苦痛农场\"来柔化当地人口的肉质以便于食用。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:177`

### English

[From.Planet.GetName] contains an abandoned outpost of an unknown design, the source of the alien signal. Abandoned databanks contain detailed plans to invade [arc_site_planet.GetName] and process the population in "pain farms" to tenderize their flesh before they are consumed.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:190`

## ancient_battle_site_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancient_battle_site_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

对[From.Planet.GetName]的扫描显示，在该地区存在大量的有加工痕迹的物品以及零零散散的放射性物质，但从轨道上看不到什么细节。为了了解更多信息，我们需要着陆进行实地调查。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:178`

### English

Scans of [From.Planet.GetName] have revealed an area with packed with refined material and scattered radiologicals, although little is visible from orbit. To find out more we will need to investigate the site on the ground.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:191`

## omnirelic_site

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aomnirelic_site&lang=en)

### 中文

$ancrel.21.name$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:179`

### English

$ancrel.21.name$

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:192`

## cool_relic_world_site_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acool_relic_world_site_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

整个行星都被巨大的建筑物所覆盖着，这个遗落的大都会星肯定有秘密等待发掘。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:180`

### English

An entire planet covered in vast structures, this fallen megalopolis surely has secrets to be unearthed.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:193`

## ancrel.108.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.108.name&lang=en)

### 中文

古战场遗址

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:181`

### English

Ancient Battle Site

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:194`

## ancrel.108.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.108.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们发现了被精炼过的矿物，虽然进一步研究亟待展开，但是我们初步认为这是一个巨型战场遗址。看起来，其中一方进行了轨道空降并与星球上的防御军发生了交火。进攻方为什么没有进行轨道轰炸？最后的胜利者究竟是谁？一切都悬而未决。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:182`

### English

The refined material has been uncovered, and while further study is needed, our initial impression is that this is the remains of a vast battle site. It appears that one side descended from orbit and was engaged by planetbound defenders. Why there was no orbital bombardment and who the victor was remains unclear.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:195`

## ancrel.109.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.109.name&lang=en)

### 中文

被遗忘的怨恨

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:183`

### English

Forgotten Grudge

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:196`

## ancrel.109.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.109.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们得出结论，来自“毁灭者”，即一族高维游牧民的军队入侵了[From.Planet.GetName]，入侵时此行星属于“抵抗组织”，一个由数个不同物种组成的联盟。通过零散的记录，我们认定这场战争主要是在星系的其他部分进行，而[From.Planet.GetName]是抵抗组织寄希望于在“毁灭者”的魔爪之外建立一个秘密殖民地的尝试。这场战役和战争整体的结果仍不得而知，但对战争机器残骸的研究已经获得了一些有价值的信息。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:184`

### English

We have concluded that forces from "The Destroyers", a race of extra-dimensional nomads, invaded [From.Planet.GetName], which at the time belonged to "Those-who-resist", a coalition of several different species. From scattered records we believe that that the war was mostly fought elsewhere in the galaxy and [From.Planet.GetName] was an attempt by Those-who-resist to establish a secret colony outside the reach of The Destroyers. The outcome of this battle and the larger war remains unknown, but study of the wrecked war machines has yielded some valuable information.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:197`

## zroni_digsite_1_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Azroni_digsite_1_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一个未知文明所建立的古老殖民地。尽管没有任何灾难迹象，但很显然这些定居者们突然之间就放弃了这个居住地。这儿到底发生了什么呢？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:185`

### English

An ancient colony from an unknown civilization. Despite no obvious sings of calamity, the settlement was clearly abruptly abandoned by its denizens. What happened here?

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:198`

## zroni_digsite_2_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Azroni_digsite_2_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在被遗弃的泽珞族殖民地中获得的坐标把我们带到了这里。我们需要再次展开探险，调查这颗初看平淡无奇的星球。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:186`

### English

The coordinates provided in abandoned Zroni colony directed us here. We will need to deploy another expedition to explore this seemingly ordinary world.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:199`

## zroni_digsite_3_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Azroni_digsite_3_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们之前调查的尽是些被遗弃的殖民地，但依据上一个发掘遗址的指引，[From.Planet.GetName]好像有着一座遗弃的神庙。那个地方为什么对这群人而言具有重要的精神意义呢？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:187`

### English

Previously we have only explored abandoned colonies, but according to the last dig site, [From.Planet.GetName] houses an abandoned shrine. Why would that place be of spiritual significance to this people?

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:200`

## zroni_digsite_4_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Azroni_digsite_4_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

从那座泽珞族神庙中我们发现了一个科研复合体的坐标。在轨扫描无法找到任何记忆水晶的共振信号，我们需要下到地表进行进一步研究。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:188`

### English

With the revelations from the Zroni's temple we have obtained the coordinates of a scientific complex. Orbital scans could not detect any resonance from their Memory Crystal, we will need to go down to the surface to learn more.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:201`

## zroni_digsite_5_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Azroni_digsite_5_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

之前探索过的所有泽珞族星球，在考古学的重要性上都比不过[From.Planet.GetName]。这颗[From.Planet.GetPlanetMoon]的一些建筑即使从太空上俯瞰都肉眼可见。[From.GetName]一定曾是泽珞帝国的中央星球。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:189`

### English

The archeological significance of all the previously explored Zroni worlds pales in comparison to [From.Planet.GetName]. This [From.Planet.GetPlanetMoon] houses structures visible from space. [From.GetName] must have been a central world of the Zroni empire.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:202`

## zroni_digsite_6_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Azroni_digsite_6_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

泽珞星可以说是一个灵能活动的漩涡。在我们尝试派遣一个离队小组到地表之前，我们需要对过去用来探测记忆水晶的工具进行重新校准。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:190`

### English

Zron is a maelstrom of psionic activity. The tools we have used to detect Memory Crystals in the past will need to be recalibrated before we can even attempt sending an away team to the surface.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:203`

## zroni_digsite_1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Azroni_digsite_1&lang=en)

### 中文

遗弃的殖民地遗址

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:191`

### English

Abandoned Colony Ruins

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:204`

## zroni_digsite_2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Azroni_digsite_2&lang=en)

### 中文

泽珞族信标

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:192`

### English

Zroni Beacon

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:205`

## ancrel.1.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.1.name&lang=en)

### 中文

神秘的废墟

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:198`

### English

Mysterious Ruins

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:211`

## ancrel.1.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.1.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.Planet.GetName]上存在一座古老的废弃殖民地遗址。从轨道上的观测情况来看，并没有明显的迹象显示发生过灾难导致大量居民丧生或者迫使他们逃离殖民地。如果我们想要找到答案，一场地面调查行动势在必行。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:199`

### English

[From.Planet.GetName] contains the remains of an ancient, abandoned colony. From orbit there are no obvious signs of disaster which would have killed the inhabitants or compelled them to flee. If we want answers, an expedition to the surface will be required.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:212`

## ancrel.2.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.2.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这片奇怪的废墟除了疏于管理之外没有任何损坏的迹象。住所，工厂，未知用途的建筑，四处空空荡荡，了无人烟。人们都去哪儿了？似乎在殖民地中心的一座大型建筑物内能找到问题的答案。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:200`

### English

The strange ruins show no sign of damage beyond neglect. Homes, factories, buildings of unknown use: they all stand empty and abandoned. Where did everyone go? It seems likely that a large building in the center of the colony may hold the answers.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:213`

## ancrel.3.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.3.name&lang=en)

### 中文

奇怪的水晶

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:201`

### English

Strange Crystal

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:214`

## ancrel.3.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.3.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在我们现在确信是殖民地中央政府的地方，我们发现了一块大型水晶。从某种难以言明的角度来看，这颗水晶似乎想要被发现，并且正尝试着同我们沟通。也许随着研究的进一步深入，我们可以进行交流？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:202`

### English

A large crystal has been found inside what we now know to have been the colony's central administrative center. In some way we cannot fully describe, the crystal seems to have wanted to be found, and is trying to communicate with us. Perhaps with further study, communication will be possible?

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:215`

## ancrel.3.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.3.a&lang=en)

### 中文

对水晶进行探测

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:203`

### English

Probe the Crystal

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:216`

## ancrel.4.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在饱经失败之母的考验之后，我们的研究取得了重大突破。这个水晶是某种形式的灵能档案库，但可惜的是，存在上面的记录已经支离破碎残缺不全了。\n\n我们所能知道的是，这里曾是一个§H泽珞族§!殖民地，他们是一个具有灵能天赋的种族，并且发现了我们称之为“虚境”的领域。当泽珞族的人口膨胀到某个阈值的时候，看起来他们的集体潜意识开始和原始虚境在梦境中产生交集，这导致了大量清晰可辨的梦境。很快，泽珞族便开始研究这种奇异现象，并发现了用灵能将自身投射入虚境的方法。在虚境中，他们发现他们能够利用灵能随心所欲地操控现实的结构。\n\n没过多久，泽珞族发现他们可以肉身进入虚境并且过上神灵般的生活，随他们的癖好改造虚境维度……\n\n档案记录到这里就结束了，没有任何信息告诉我们[From.Planet.GetName]的定居点在此之后发生了什么。尽管这里已经没有更多的答案了，但是我们了解到还有一个泽珞族殖民地，那里也许保存有一座更完整的档案库……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:204`

### English

After a great deal of trial and error, a breakthrough has been made. The crystal is some kind of Psionic Archive, but its records are fragmented.\n\nWhat we know is that this was a colony of §HThe Zroni§!: a psionically gifted race who discovered a dimension which came to be known as "The Shroud". Once the Zroni population reached critical mass, it seems that their collective unconscious minds began to make contact with the proto-Shroud in dreams, leading to mass lucid dreaming. The phenomenon was studied, and the Zroni discovered how to psionically project themselves into the Shroud - at which point they also found that they could manipulate the very fabric of reality, using their psionic powers.\n\nIt wasn't long before the Zroni discovered they could physically enter the Shroud and live like gods, transforming the dimension to suit their whims...\n\nThe Archive's records end there, with no information on what happened to the inhabitants of [From.Planet.GetName]. But while there are no answers here, we have learned of another Zroni colony which may contain a more complete Archive...

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:217`

## ancrel.5.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.5.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.Planet.GetName]上存在着另一个废弃的泽珞族殖民地。为了探寻秘密的真相，我们首先需要找到并打开这颗星球上的灵能档案库。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:205`

### English

[From.Planet.GetName] contains the ruins of another abandoned Zroni colony. The first step to solving this mystery is to find- and make contact with this world's Psionic Archive.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:218`

## ancrel.6.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.6.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

利用之前调查水晶建立的流程，这回我们接入这一块水晶就变得轻而易举了。这个水晶的记录同样残缺不全，但是我们发现了能够进入虚境的另一个优越性，虚境同时连接着整个现实星系，也许乃至整个宇宙的每个角落。\n\n如果一个人能够进入虚境，那么这也意味着它能够从任何已知的地方出现，这种能够在任何现实地点间进行“灵能跃迁”的能力让泽珞族遍布整个星系。由于他们在虚境中渡过了大部分时间，泽珞族永久殖民地数量相对较少，且大都临近母星。\n\n这块水晶告诉了我们另一处神殿的位置。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:206`

### English

It was much easier to establish contact with the crystal this time around, using protocols we established with the previous one. Again its records are fragmented, but we have learned that another advantage of being able to enter the Shroud was that the Shroud touches all parts of the physical galaxy - perhaps all parts of the universe - simultaneously.\n\nIf one can enter the Shroud, it is possible to exit it at any known location. This ability to "Psionically jump" between physical locations allowed the Zroni to spread to all corners of the galaxy. Because they spent most of their time within the Shroud, the Zroni established relatively few permanent colonies. Those they did set up were mostly located near to their homeworld.\n\nThis crystal has given us the location of a sort of shrine.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:219`

## ancrel.7.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.7.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

现在我们可以很容易地找到灵能档案库并读取其中的信息。几乎在我们的考察队刚一着陆的时候就已经完成了连接，然后我们就可以获取更多详细的记录和概念。从中我们了解到，虚境赋予泽珞族神格的好事并非天上掉馅饼。\n\n虚境中移山填海般的变化需要大量的能源，这些能源必须来自于某个地方，泽珞族最终发现，星系中央的巨型黑洞正是能量的源头。随着泽珞族不停地改造虚境，黑洞奇点的影响范围也在扩大，吞噬了星系中心的大量质量并将其转化为能量抽取进虚境之中。这个发现震动了整个泽珞族，渐渐地讨论变成了争吵，变成了分裂，最终演变成两大主要派系间的内战。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:207`

### English

Finding and communicating with the Psionic Archives has become much easier now. Contact can be made as soon as our expedition lands, at which point we can access more detailed records and concepts. From what we have learned here, some things are too good to be true - and so it was with the godhood granted to the Zroni by the Shroud.\n\nThe energy required to power the vast changes in the Shroud's composition had to come from somewhere, and for them it originated from the supermassive black hole at the center of our galaxy. As the Zroni made their changes within the Shroud, so the singularity expanded: consuming the mass of the galactic center and converting it into energy which was then siphoned into the Shroud. This discovery shook the Zroni: debate turn to argument; arguments led to division; division turned to civil war between their two main factions.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:220`

## ancrel.8.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.8.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

从水晶中，我们找寻了到了关于两派内战更进一步的细节。其中一派，被称为“升格派”，他们认为现实宇宙的命运之于泽珞族无关痛痒，他们能够在虚境中继续他们为所欲为的生活直到在无比遥远的未来整个星系都被吞噬——即使到了那时候，还有其他星系可以消耗，又甚至其他宇宙也能够成为泽珞族的燃料。\n\n另一派，称为“福音派”，他们激烈地反对“升格派”的观点——泽珞族不应该为了自己神一般的生活便毁灭整个星系！即使离开了虚境，他们的灵能天赋也能让他们过着乌托邦般的生活。\n\n另一方面，一处泽珞族研究哨站的位置被纳入我们的数据库中。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:208`

### English

Further details of the Zroni civil war factions have been extracted from the crystal. One faction - which came to be known as "The Divine" - felt that the fate of the physical galaxy was of no concern to the Zroni, who could continue their omnipotent existence in the Shroud until the entire galaxy was consumed in some unimaginably far off future. Even then, there are other galaxies, perhaps even other universes which could conceivably be consumed.\n\nThe other faction, "The Saviors", were aghast at this; the Zroni should not destroy the galaxy to live as parasitic gods! Even outside of the Shroud, their Psionic abilities guaranteed them a utopian existence.\n\nThe location of a Zroni research outpost was included in the torrent of data.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:221`

## ancrel.9.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.9.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

奇怪的是，在[From.Planet.GetName]上很难通过三角定位确定档案库的位置——但我们一找到档案库，就很容易理解之前难以寻找它的原因了：这里的水晶档案库被人为破坏，里面已经没有我们可以访问的记录了。然而，我们在周边探测到了一个微弱的信号，或许希望依旧存在。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:209`

### English

It was strangely difficult to triangulate the Archive on [From.Planet.GetName] - but once we did it was easy to see why it had been so elusive: the crystal has been shattered, and contains no records that we can access. However, hope persists as we have detected a faint signal somewhere nearby.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:222`

## ancrel.9.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.9.a&lang=en)

### 中文

继续搜索

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:210`

### English

Continue searching.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:223`

## ancrel.10.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.10.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在一个像是某种实验室的地方，我们定位出了一个小得多的灵能水晶。从记录中我们得知，这些泽珞族人非常沮丧，他们的同侪嘲笑他们的研究，认为他们只是不知廉耻的骗子。他们认为泽珞族不再是虚境中唯一存在的实体，他们整个种族的恐惧和渴求的情绪正在逐渐融合形成一些明晰的智慧实体。\n\n这些实体希图变得更为强大，便开始对合适的泽珞族人的情绪和思维施加潜移默化的影响，逐渐使他们的行为变得极端，并可能最终引发了泽珞族内战。\n\n在水晶中我们发现了又一条信息，标注出泽珞族的核心星球之一的位置。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:211`

### English

A much smaller psionic crystal has been located in some kind of laboratory. The Zroni who recorded their findings within were extremely frustrated: their research had been ridiculed by their peers, and was largely discredited. They believed that the Zroni were no longer the only entities in the Shroud, and that the collective fears and desires of their entire race were coalescing into distinct, sapient entities.\n\nYearning to grow stronger, these entities had begun to subtly influence the emotions and thoughts of suitable Zroni: slowly causing their behaviors to become more extreme. This may have directly caused the Zroni civil war.\n\nWe also discovered a location reference for one of the Zroni's core worlds.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:224`

## ancrel.11.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.11.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

不像先前的泽珞族星球，[From.Planet.GetName]明显是一颗核心星球，地表上存有数座城市以及大量的大小定居点。此处又同其他殖民地一样空荡，没有任何灾难亦或是死亡的迹象，这些居民的命运无从知晓。我们已经检测到数个水晶档案库的存在，将准备进行调查。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:212`

### English

Unlike the previous Zroni worlds, [From.Planet.GetName] was clearly a core world, with several cities of varying size covering the surface. Like the others it now sits empty, showing no signs of disaster or death; the fate of its population is unknown. Several Archive crystals have been detected, and will need to be investigated.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:225`

## ancrel.12.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.12.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

大多数能读取的记录都着重记载了战争的过程。我们得出的结论是，人们的想愿会影响特定主题记录的“强度”，这使得大多数人关心的问题会被着重记载。并不出人意料，战争的过程在每个人的脑海中都有.\n\n大多数泽珞族人站在福音派一方，然而，尽管福音派拥有大量的支持者，他们的局面却非常不利。升格派完全不考虑对星系产生的影响，从而可以肆无忌惮的施展他们的灵能力量，而福音派则受限于他们能从自身获取的力量的多少。\n\n起初，这场战争似乎呈一边倒，但是福音派的一位英雄取得了一个可怕而又至关重要的发现，这使得局势变得扑朔迷离。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:213`

### English

Most of the accessible records focus on the course of the war. Our working theory is that the mood of the population influences the "strength" of the record about a subject, meaning the most thought-about issues are recorded with greater prominence. Unsurprisingly, the course of the war was on everyone's mind.\n\nThe majority of the Zroni sided with the Saviors, but while they had an advantage in numbers they bore one significant disadvantage: heedless of their effects on the galaxy, the Divine were free to use their full Psionic might; meanwhile the Saviors were limited to whatever power could be drawn from their own bodies.\n\nAt first the war seemed hopeless, but eventually a hero amongst the Saviors would make a terrible - yet vital - discovery.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:226`

## ancrel.12.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.12.a&lang=en)

### 中文

调查所取得的发现

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:214`

### English

Investigate the Discovery

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:227`

## ancrel.13.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.13.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

水晶告诉我们，曾经有一个疯狂的泽珞族研究员，致力于研究一种链式反应理论，将灵能使用在身体内部并在受控的灵能链式反应中燃烧掉自己的身体，这种理论是如此的离经叛道，其同侪一致拒绝予以接受。根据他最后的遗言，他崇高牺牲后残存的灰烬经过检测被确认为一种难以置信的强大灵能源，其富含的能量等同于他一生的灵能潜势总和，而且可以直接为另一位灵能者所用。\n\n这个水晶最后也指给了我们泽珞族母星的位置！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:215`

### English

The crystal tells us how a rogue Zroni researcher - working on a theory which his peers refused to believe - turned his power inwards, consuming his own body in a contained Psionic chain-reaction. Following his last instructions, the dusty residue of his supreme sacrifice was tested and confirmed to be an incredibly potent source of Psionic energy. It was essentially the sum total Psionic potential of his life, rendered instantly usable by another Psion.\n\nThe crystal finally pointed us towards the location of the Zroni homeworld!

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:228`

## ancrel.13.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.13.a&lang=en)

### 中文

这些灰尘……竟然是……泽珞族！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:216`

### English

The Dust... is the Zroni!

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:229`

## ancrel.15.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.15.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

泽珞星已经被灵能风暴彻底摧毁，看上去只在某些时间才能和宇宙其他空间处于同一位面。无论发生过多么可怕的灾难，泽珞族应该自我们脚下的星球起源。\n\n就像先前发现的殖民地一样，泽珞星依旧空无一人。但是周围的空间中充斥着水晶信号，由于存在如此多的干扰信号，这里的大气又非常诡异，搜寻有用信息可能要花费一定的时间。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:217`

### English

Zron is wracked by storms of psionic energy, and appears to only be partially in phase with the rest of the universe. Whatever calamity befell the Zroni seems to have originated here.\n\nLike all the previous colonies, it is devoid of Zroni but is teeming with crystalline signals. Given this much signal noise - on top of the strange atmospheric conditions - it may take some time for us to locate any useful information.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:230`

## ancrel.16.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.16.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们发现了数个水晶并成功与其通讯，其中包括一位被流放的虚境实体研究员的记录。有了这个，我们对泽珞族的战争有了更多的了解。\n\n以生命为代价得到的泽珞尘为福音派带来了转机，他们与升格派有了一战之力。然而，随着战败的阴影逐渐笼罩，升格派的愤怒和恐惧更进一步催化了新的可怕的虚境实体的诞生。这种反馈循环不断发展，将升格派推入疯狂的深渊。他们不断地制造更为骇人听闻的暴行，而福音派则越来越不计代价地试图终结这场战争。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:218`

### English

We have found- and communicated with several crystals, including one from the outcast Shroud-entity researchers. With these, we are learning more about the Zroni's war.\n\nUsing the Zro dust required great sacrifice from the Saviors, but with it they were able to turn the tide of their war against the Divine. As their defeat loomed, the ever-increasing rage and fear of the Divine spawned new and terrible entities within the Shroud. The feedback loop grew, pushing the Divine into the depths of madness. They were driven to ever-worsening atrocities, urging the Saviors to end the war at any cost.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:231`

## ancrel.16.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.16.a&lang=en)

### 中文

继续……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:219`

### English

Continue...

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:232`

## ancrel.17.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.17.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

随着我们与更多水晶建立通讯，越来越多的细节浮出水面：升格派在即将败亡之际决定实施最终计划：轮回之终末。星系核心处黑洞的增长将被加速，以尽可能快的速度吞噬掉整个星系。\n\n在最坏的情况下，福音派（和所有其他物种）都将被毁灭，留下升格派在一段漫长却有限的时间内统治虚境，但也有可能黑洞的膨胀会达到自我维续的程度，不仅吞噬掉本星系，还会毁灭宇宙本身，给升格派提供无穷无尽的能量——就算不是无穷尽，因为能量总量和无穷足够接近，因此没有任何实际差别。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:220`

### English

As we communicate with more crystals, more details emerge. On the brink of defeat, the Divine decided upon their ultimate plan: the end of the cycle. The growth of the black hole galactic core would be accelerated to consume the galaxy as soon as possible.\n\nAt worst, the Saviors - and every other species in the galaxy - would be destroyed, leaving the Divine to rule the shroud for a vast but finite time. It was, however, possible that the expansion would become self-sustaining, consuming not just the galaxy but the universe itself: providing a source of energy which may not be infinite, but would at least come close enough amount to make no practical difference.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:233`

## ancrel.18.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.18.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们已经知悉了泽珞族的末路：当福音派知晓了升格派的最终计划后，加之升格派在虚境的防御简直固若金汤，他们只剩一条路可走了。毁灭已然近在眼前，他们选择牺牲几乎所有人口，只留下最后一人，最后的福音派将使用整个物种献祭而来的灵能尘去消灭每一个泽珞人——不放过升格派的任何一人——从而彻底终结这场浩劫。\n\n泽珞族被几乎不留痕迹地从宇宙抹去，只剩下一小点未使用的泽珞尘……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:221`

### English

We have learned the fate of the Zroni: when the Saviors learned of the Divine's plan, they knew there was only one possible counter to the Divine's almost-impenetrable defenses in the Shroud. Faced with annihilation, they chose to sacrifice their entire population save for one Zroni. This last remaining Savior used the psionic residue of an entire species to destroy every Zroni - and thus every Divine - in existence, ending the threat for all time.\n\nThe Zroni were wiped out almost without a trace, save for a small amount of unconsumed dust.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:234`

## ancrel.18.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.18.a&lang=en)

### 中文

难以置信！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:222`

### English

Inconceivable!

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:235`

## ancrel.19.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.19.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

当我们的探险队准备离开时，其中一块灵能晶体突然与我们重新建立了联系。它表示自己将会对我们有用，应该带着它一起走。即使它所言为虚，其它晶体也没有表现出任何智慧。因此我们应当带走它进行进一步的调查。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:223`

### English

As our expedition made ready to leave, one of the psionic crystals suddenly re-established contact. It has indicated that it could prove useful to us, should we decide to bring it with us. Even if the claim is untrue, none of the other crystals we encountered showed signs of sapience; this could warrant further investigation.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:236`

## ancrel.19.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.19.a&lang=en)

### 中文

拿走水晶。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:224`

### English

Take the crystal.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:237`

## ancrel.21.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.21.name&lang=en)

### 中文

废弃星球

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:225`

### English

Ruined World

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:238`

## ancrel.21.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.21.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

大量年久失修的建筑结构覆盖在整颗星球的表面。想必这里一度人口数以亿计，但现在却空无一人。不论谁在这里生活过，他们都一定留下了发生事情的线索，但要找到这些线索需要对行星表面进行仔细检查。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:226`

### English

Massive, dilapidated structures cover the entire surface of this world. At one time, this city-planet must have been home to billions - but now it is empty. Whoever lived here must have left clues about what happened to them, but finding them will require a detailed examination of the surface.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:239`

## ancrel.22.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.22.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

对废墟的搜索绝大多数毫无成效，好在尚有一些线索指出了我们要搜寻的正确方向：我们发现了一处似乎是某种行星级数据处理和存储中心的地方。如果我们可以找到一种获取其中档案的方法，将会对解读这颗行星神秘过往起到不可估量的巨大作用。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:227`

### English

After much fruitless sifting through the ruins, a number of clues directed our search towards remains of a kind of planetary data processing- and storage center. If we can find a way to gain access to whatever records remain, they will be invaluable for unraveling this planet's mysterious past.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:240`

## ancrel.23.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.23.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在找到数据中心的入口并打通穿越废墟的道路后，重新激活和接入计算机系统出人意料的容易。自修复电路和学习算法很快上线以恢复受限的功能并开始与我们的计算机进行通讯。\n\n事情的原因很快变得明了，[From.Planet.GetName]曾一度是一个被称为§H“有机全典”§!的机械帝国的首都。因其致力的目的而得名，此帝国由机器人和义体人混合组成，曾试图获取星系中一切有机生命的样本，以便防止这些生命在未知的星系级灾难中灭绝。\n\n从现存记录中，我们搜集到的信息表明这一目的在他们年代的其他星系势力中并不受欢迎，他们最终被多个势力的联合攻击打败和毁灭。\n\n然而，在该帝国覆灭前，他们已经取样了许多种族，并投入了大量精力研发出一台可以在有需要时重新创造出这些种族的装置进行。这一装置，即有机全典本身，被保存在附近某处。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:228`

### English

Once we found an entrance to the data center and forced passage through the wreckage, it was a surprisingly easy task to reactivate- and interface with the computer systems. Self-repairing circuits and learning algorithms quickly came online to restore limited functionality, and communicate with our own computers.\n\nThe cause for this burst of activity quickly became clear: [From.Planet.GetName] was once the capital of a machine empire known as §H"The Omnicodex."§! Named for their driving purpose, the Omni were a hybrid of robots and cyborgs who had attempted to assimilate samples of all biological life in the galaxy, in order to prevent their extinction in the face of unknown galactic calamity.\n\nFrom the records which survive, we gather that this course of action was not popular amongst the other galactic powers of their era; the Omni were eventually defeated in a joint campaign conducted by several of these empires.\n\nBefore their downfall, the Omni managed to sample a great deal of races, and much work was put into developing a device capable of re-creating them if the need should ever arise. This device - The Omnicodex proper - is apparently stored somewhere nearby.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:241`

## ancrel.24.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.24.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们已经找到了有机全典。这件独特的装置内含建造其的机械帝国曾取样的每个物种的基因序列，并可以以基本元素作为原料重新创造出他们。不幸的是，看起来此机械帝国在覆灭前并没有足够的时间来完善这台装置，所以我们无法选择在激活该装置时要创造哪一个被取样的种族。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:229`

### English

The Omnicodex has been unearthed. This unique device contains the genetic sequence of every race that the Omni were able to sample, and can re-create them using only basic elements as raw materials. Unfortunately, it seems the Omni did not have time to perfect it before meeting their annihilation, so there is no means of selecting which of the many sampled races would be created in any given activation of the device.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:242`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AANCREL_MECHANO_CAT&lang=en)

### 中文

行星规模的机器

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:232`

### English

A Planetary Machine

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:247`

## ANCREL_MECHANO_CAT_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AANCREL_MECHANO_CAT_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

这颗行星两极之间的所有表面均被锈迹斑斑的古老机械所覆盖，而这些机械中的大部分包裹着一种不明的有机组织。如此技术奇观是出自什么样的生物之手？他们又发生了什么？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:233`

### English

This planet is covered from pole to pole in rusting, ancient machinery, much of which is itself covered in an unidentified, organic tissue. What manner of creatures could have built this technological curiosity, and what happened to them?

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:248`

## baol_chain_title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Abaol_chain_title&lang=en)

先驱者——巴奥

### English

Precursors - The Baol

## baol_chain_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Abaol_chain_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的研究人员发现了一些七百万年前的数据晶体，晶体内按年代顺序记录了一族被称作§Y古茹努尔§!的太空战士的事迹，以及名为§Y巴奥有机体§!的外星生命，其行星被古茹努尔所入侵。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:235`

### English

Our researchers have uncovered seven million year old datacrystals, chronicling the existence of a space-faring warrior race known as the §YGrunur§!, and the §YBaol Organism§!, an alien lifeform whose planets the Grunur invaded.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:251`

### 中文

我们从古代古茹努尔数据晶体中提取到了另一个前§Y巴奥§!殖民地的坐标，应该对该遗址进行深入调查。

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATHE_LAST_BAOL&lang=en)

古茹努尔求救信号

### English

Grunur Distress Signal

## THE_LAST_BAOL_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATHE_LAST_BAOL_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的传感器收到了似乎包含古茹努尔数字签名的求救信号。在这一迄今未被发现的恒星系里，这个早已消失的物种还有可能留下些什么呢？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:244`

### English

Our sensors have picked up what appears to be a distress signal bearing Grunur datalogical signatures. What could possibly remain of this long lost species, in this hitherto-undiscovered star system?

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:261`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AFERROPHAGE_CURE&lang=en)

研究噬铁体治疗方案

### English

Develop A Cure For The Ferrophage

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:262`

## FERROPHAGE_CURE_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AFERROPHAGE_CURE_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]上的C.A.R.E交互界面请求我们帮助开发一种治疗以金属为食的病毒的方法，这种病毒已经困扰了它数千年。\n\n如何针对这种古老的外星生化病毒的开发解毒剂，使这里的人工智能免受荼毒，还有待研究。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:246`

### English

The C.A.R.E Interactive Interface on [Root.GetName] has asked for our assistance in developing a cure for the metal-eating virus that has plagued it for thousands of years.\n\nHow one might develop an antidote for an ancient biological alien virus inflicting an artificial intelligence remains to be seen.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:263`

## ancrel.2000.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.2000.name&lang=en)

### 中文

古茹努尔

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:257`

### English

The Grunur

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:276`

## ancrel.2000.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.2000.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]的船员们兴奋地报告称他们在[From.GetName]上发现了一处古代太空文明的遗迹，其似乎于七百万年前定居于这一行星！\n\n尽管这些自称§Y古茹努尔§!的物种离开[From.GetStarName]星系的原因尚不明确，我们的科学家已经在这颗行星上分离出了一处很有发掘价值的考古遗址。\n\n也许进一步的研究会找到更多线索。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:258`

### English

The [Root.GetName] crew are eager to report they have uncovered the remains of an ancient space-faring species on [From.GetName], who appear to have inhabited the planet some seven million years ago!\n\nWhile it is unclear why this species, who called themselves the §YGrunur§!, disappeared from the [From.GetStarName] system, our scientists have isolated a promising archaeological dig site on their planet.\n\nPerhaps further study will yield more clues.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:277`

## ancrel.2001.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.2001.name&lang=en)

### 中文

巴奥有机体

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:259`

### English

The Baol Organism

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:278`

## ancrel.2001.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.2001.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在对发掘现场找到的古代数据晶体进行解密后，我们获得了一份§Y古茹努尔§!战功的编年史。虽然大多只是他们的本地事务，对我们意义不大，其中仍有数卷详细描述了§Y古茹努尔§!与被称为§Y巴奥有机体§!的外星物种的第一次接触。\n\n除了§Y巴奥§!是一种巨型植物类物种，在群星间播种了众多殖民地以外，我们目前对它还所知无几。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:260`

### English

Decoded ancient datacrystals have been found at the dig site, chronicling the §YGrunur§!'s military exploits. While most are local and of little interest, several volumes detail the §YGrunur§!'s first encounter with an alien species referred to as §YThe Baol Organism§!.\n\nLittle more can be gleaned about the §YBaol§! at this point, save that they were a massive, plantoid species, and had many colonies seeded among the stars.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:279`

## ancrel.2001.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.2001.a&lang=en)

### 中文

很迷人。继续研究下去！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:261`

### English

Enticing. Keep digging!

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:280`

## ancrel.2002.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.2002.name&lang=en)

### 中文

巴奥有机体

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:262`

### English

The Baol Organism

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:281`

## ancrel.2002.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.2002.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

数据晶体中的记录表明§Y古茹努尔§!曾对§Y巴奥§!发动战争，希图夺取其行星据称拥有的丰富资源。我们已经成功从一份该类型的记录中提取出了一组恒星系坐标！\n\n我们的考古学家正在将该行星的位置上传至我们的系统中。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:263`

### English

Datacrystal records reveal that the §YGrunur§! waged war against the §YBaol§!, desiring the plentiful resources purportedly found on their planets. A set of system coordinates has been successfully extracted from one such record!\n\nOur archaeologists are uploading the planet's location to our systems now.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:282`

## ancrel.2002.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.2002.a&lang=en)

### 中文

或许对[baol_colony_1.GetName]开展深入调查会让我们获益良多。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:264`

### English

Perhaps a closer investigation of [baol_colony_1.GetName] will prove fruitful.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:283`

## ancrel.2003.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.2003.name&lang=en)

### 中文

前巴奥殖民地：不毛之地

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:265`

### English

Former Baol Colony: The Barren

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:284`

## ancrel.2003.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.2003.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

尽管在[first_grunur_site.GetName]发现的古代§Y古茹努尔§!数据晶体宣称此处存在丰富的资源，[baol_colony_1.GetName]只是一颗干旱、荒凉的星球，甚至没有大气层。\n\n幸运的是，地质扫描表明在行星深处的沉积层中存在几乎取之不尽的化石状生物遗体！遗体中最有可能是§Y巴奥§!的是一种植物类物种，因为只有该物种具备开智生物所需要的神经结构。\n\n一次定向发掘应当可以揭示更多信息。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:266`

### English

Despite the promise of riches suggested in the ancient §YGrunur§! datacrystals recovered from [first_grunur_site.GetName], [baol_colony_1.GetName] is a dry, barren world, void of atmosphere.\n\nFortunately, geological scans have revealed a near-endless trove of fossilized remains deep in the planet's sedimentary record! Among them is a plantoid species most likely to have been the §YBaol§!, as it is the only one identified in possession of the neurological structures required for sapience.\n\nA targeted excavation should uncover more information.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:285`

## ancrel.2004.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.2004.name&lang=en)

### 中文

军事级种族灭绝

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:267`

### English

Military Mass-Extinction

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:286`

## ancrel.2004.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.2004.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

地质编录中显示的灰烬厚层和引人注目的大规模灭绝事件与先前从§Y古茹努尔§!数据晶体中提取到的材料相吻合，表明是§Y古茹努尔§!在大约七百万年前使用胶凝燃烧弹轰炸了[From.Planet.GetName]。\n\n在行星地表肆虐的森林大火扰乱了当地的生态系统，由于缺少能进行光合作用的植物，行星的大气层在接下来六万年间被逐渐消耗殆尽。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:268`

### English

Thick layers of ash and a notable mass-extinction event in the geological record coincide with material previously extracted from §YGrunur§! datacrystals, suggesting the §YGrunur§! firebombed [From.Planet.GetName] with a gelling incendiary roughly seven million years ago.\n\nWidespread deforestation of the planet's surface disrupted local ecosystems, and the lack of oxygen-fixing vegetation gradually depleted the planet's atmosphere over the next sixty-thousand years.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:287`

## ancrel.2005.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.2005.name&lang=en)

### 中文

巴奥有机体

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:269`

### English

The Baol Organism

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:288`

## ancrel.2005.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.2005.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们找到了数具保存完好的§Y巴奥§!化石，其揭示出这一早已消失的物种具有蜂巢思维：数十亿植物体单位同心协力一体合作，如同一棵树上的枝、叶和根。树木年轮学分析表明个体§Y巴奥§!的寿命可达数百年。\n\n[From.Planet.GetName]上缺少残留的建筑物似乎是由巴奥对其高度发达的有机科技的依赖造成的。时光流逝让他们的舰船、建筑和湿体电脑消失殆尽。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:270`

### English

Several exemplary §YBaol§! fossils have been recovered, revealing the long-lost species to have been a Hive Mind: billions of plantoid units working together as a whole, like the leaves and roots and branches of a tree. Dendrochronology suggests individual §YBaol§! specimens had lifespans in the hundreds of years.\n\nThe lack of structural ruins on [From.Planet.GetName] appears to be the result of the Baol's reliance on remarkable organic technology, from ships and buildings to wetware biocomputers, and the ravages of time.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:289`

## ancrel.2006.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.2006.name&lang=en)

### 中文

古茹努尔数据晶体

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:271`

### English

A Grunur Datacrystal

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:290`

## ancrel.2006.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.2006.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们从一块破碎的§Y古茹努尔§!数据晶体中提取到了另一个前§Y巴奥§!殖民地的坐标！也许这颗行星上会有巴奥古老的敌人们所追寻的财富。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:272`

### English

Coordinates describing the location of another former §YBaol§! colony have been extracted from a shattered §YGrunur§! datacrystal! Perhaps this planet will boast some of the riches sought after by the Baol's ancient enemies.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:291`

## ancrel.2006.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.2006.a&lang=en)

### 中文

去下一个殖民地！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:273`

### English

To the next colony!

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:292`

## ancrel.2007.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.2007.name&lang=en)

### 中文

前巴奥殖民地：破碎行星

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:274`

### English

Former Baol Colony: The Shattered

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:293`

## ancrel.2007.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.2007.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]的船员最初确信他们在进行三角定位以推算[Root.GetStarName]恒星系中的§Y巴奥§!殖民地的理论位置时出了差错，因为在原本该有足以支持生命存在的行星的位置上除了若干小行星之外什么都没有。\n\n但是，对这些小行星成分更进一步的研究表明它们事实上是前§Y巴奥§!殖民地的残骸！大约是在七百万年前，曾经草木葱茏的行星被未知撞击炸成了数千块碎片。可以推断§Y古茹努尔§!在这颗行星的悲惨结局中起到了某种作用。\n\n一颗最有发掘价值的小行星已被选定为接下来研究的对象，在其上保留有消失行星上大量生物的化石化残骸。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:275`

### English

The crew of the [Root.GetName] were initially certain they had made a triangulation mistake when deducing the coordinates of the supposed §YBaol§! colony in the [Root.GetStarName] system, having found nothing but asteroids where they expected a life-bearing planet.\n\nHowever, closer study of the asteroids' composition has revealed that they are in fact what remains of the former §YBaol§! colony! The once-lush planet was blown into several thousand rocky pieces by some unknown collision, occurring roughly seven million years ago. Deduction suggests the §YGrunur§! played some role in the planet's dark fate.\n\nOne particularly promising asteroid has been selected for further study, as it appears to possess a large number of fossilized lifeforms from the lost planet.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:294`

## ancrel.2007.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.2007.a&lang=en)

### 中文

真可惜。也许我们会从中学到些什么。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:276`

### English

A pity. Perhaps we will learn more.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:295`

## ancrel.2008.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.2008.name&lang=en)

### 中文

生物计算机化石

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:277`

### English

Biocomputer Fossil

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:296`

## ancrel.2008.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.2008.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们设法从一块保存状况良好的§Y巴奥§!生物计算机化石中提取出了一些数据片段！这一千万年前的古老遗物看起来存贮着教育学相关的信息，供该物种的未成年个体使用。由于尚未准备好与蜂巢思维进行完整的神经链接，幼年§Y巴奥§!，或者说幼树，会被以更为精心设计的方式教授育苗技术、园艺学与营养学。\n\n这些发现表明§Y巴奥§!社会十分重视陆地景观美化和生态环境管理，其自身的生存也高度依赖这些技术。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:278`

### English

We have managed to extract some data fragments from a particularly well-preserved §YBaol§! biocomputer fossil! This ten million year old relic appears to contain pedagogical information, intended for immature members of the species. Not yet ready to receive the full range of the Hive Mind's neural connection, young §YBaol§!, or saplings, were introduced to growth techniques, horticulture, and nutrition in a more curated experience.\n\nThese findings suggest §YBaol§! society prized terrestrial landscaping and ecosystem management, and were also highly dependent on it for their survival.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:297`

## ancrel.2009.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.2009.name&lang=en)

### 中文

生物计算机残片

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:279`

### English

Biocomputer Remnant

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:298`

## ancrel.2009.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.2009.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

发现另一台§Y巴奥§!生物计算机的残片让我们对这一植物类蜂巢思维物种的历史有了更深入的了解。\n\n§Y巴奥§!看起来是纯粹的植物生命体，发源于位置未知的单一行星。史前§Y巴奥§!全都是其亲代植物的克隆体，在其母星上缓慢而平和地生长了数百万年，并逐步发展出了承载蜂巢意识的能力。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:280`

### English

The discovery of another ancient §YBaol§! biocomputer remnant has yielded further insight into this plantoid Hive Mind's history.\n\nThe §YBaol§! appear to have originated from a single planet, its location unknown, as mere plant life. Prehistoric §YBaol§! were all clones of their parent plants, and gradually developed the capacity for Hive consciousness over millions of years of slow, peaceful growth on their home planet.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:299`

## ancrel.2010.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.2010.name&lang=en)

### 中文

古茹努尔数据晶体

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:281`

### English

A Grunur Datacrystal

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:300`

## ancrel.2010.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.2010.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在[From.Planet.GetName]上找到了一块§Y古茹努尔§!数据晶体，大约是在七百万年前遗留在此。这块数据晶体存储了部分尚可读取的军事记录，其内容与入侵这颗行星有关。\n\n显然，§Y古茹努尔§!预料到会有某种形式的武装抵抗，移动极为缓慢的§Y巴奥§!并没有给他们留下深刻的印象。记录中以高度贬损的语气描述了规避§Y巴奥§!迟钝的火力回击有多么轻松。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:282`

### English

A §YGrunur§! datacrystal has been recovered on [From.Planet.GetName], dating to some seven million years ago. The datacrystal contains partially readable military records, pertaining to the planet's invasion.\n\nApparently, the §YGrunur§! were expecting some measure of armed resistance, and were not impressed by the incredibly slow-moving §YBaol§!. The record describes the ease with which the §YBaol§!'s delayed responses were circumvented, in a highly derogatory fashion.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:301`

## ancrel.2011.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.2011.name&lang=en)

### 中文

古茹努尔作战命令

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:283`

### English

Grunur Battle Orders

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:302`

## ancrel.2011.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.2011.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

另一块§Y古茹努尔§!数据晶体被发掘出土了，其内详细记述了一系列军事命令——包括另一处前§Y巴奥§!殖民地的坐标。\n\n我们的考古学家十分渴望弄清这两个相互敌对的国家的历史，并正在将数据上传。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:284`

### English

Another §YGrunur§! datacrystal has been unearthed, detailing a series of military orders -- including the coordinates of another former §YBaol§! colony.\n\nOur archaeologists, eager to further unravel the history of these two foes, are uploading the data now.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:303`

## ancrel.2011.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.2011.a&lang=en)

第三次会有好运气了吧……？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:285`

### English

Third time's the charm...?

## ancrel.2012.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.2012.name&lang=en)

### 中文

前巴奥殖民地：寂静无声

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:286`

### English

Former Baol Colony: The Silenced

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:305`

## ancrel.2012.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.2012.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的研究人员兴奋地发现第三个§Y巴奥§!殖民地的位置与一颗仍拥有大气层和生命体的行星相吻合！\n\n尽管[From.From.GetName]看起来并非特别草木葱茏或资源丰富，我们的科学家们还是急切地想要开展一次定向发掘，以寻找§Y巴奥§!或§Y古茹努尔§!留下的任何残余物。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:287`

### English

Our researchers were excited to discover the location of the third §YBaol§! colony corresponds to a planet still possessing an atmosphere and life!\n\nWhile [From.From.GetName] does not seem particularly lush or remarkably resource-laden, our scientists are eager to begin a targeted excavation to search for any remnants of the §YBaol§!, or the §YGrunur§!.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:306`

## ancrel.2013.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.2013.name&lang=en)

### 中文

机能退化

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:288`

### English

Diminished Capacities

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:307`

## ancrel.2013.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.2013.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在[From.Planet.GetName]的发掘工作中，我们找到了大量与§Y巴奥§!明确相关的化石。不过，令人好奇的是，这些样本用于亚空间假突触通讯——一种蜂巢思维的专有天赋，可以在遥远的距离上进行神经通讯——的神经器官似乎已经退化。\n\n尽管假定§Y古茹努尔§!入侵与此无关是不负责任的，造成这种摇摆演化的明确原因仍然未知。相当有必要进行进一步的调查。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:289`

### English

Excavation at [From.Planet.GetName] has revealed a number of fossils clearly related to the §YBaol§! -- but, curiously, these specimens appear to have diminished neurological organs required for subspace ephapse communication -- that is to say, the ability to communicate neurologically over great distances, a faculty unique to Hive Minds.\n\nWhat definitive causes may have precipitated this evolutionary swing are unknown, though it would be remiss to assume to the §YGrunur§! invasion was unrelated. Further investigation is warranted.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:308`

## ancrel.2024.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.2024.name&lang=en)

### 中文

蜂巢解体

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:290`

### English

Devolution

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:309`

## ancrel.2024.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.2024.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

更新的§Y巴奥§!化石从地质记录中被挖掘出来了，这些标本已不再具有退化的蜂巢神经系统。反之，亚空间假突触交流所需的结构完全消失了！\n\n样本的碳年代测定已经证实，在§Y古茹努尔§!入侵后的几百年里，§Y巴奥§!开始放弃作为蜂巢思维彼此连接的能力。\n\n不幸的是，在这个遗迹中似乎无法取得更多相关数据了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:291`

### English

Younger §YBaol§! fossil specimens have been uncovered from the geological record, though these no longer have diminished Hive neurologies. Instead, the structures required for subspace ephapse communication are entirely missing!\n\nCarbon-dating of the specimens has confirmed that some hundreds of years following the §YGrunur§! invasion, the §YBaol§! began to devolve their ability to connect to one another as a Hive Mind.\n\nUnfortunately, it seems no further relevant data can be extracted from this site.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:310`

## ancrel.2015.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.2015.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的科学家正在接收一个包含§Y古茹努尔§!数字签名的求救信号！它似乎来源于一个迄今未被我们发现的恒星系，在[last_baol_neighbor.GetName]附近。\n\n它可能包含着什么样的古老信息？\n\n我们的科学家想要调查信号的来源地。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:294`

### English

Our scientists are picking up a distress signal bearing §YGrunur§! datalogical signatures! It appears to originate from a system previously unknown to us, near [last_baol_neighbor.GetName].\n\nWhat kind of ancient message could it possibly contain?\n\nOur scientists would like to investigate the signal's point of origin.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:313`

来自§H[From.GetName]§!的科学官已经定位了最后的§H巴奥§!样本！

### English

Scientists from the §H[From.GetName]§! have located the last §HBaol§! specimen!

巴奥有机体

### English

The Baol Organism

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:317`

## ancrel.2016.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.2016.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]船员跟踪到了§Y古茹努尔§!求救信号源并发现了摇摇欲坠的§Y古茹努尔§!研究设施，其处于一个废弃已久的行星的轨道。该设施即将失去能源供应，其上工作了数百万年的大型太阳能电池板网络和逐渐腐朽的自动维修系统很快就要达到使用寿命的极限了。\n\n我们对设施进行了扫描并震惊地发现这里有一个生命体存在。船员派出了现场调查队伍，他们已经准备好进行报告。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:299`

### English

The [Root.GetName] crew have traced the §YGrunur§! distress signal to its source, uncovering a crumbling §YGrunur§! research facility, hanging in orbit of an otherwise long-abandoned planet. The facility is reaching critical energy failure, having survived for millions of years on an extensive network of solar panels and decaying auto-repair systems, all of which are now approaching terminal status.\n\nShockingly enough, scans of the facility are revealing the presence of a single lifeform. The crew have dispatched an away team, and are ready to report.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:318`

## ancrel.2017.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.2017.name&lang=en)

### 中文

最后的巴奥

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:300`

### English

The Last Baol

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:319`

## ancrel.2017.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.2017.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§H在残破的古茹努尔旧研究设施残骸深处，在长久废弃的电脑面板和破损的研究设备之间，我们的船员有了难以置信的发现。\n\n靠着营养管和损坏严重的生命支持系统的维持，该物种的孑遗个体静静地躺在这里：最后的巴奥，奇迹般地依旧存活，被一排从破损的罐子中流淌出的已然高度腐烂的其他个体的尸体所环绕。\n\n由于我们的考古学家之前对该物种有过大量的研究，我们得以和这一物种最后的个体进行交流。§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:301`

### English

§HDeep in the dilapidated ruins of the timeworn Grunur research facility, among the long-defunct computer panels and deteriorated research equipment, our ship's crew has made an incredible find.\n\nPincushioned by nutrient tubes and critically failing life support systems, lies one single organism: the last Baol, miraculously still alive, surrounded by the sludgy remains of other specimens in a row of cracked and leaking vats.\n\nOwing to our extensive previous archaeological studies on the species, we should be able to attempt communications with this last specimen.§!

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:320`

## ancrel.2018.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.2018.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

“一个访客？怎么可能？”\n\n§H巴奥在他的罐子里瑟瑟发抖。§!\n\n“尽管你的到来带来了警报声，但是你并不是把我们置于此地的人。你最终能为我们带来安宁吗？”

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:306`

### English

"A visitor? Can it be?"\n\n§HThe Baol shudders in its vat.§!\n\n"But you are not the ones who put us here, though you come as the alarms sound. Have you come to give us peace, at last?"

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:325`

## ancrel.2018.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.2018.a&lang=en)

可能吧。但是首先，我们有几个问题。

### English

Perhaps. But first, we have a few questions.

You will answer to us, xeno. Not the reverse.

## ancrel.2019.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.2019.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§H巴奥沉默了数分钟。§!“那么问吧。我们已经等了数百万年。我们还能够再忍受一会儿。”

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:310`

### English

§HThe Baol is silent for several minutes.§! "Then ask. We have waited millennia. We can endure a moment more."

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:329`

## ancrel.2019.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.2019.a&lang=en)

### 中文

和我们说说你自己。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:311`

### English

Tell us about yourself.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:330`

## ancrel.2019.a.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.2019.a.response&lang=en)

### 中文

§H过了相当长一段时间，巴奥有了回应。§!\n\n“我们自己？我们……我。是的。还能说什么呢？我们在柔软的土地上沐浴着星辰的光芒，迎风舒展着枝叶，如同迎着太阳的风帆。我们记得灼热的炎夏中滴落的每一粒水滴，也记得颤栗的寒冬中覆落的每一片霜雪。当群星在头顶不断旋转时，我们会迎着着微风摇摆着身躯。\n\n后来，我们被连根拔起，被焚为灰烬，被大卸八块。我们被监禁在这里，互相看着彼此，腐烂在玻璃的监狱里。”

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:312`

### English

§HLong minutes pass before the Baol responds.§!\n\n"Ourself? Our...self. Yes. What is there to tell? We sat beneath the stars on soft earth, unfurled our leaves like sails to the sun overhead. We remember every drop of water that quenched them in the parched summers. Every shivering hoar that blanketed them in winters. We bent in the wind as the stars wheeled overhead.\n\nWe were uprooted. We were burned. We were sliced. We were left here. We watched our others rot in their glass prisons.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:331`

## ancrel.2019.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.2019.b&lang=en)

### 中文

巴奥身上发生了什么？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:313`

### English

What happened to the Baol?

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:332`

## ancrel.2019.b.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.2019.b.response&lang=en)

### 中文

§H再一次，巴奥在回答前陷入了长时间的沉默。§!“当焚烧者到来的时候，我们意识到危险之前整个殖民地就陷入了沉寂。如同半身不遂一般，各处突触被迅速切断，从根本上断开连接。然后我们失去了下一个，下一个，再下一个。一个又一个，他们的声音离开了合唱。我们失去了许多个体。他们的沉默使剩下的我们陷入无尽的沮丧。\n\n你能理解吗，来访者？我们承受了无上的痛苦。焚烧者能迅速移动，就像你们一样，在我们能够撤离之前他们就使我们都陷于了火海中，尽数化为灰烬。整个空气中充斥着灰色的痛苦，那是我们逝去同伴的骨灰。”

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:314`

### English

§HAgain, the Baol is long silent before managing a reply.§! "When the burners came, entire colonies were silenced before the danger was known to us. Like a limb gone limp, nerves cut suddenly and totally at the base. And then we lost the next, and the next, and the next. One by one, their voices left the chorus. We lost our others. Their silence deafened us who remained.\n\nCan you understand, visitor? We could not bear to hear more agony. The burners were swift, like you, swifter, and before we could withdraw, they had set us all aflame. All burnt to ash, and the air filled with that gray agony: the dust of our lost."

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:333`

## ancrel.2021.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.2021.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§H巴奥全身一阵颤抖，让人感觉它似乎正在狂笑。§!“不用了。我们已经在这种扭曲的状态下生活很久了。现在我们只求一死。不过首先……你可以告诉我们吗？我们被封闭在这里太久，除了这个房间什么也见不到，就这样看着污浊的空气一点一点将空间站腐蚀到只剩下金属骨架。\n\n告诉我们。我们的族群……还存在吗？”

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:319`

### English

§HA sort of shiver runs through Baol. You have the distinct impression it is laughing.§! "No. We have lived too long in this wretched state. Now we long only for an end. Though first... Would you tell us? We have been sealed here for so long, blind to all but this room, watching the stale air slowly wear the station down to its metal bones.\n\nTell us. Does our kind... yet live?"

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:338`

## ancrel.2021.desc.phobe

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.2021.desc.phobe&lang=en)

### 中文

“等等。”§H巴奥颤抖道。§!“在一切结束之前。我们的亲族，求求你们。我们被封闭在这里太久，除了这个房间什么也见不到，就这样看着污浊的空气一点一点将空间站腐蚀到只剩下金属骨架。\n\n告诉我们。我们的族群……还存在吗？”

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:320`

### English

"Wait." §HThe Baol trembles.§! "Before it all ends. Our kin. Please. We have been sealed here for so long, blind to all but this room, watching the stale air slowly wear the station down to its metal bones.\n\nTell us. Does our kind... yet live?"

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:339`
