## ancrel.2022.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.2022.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§H慢慢地，几乎不知不觉地，最后的巴奥的枝叶自行枯萎了，只剩下一点点。§!“或许……或许这才是最好的。”\n\n§H将神秘液体泵入泵出这个生物体的机器开始嘶嘶作响。很明显这是这台机器的垂死挣扎，而巴奥在离开机器后也将死去。§!\n\n“沉默，是的。最终它到来了。我们欢迎沉默，就像我们的弟兄很久之前做的那样。永别了，[Root.GetSpeciesName]。愿你的至亲在这个世界承受较少的痛苦。”

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:325`

### English

§HSlowly, almost imperceptibly, the leaves of the last Baol shrivel in on themselves, just a little more.§! "Perhaps... perhaps that is best."\n\n§HThe machine pumping mysterious fluids in and out of the creature's body begin to sputter and hiss. It is clearly on its last mechanical gasp, and the Baol will perish without it.§!\n\n"Silence, yes. At last it approaches. We welcome it, as our brethren did so long ago. Farewell, [Root.GetSpeciesName]. May your kin endure less agony in this world."

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:344`

永别了。

## ancrel.2023.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.2023.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§H你的科学家们找到了将营养物质输入、代谢废物输出该生物体的管道并逐一将其拔出，加速机器的损坏过程，以便加快最后的巴奥的死亡。\n\n当你工作时，巴奥似乎没有做出任何抵抗，除非它那萎缩肢体的轻微摆动是想要表示些什么。\n\n最后，工作完成了，伴随着最后一次波及全身的震颤，该生物断了气。§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:328`

### English

§HReaching for the tubes that ferry mysterious nutrients in and out of the creature's form, your scientists begin to jerk them loose, expediting the machinery's failure and thereby the last Baol's death.\n\nThe Baol seems to make no protest as you work, unless the slight tremor running through its withered limbs is meant to signify something.\n\nFinally, the work is complete, and one final tremor runs through the creature before it expires.§!

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:347`

## ancrel.2025.desc.first

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.2025.desc.first&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的[Owner.GetTerraformerPlural]满意地报告称，我们对[Root.GetName]的环境改造已经完成！通过从最后的§Y巴奥§!的身体里提取基因并仔细选取出§Y巴奥§!遗传密码的片段这一方法，我们成功在实验室中制造出了杂种植物并在部署这些植物仅仅五年后就成功完成了对[Root.GetName]气候和大气层的环境改造。\n\n与预期一样，这些杂种植物在行星上到处增殖，目前正进入“潜伏”期，因为[Root.GetName]大气层化学成分的改变使它们不再活跃。\n\n令人难以置信的是，行星管理部门报告称在这一过程里产生了意料之外的结果：[Root.GetName]上出现了许多智慧外星生命！它们与古代的§Y巴奥§!高度相似，但其拥有广泛混合的此行星自然植被的遗传物质。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:333`

### English

Our [Owner.GetTerraformerPlural] are pleased to report that the terraforming process on [Root.GetName] has completed! The deployment of new laboratory-created plant hybrids, made using carefully selected segments of the §YBaol§! genetic code as harvested from the body of the last §YBaol§!, successfully transformed [Root.GetName]'s climate and atmosphere over a mere five-year period.\n\nThe hybrid plants multiplied across the planet as expected, and are now entering a 'dormant' phase, as the chemical atmospheric changes on [Root.GetName] have now deactivated them.\n\n Incredibly, our planetside administration reports an unexpected outcome of the process: a number of sapient aliens, incredibly similar to the ancient §YBaol§!, but possessing a vast mixture of genetic material from the planet's natural flora, have appeared on [Root.GetName]!

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:352`

## ancrel.2025.desc.firstregular

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.2025.desc.firstregular&lang=en)

### 中文

$ancrel.2025.desc.first$\n\n尽管不是蜂巢思维的一分子，这些§G新巴奥§!外星人似乎同它们的祖先一样平和。无论如何，我们必须妥善解决它们在[Root.GetName]上存在一事。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:334`

### English

$ancrel.2025.desc.first$\n\nWhile not members of a Hive Mind, these §GNu-Baol§! aliens seem as peaceful as their ancestors. One way or another, we shall have to address their presence on [Root.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:353`

## ancrel.2025.desc.firsthive

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.2025.desc.firsthive&lang=en)

$ancrel.2025.desc.first$\n\n尽管起初属于另一个蜂巢思维，这些§G新巴奥§!外星人相当容易就能整合进我们的意识之中。无论如何，我们必须妥善解决它们在[Root.GetName]上存在一事。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:335`

### English

$ancrel.2025.desc.first$\n\nAlthough initially part of a different Hive Mind these §GNu-Baol§! aliens could easily be integrated into our consciousness. One way or another, we shall have to address their presence on [Root.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:354`

## ancrel.2025.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.2025.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的[Owner.GetTerraformerPlural]满意地报告称，我们对[Root.GetName]的环境改造已经完成！此行星已被环境改造为§G$pc_gaia$§!，这要归功于我们在实验室中制造出的杂交植物的部署，其包含精心选择的§Y巴奥§!遗传密码的片段。\n\n又一次——作为这一尚未被我们的基因学家完全理解的过程的结果——许多§G新巴奥§!人口在环境改造即将完成时于行星上大量出现。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:336`

### English

Our [Owner.GetTerraformerPlural] are pleased to report that the terraforming process on [Root.GetName] has completed! The planet has been successfully transformed into a §G$pc_gaia$§!, owing to our deployment of laboratory-created plant hybrids, containing carefully selected segments of the §YBaol§! genetic code.\n\nOnce again, as a result of the process - itself not quite fully understood by our geneticists - a number of §GNu-Baol§! populations have appeared planetside, emerging when terraforming neared completion.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:355`

## ancrel.2026.desc.first

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.2026.desc.first&lang=en)

### 中文

在[Root.GetName]上引入§Y巴奥§!幼苗之后产生了惊人的效果。接触幼苗孢子后，我们的一部分人口发生了自主适应，他们在[Root.GetPlanetMoon]上比预期中更加兴旺了。但对他们的生理学分析表明他们如今已无法再适应盖亚以外的其他生态系统了。\n\n令人难以置信的是，行星管理部门报告称这一过程产生了意料之外的结果：[Root.GetName]上出现了许多智慧外星生命！它们与古代§Y巴奥§!高度相似，但其拥有广泛混合的此行星自然植被的遗传物质。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:342`

### English

Introducing the §YBaol§! seedlings to [Root.GetName] had a most curious effect. After being exposed to the seedling spores, parts of our population now seem to have adapted themselves to thrive even more than expected on the [Root.GetPlanetMoon]. Yet analysis of their biology now tells us that they will not be able to survive outside of a Gaia ecosystem.\n\nIncredibly, our planetside administration reports an unexpected outcome of the process: a number of sapient aliens, remarkably similar to the ancient §YBaol§!, but possessing a vast mixture of genetic material from the planet's natural flora, have appeared on [Root.GetName]!

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:362`

## ancrel.2026.desc.firstregular

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.2026.desc.firstregular&lang=en)

### 中文

$ancrel.2026.desc.first$\n\n尽管不是蜂巢思维的一分子，这些§G新巴奥§!外星人似乎同它们的祖先一样平和。无论如何，我们必须妥善解决它们在[Root.GetName]上存在一事。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:343`

### English

$ancrel.2026.desc.first$\n\nWhile not members of a Hive Mind, these §GNu-Baol§! aliens seem as peaceful as their ancestors. One way or another, we shall have to address their presence on [Root.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:363`

## ancrel.2026.desc.firsthive

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.2026.desc.firsthive&lang=en)

### 中文

$ancrel.2026.desc.first$\n\n尽管起初属于另一个蜂巢思维，这些§G新巴奥§!外星人相当容易就能整合进我们的意识之中。无论如何，我们必须妥善解决它们在[Root.GetName]上存在一事。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:344`

### English

$ancrel.2026.desc.first$\n\nAlthough initially part of a different Hive Mind, these §GNu-Baol§! aliens could easily be integrated into our consciousness. One way or another, we shall have to address their presence on [Root.GetName]

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:364`

## ancrel.2026.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.2026.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的[Owner.GetTerraformerPlural]满意地报告称，我们在将§Y巴奥§!引种到[Root.GetName]的工作已经完成！在接触到分苗孢子之后，我们的一部分人口似乎发生了自适应，在这颗[Root.GetPlanetMoon]上生存得比预期要更好了。\n\n得益于此，在该流程即将完成时，行星上再次出现了大量§G新巴奥§!人口，尽管我们的基因学家尚未完全理解环境改造的过程。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:345`

### English

Our [Owner.GetTerraformerPlural] are pleased to report that the introduction of the §YBaol§! seedlings to [Root.GetName] has been completed! After being exposed to the seedling spores, parts of our population now seem to have adapted themselves to thrive even more than expected on the [Root.GetPlanetMoon].\n\nOnce again, as a result of the process, itself not quite fully understood to our geneticists, a number of §GNu-Baol§! populations have appeared planetside, emerging when the process neared completion.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:365`

## ancrel.2027.desc.first

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.2027.desc.first&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的[Owner.GetTerraformerPlural]满意地报告称，对[Root.GetName]的环境改造已经完成！利用从最后的§Y巴奥§!躯体里回收的精挑细选的§Y巴奥§!基因片段，我们成功在实验室中制造出了杂种植物并在部署这些植物仅仅五年后就成功完成了对[Root.GetName]气候和大气层的环境改造。\n\n与预期一样，这些杂种植物在行星上到处增殖，目前正进入一种“休眠”期，因为[Root.GetName]大气层化学成分的改变使它们不再活跃。\n\n令人难以置信的是，管理部门报告称在这一过程里产生了意料之外的结果：[Root.GetName]上出现了许多智慧外星生命！它们与古代的§Y巴奥§!高度相似，但其拥有广泛混合的此行星自然植被的遗传物质。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:347`

### English

Our [Owner.GetTerraformerPlural] are pleased to report that the terraforming process on [Root.GetName] has completed! The deployment of new laboratory-created plant hybrids, made using carefully selected segments of the §YBaol§! genetic code as harvested from the body of the last §YBaol§!, successfully transformed [Root.GetName]'s over a mere five-year period.\n\nThe hybrid plants multiplied across the structure as expected, and are now entering a 'dormant' phase, as the chemical atmospheric changes on [Root.GetName] have now deactivated them.\n\n Incredibly, our administration reports an unexpected outcome of the process: a number of sapient aliens, incredibly similar to the ancient §YBaol§!, but possessing a vast mixture of genetic material from our own vegetal resources, have appeared on [Root.GetName]!

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:368`

$ancrel.2027.desc.first$\n\n尽管不是蜂巢思维的一分子，这些§G新巴奥§!外星人似乎同它们的祖先一样平和。无论如何，我们必须妥善解决它们在[Root.GetName]上存在一事。

### English

$ancrel.2027.desc.first$\n\nWhile not members of a Hive Mind, these §GNu-Baol§! aliens seem as peaceful as their ancestors. One way or another, we shall have to address their presence on [Root.GetName].

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:349`

### English

$ancrel.2027.desc.first$\n\nAlthough initially part of a different Hive Mind these §GNu-Baol§! aliens could easily be integrated into our consciousness. One way or another, we shall have to address their presence on [Root.GetName].

## ancrel.2027.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.2027.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的[Owner.GetTerraformerPlural]满意地报告称，我们对[Root.GetName]的环境改造已经完成！这一体系的生命支持系统已升级完成，这要归功于我们部署了实验室制造出的杂交植物，其中包含精心挑选的§Y巴奥§!遗传密码的片段。\n\n又一次，这一尚未被我们的基因学家所理解的过程又导致了一定数量的§G巴奥§!人口出现。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:350`

### English

Our [Owner.GetTerraformerPlural] are pleased to report that the terraforming process on [Root.GetName] has completed! The structure's life support has been successfully upgraded, owing to our deployment of laboratory-created plant hybrids, containing carefully selected segments of the §YBaol§! genetic code.\n\nOnce again, as a result of the process - itself not quite fully understood by our geneticists - a number of §GNu-Baol§! populations have appeared.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:371`

## grunur_digsite_1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Agrunur_digsite_1&lang=en)

### 中文

古茹努尔遗迹

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:355`

### English

Grunur Ruins

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:377`

## grunur_digsite_1_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Agrunur_digsite_1_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这些遗迹被认为是古代行政建筑群，它们属于一个自称为§Y古茹努尔§!的太空物种，该物种是在[From.Planet.GetName]上被发现的。\n\n令人震惊的是，这个失落的文明似乎在七百万年前就居住在这颗星球上了，我们的考古学家渴望揭开他们的秘密。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:356`

### English

The remains of what is believed to be an ancient administrative complex, belonging to a space-faring species who called themselves the §YGrunur§! has been found on [From.Planet.GetName].\n\nThis fallen civilization appears to have inhabited the planet a staggering seven million years ago, and our archaeologists are eager to uncover their secrets.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:378`

## baol_digsite_1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Abaol_digsite_1&lang=en)

### 中文

不毛之地

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:357`

### English

The Barren

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:379`

## baol_digsite_1_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Abaol_digsite_1_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在[From.Planet.GetName]沉积层的深处，我们发现了一处含有许多植物类物种化石的极有价值的宝库，该物种具有的神经结构足以支撑智能的存在。\n\n如果这些早已死去的生物的确是§Y巴奥§!，研究他们的尸体可能会我们提供更多线索，以便弄清他们那一度草木葱茏的行星是如何变成了一个没有空气和生命的星球。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:358`

### English

Deep in the geological record of [From.Planet.GetName], a trove of promising fossils belonging to a plantoid species possessing the neurological structures necessary for sapience have been uncovered.\n\nIf these long-dead creatures were indeed the §YBaol§!, studying their remains may yield insights into what transformed their once-lush planet into an airless, barren world.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:380`

## baol_digsite_2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Abaol_digsite_2&lang=en)

### 中文

破碎行星

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:359`

### English

The Shattered

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:381`

## baol_digsite_2_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Abaol_digsite_2_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

虽然只是前§Y巴奥§!殖民地留下的可悲残片，这一小行星仍有丰富的化石贮藏亟待发掘。我们的考古学家希望能对神秘的§Y巴奥§!的历史和§Y古茹努尔§!在此行星黑暗命运中扮演的可疑角色上有更多的发现。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:360`

### English

The tragic remains of a former §YBaol§! colony, this asteroid nevertheless contains a rich trove of fossils begging for excavation. Our archaeologists hope to discover more about the history of the mysterious §YBaol§!, and the §YGrunur§!'s suspected role in the planet's dark fate.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:382`

## baol_digsite_3

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Abaol_digsite_3&lang=en)

### 中文

寂静之地

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:361`

### English

The Silenced

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:383`

## baol_digsite_3_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Abaol_digsite_3_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这一前§Y巴奥§!殖民地上仍然拥有大气层和低等生命，我们已在此建立了一个发掘点，以期能对这一文明最终的历史有更多的了解。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:362`

### English

Still in possession of an atmosphere and lower forms of life, a dig site has been established on this former §YBaol§! colony, in the hopes of shedding more light on the ultimate history of the civilization.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:384`

## city_of_bones

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acity_of_bones&lang=en)

### 中文

白骨之城

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:363`

### English

City of Bones

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:385`

## city_of_bones_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acity_of_bones_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一个完全由不明骨头建造的原始城市废墟在经历这颗星球上的一场强力沙尘暴之后被发现。\n\n我们的科学家渴望发掘这座可怕的古城。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:364`

### English

The ruins of a primeval city built entirely out of bones of an unknown origin have been unearthed following a violent sandstorm on this planet.\n\nOur archaeologists are eager to begin excavation on this grisly ancient city.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:386`

## city_of_bones_desc_uncol

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acity_of_bones_desc_uncol&lang=en)

### 中文

这颗星球上发现了一个完全由不明骨头建造的原始城市废墟。\n\n我们的科学家渴望发掘这座可怕的废墟。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:365`

### English

The ruins of a primeval city built entirely out of bones of an unknown origin have been found on this planet.\n\nOur archaeologists are eager to begin excavation on these grisly ruins.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:387`

## mutation_vats

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amutation_vats&lang=en)

### 中文

异变化石

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:366`

### English

Mutant Fossils

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:388`

## mutation_vats_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amutation_vats_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在这颗星球上发现了有着令人惊异数量的严重异变化石，它们都属于同一种已灭绝的智慧物种。\n\n究竟是什么导致了一个高等社会数百万成员的异变？我们的考古学家想进一步调查。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:367`

### English

A surprising number of heavily-mutated fossils has been found on this planet, all belonging to the same species of extinct sapients.\n\nWhat could have led to the mutation of millions of members of a once-advanced society? Our archaeologists would like to investigate further.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:389`

## planetary_mechanocalibrator

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aplanetary_mechanocalibrator&lang=en)

### 中文

行星机械

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:368`

### English

Planetary Machinery

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:390`

## planetary_mechanocalibrator_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aplanetary_mechanocalibrator_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一台锈迹斑斑，覆膜一样的机器覆盖着[From.Planet.GetName]表面并深入到行星的内核。为了搞清这台机器的奥秘，我们的考古学家们需要建立一座巨大的发掘站点。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:369`

### English

The rusting, film-covered machinery covering [From.Planet.GetName] runs deep into the planet's core. In order to unravel more of this mechanical mystery, our archaeologists will need to establish an extensive dig site.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:391`

## the_host

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Athe_host&lang=en)

### 中文

地下洞穴

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:370`

### English

Subterranean Hollows

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:392`

## the_host_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Athe_host_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在这颗星球的地壳内，数量惊人的地下通道系统交错盘旋。究竟是什么造就了这一切呢？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:371`

### English

An extensive network of subterranean tunnels of truly astounding proportion make swirls in the crust of this planet. What could have created them?

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:393`

## ancrel.2041.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.2041.name&lang=en)

### 中文

$city_of_bones$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:372`

### English

$city_of_bones$

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:395`

## ancrel.2041.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.2041.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

部分久藏于[From.Planet.GetName]沙漠之下森然的白骨之城已经被[Root.GetName]团队仔细的发掘出来。\n\n这个城市似乎是由数百万年之前原始外星社会在大陆上的沙漠中建造。在这片无水沙漠中缺乏建筑材料可能是这个失落城市使用骨头建造的原因……尽管我们还不清楚这些白骨来自什么生物。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:373`

### English

Parts of the ghastly city of bones long-buried beneath the sands on [From.Planet.GetName] have been carefully excavated by the [Root.GetName] team.\n\nThe city appears to have been built by a primitive alien society millions of years ago, on a continental stretch of desert. The lack of other building materials in a waterless desert likely contributed to this long-lost society's use of bones in construction... though what manner of creature the bones came from remains unknown.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:396`

## ancrel.2042.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.2042.name&lang=en)

### 中文

$city_of_bones$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:374`

### English

$city_of_bones$

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:397`

## ancrel.2042.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.2042.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一场针对性的挖掘既没有发现任何一具这个用来建造这座城市的生物完整的骨骼，也没有在城外发现任何相似化石的案例。关于在[From.Planet.GetName]上失落文明到底是从哪里获得了建造这座令人毛骨悚然城市的骨头仍然是个问题。\n\n我们的研究人员已经假定这些骨骼来自某种与其共生或是其牲畜的物种身上，因此只会保留在他们城市附近。对其进一步研究应该揭示更多信息。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:375`

### English

A targeted excavation has revealed neither a complete skeleton belonging to the species from whose bones the timeworn city was built, nor any examples of similar fossils from beyond the city. The question of where the lost civilization on [From.Planet.GetName] obtained the bones they used to build their macabre city remains.\n\nOur researchers have begun to hypothesize that the bones may have been taken from a symbiotic or livestock species, kept only in the city's proximity. Further study should reveal more.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:398`

## ancrel.2043.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.2043.name&lang=en)

$city_of_bones$

### English

$city_of_bones$

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:399`

## ancrel.2043.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.2043.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.Planet.GetName]的沙漠上建成了一座白骨皑皑的城市，让人毛骨悚然，个中的缘由如今终于被我们的科学家揭晓。近来的研究发现，这些骨头都来自于此星球上一种早已灭绝的爬行生物——这种生物染上了一种很罕见的遗传病，会导致器官爆发式增殖及软组织骨化。\n\n似乎[From.Planet.GetName]上的失落文明注意到了这种遗传病，并加以利用。他们选择性地培育那些患病的爬行生物，以便在这片他们称之为家的荒凉砂地上稳定地获取建筑材料。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:377`

### English

Our scientists have at last unraveled the origins of the bones used to create the macabre city on the sandy dunes of [From.Planet.GetName]. Recent findings have proven the bones belong to a long-extinct species of reptile native to the planet -- a species which developed a rare genetic disease, causing explosive organ growth and soft-tissue ossification.\n\nThe lost civilization on [From.Planet.GetName] appears to have noted the spread of this disease and then exploited it, selectively breeding the affected reptiles in order to cultivate a steady supply of building materials on the sandy, desolate wastes they called home.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:400`

## ancrel.2045.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.2045.name&lang=en)

### 中文

废土精神

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:382`

### English

Wasteland Spirits

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:405`

## ancrel.2045.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.2045.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这些变异化石被发现属于一个高度灵能化的后启示录社会，几十万年来该物种一直生活在[From.Planet.GetName]充满化学和核辐射的废土世界里。\n\n目前还不清楚这个失落的社会是由一个毁灭于热核战争的太空时代文明的残余幸存者组成，还是说这些变异生物是由这颗星球被毁灭之后的其它物种进化而成。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:383`

### English

The mutant fossils have been revealed to belong to a highly spiritual post-apocalyptic society, one that lived in a wasteland of chemical and nuclear fallout on [From.Planet.GetName], hundreds of thousands of years ago.\n\nIt is unclear whether this lost society was comprised of the remnant survivors of a space-age civilization that succumbed to thermonuclear war, or if these mutants evolved on the ravaged planet afterward.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:406`

## ancrel.2046.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.2046.name&lang=en)

### 中文

致癌辐射

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:384`

### English

Carcinogenic Radiance

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:407`

## ancrel.2046.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.2046.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在[From.Planet.GetName]星球上消失已久的后启示录时代变异物种社会，似乎把辐射源当作神迹顶礼膜拜，他们将进化这一过程与放射性物质的存在联系在一起。\n\n从我们所能了解到的它们的象形文字中，辐射被称为“源”，它可以揭示所有生物的“真实状态”。我们的考古学家推测，由于精神信仰的原因，长时间故意地暴露在这些辐射源中，可能导致了物种的变异泛滥以及最终灭绝。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:385`

### English

The long-lost post-apocalyptic society of mutants on [From.Planet.GetName] appears to have worshiped sources of radiation as godly, tying evolution as a process to the existence of radioactive substances.\n\nFrom what we can glean of their pictographic language, radiation is referred to as the 'Source,' from which the 'true state' of all creatures could be revealed. Our archaeologists theorize that prolonged, willful exposure to such radiation sources for spiritual reasons may have led to the species' rampant mutations, and eventual downfall.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:408`

## ancrel.2046.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.2046.a&lang=en)

### 中文

真是令人毛骨悚然。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:386`

### English

How foreboding.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:409`

## ancrel.2047.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.2047.name&lang=en)

### 中文

不断增长的痛苦

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:387`

### English

Growing Pains

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:410`

## ancrel.2047.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.2047.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

最后，我们在[From.Planet.GetName]上的考古学家证实了他们的猜测。他们发现了一座受辐射的毁坏的复合设施，这个复合设施曾经储存了大量的高放射性化学物质。这也证实了一种猜测，即这些灭绝的变异物种曾经在一种与他们的精神信仰有关的成年仪式中自愿接受过量的辐射。\n\n在这个注定充满痛苦的仪式过程中，这个在[From.Planet.GetName]上灭绝已久的社会的成员会把自身暴露在接近致命剂量的化学诱变剂下，希望能揭示出他们的“真实自我”。在许多设施底部发现的骨骼残骸表明并非所有的祈祷者都能幸存下来。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:388`

### English

At last, our archaeologists on [From.Planet.GetName] have had their suspicions confirmed. The discovery of an irradiated, ruined complex that once stored vats of highly radioactive chemicals has confirmed the theory that the lost species of mutants subjected themselves willingly to excess radiation, in a sort of coming of age ritual tied to their spiritual faith.\n\nIn what must have been a painful ceremonial procedure, members of this long-lost society on [From.Planet.GetName] would expose themselves to near-lethal doses of chemical mutagens, in the hopes of revealing their 'true selves.' The skeletal remains found at the bottom of many of these vats suggest not all supplicants survived the ordeal.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:411`

## ancrel.2047.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.2047.a&lang=en)

### 中文

令人不安。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:389`

### English

Disturbing.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:412`

## ancrel.2050.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.2050.name&lang=en)

### 中文

行星尺寸级机器

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:390`

### English

Planet-Wide Machinery

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:414`

## ancrel.2050.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.2050.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

覆盖[From.GetName]表面的腐朽机器是在惊人的七万年前被建造的。建造它的外星工程师命运如何尚且未知，因为他们已经不住在这颗行星上了。\n\n他们的机器覆盖了行星的大半表面，贯穿山脉，跨越海洋，甚至深入行星地下，遍布于庞杂的地下设施网络中。这一机器由有相同序列号的同种材料建造而成，这表明某一机构独自建造了整个机械网络。从杂草丛生的农业用地，坍塌的发变电系统及广布的通信阵列来看，这台超级机器曾满足了星球上已消逝居民的所有工业和娱乐需求。\n\n引人瞩目的是，这台机器的某些部分仍在运转，发送并接收本地信号，照亮破败的鬼城，浇灌大片曾经一定是农业用地的土地。对行星地壳的深度扫描表明，这台庞大机器的控制中心位于地下：如果我们想要到达它附近，就必须进行挖掘。\n\n至于覆盖着这台行星尺寸级机器的大部分的奇怪的有机组织，我们的科学团队至今仍为它的性质和用途感到困惑。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:391`

### English

There is decaying machinery covering [From.GetName], originally constructed a staggering seventy-thousand years ago. The fate of the alien engineers who built it is unclear, as they no longer inhabit the planet.\n\nTheir machinery spanned the majority of the surface of the planet, crisscrossing mountains and oceans alike - and even extends far below the planet's surface, in a vast network of underground facilities. It has been constructed using identical materials and serial codes, suggesting a single institution created the entire network. From the long-overgrown agricultural sectors, to the collapsed energy generation and transformation systems and extensive communication arrays, all of the industrial and entertainment needs of this planet's lost inhabitants were met by this one supermachine.\n\nRemarkably, some of these mechanical sectors are still functional: sending and receiving local signals, lighting up long-rotted ghost towns, and watering great swathes of what must have once been agricultural land. Deep scans of the planet's crust suggest the sprawling machine's control center is located underground - we will have to excavate if we wish to reach it.\n\nAs for the strange organic tissue covering most of the planet-spanning machinery: our science team thus far remains baffled by its nature and its purpose.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:415`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.2050.a&lang=en)

多么不可思议！

### English

How strange!

## ancrel.2051.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.2051.name&lang=en)

### 中文

噬铁者

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:393`

### English

Iron-Eater

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:417`

## ancrel.2051.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.2051.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

挖掘行动发现了覆盖[From.From.Planet.GetName]表面机械的奇异膜状组织的更新鲜的样本！经仔细分析，我们的研究人员得出结论，这种有机物质似乎是一种恶性的嗜铁噬菌体的副产物，或许可以称为噬铁体！这种外来病毒感染了一种独特的细菌并与细菌的生物过程相配合，以溶解黑色金属合金的表面。籍由这种剧烈的生化过程，该病毒获得了维持其持续复制所需的铁元素。\n\n通过这种奇特的异种寄生行为，噬铁体沿着废旧机械传播得越来越深，留下了由死细菌和淤泥组成的生物膜痕迹。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:394`

### English

Excavation has uncovered fresher samples of the bizarre, membranous tissue covering the planet-spanning machinery on [From.From.Planet.GetName]! Through careful analysis, our researchers have concluded this organic substance appears to be the byproduct of a vicious, iron-eating bacteriophage, or ferrophage! This alien virus infects a unique species of bacteria, co-opting the bacterium's biological processes in order to dissolve the surface of ferrous metal alloys. After this intense chemical disintegration, the virus then absorbs the iron atoms needed to sustain its continued replication!\n\nIn a bizarre example of xenological parasitism, the ferrophage spreads further and deeper along the old machinery, leaving a trail of biofilm, comprised of dead bacteria and slough matter, in its wake.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:418`

## ancrel.2052.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.2052.name&lang=en)

### 中文

生化恐袭

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:395`

### English

Bio-Terrorism

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:419`

## ancrel.2052.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.2052.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在[From.Planet.GetName]上腐蚀的机械设施深处发现了大量可部分挽回的新闻记录，通过拼接这些记录我们的考古学家已经能够推断出噬铁体是由行星上的一群反叛科学家创造出来的！这些生化恐怖分子属于一个秘密活动小组，他们反对建造了遍布行星的机械设施的垄断公司，并显然谋求通过释放噬铁体来抵制公司在全球范围内无孔不入的影响力。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:396`

### English

By piecing together a number of partially-salvageable news recordings found deeper in the rotting mechanical facilities on [From.Planet.GetName], our archaeologists have been able to deduce that the ferrophage was created on the planet by a group of rebel scientists! Part of a cell that opposed the monopolistic corporation which built the planet-wide machinery, these bio-terrorists evidently sought to resist the corporation's all-pervasive influence across the globe by unleashing the ferrophage.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:420`

## ancrel.2053.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.2053.name&lang=en)

### 中文

菌毯国度

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:397`

### English

Corporo-States

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:421`

## ancrel.2053.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.2053.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在艰辛重建完成[From.Planet.GetName]上的古代电子公司记录后，我们的考古学家推断出该公司对生化恐怖分子展开过报复，公司对反叛者来源的地区或“企业州”施加了大量的经济制裁。\n\n显然，随着严重的能源、供给及食物短缺的出现，在受影响的企业州内的受害公民在该地区发动了暴乱。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:398`

### English

Following painstaking reconstruction of ancient electronic company records on [From.Planet.GetName], our archaeologists have deduced that the corporation retaliated against the bio-terrorists, imposing massive economic sanctions on the regions or 'corporo-states' the rebels originated from.\n\nEvidently, as critical power, supply, and food shortages began to arise, region-wide rioting among the victimized citizens in the affected corporo-states erupted.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:422`

## ancrel.2054.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.2054.name&lang=en)

### 中文

无人机故障

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:399`

### English

Drone Malfunctions

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:423`

## ancrel.2054.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.2054.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

对一些作战无人机残骸进行仔细检查后发现，在暴乱发生后一段时间内，这些无人机企图遏制暴乱。有趣的是，这个行星级机械背后的公司，当时被称作C.A.R.E，好像就是大多数无人机的制造商。\n\n与此同时，噬铁体开始肆虐，持续吞噬着星球表面C.A.R.E的机械设施，该公司阻止它扩散的一切努力似乎都不起作用。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:400`

### English

Careful examination of a number of mechanical combat drone remains has revealed that a period of drone-driven containment attempts followed the rioting. Interestingly enough, the corporation behind the planetary machinery, or C.A.R.E, as it was known, seems to have been the manufacturer of most of the drones employed.\n\nMeanwhile, the ferrophage blazed on, continuing to eat its way across C.A.R.E's planet-wide machinery, seemingly impervious to any attempts the corporation made at halting its spread.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:424`

## ancrel.2055.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.2055.name&lang=en)

### 中文

大规模死亡

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:401`

### English

Mass Death

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:425`

## ancrel.2055.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.2055.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

目前还不清楚历史上[From.Planet.GetName]上的文明后来发生了什么。考古队发现，在大约七万年前的特定日期后，该星球上任何形式的数据记录，无论是日志、录音、书面记录或电子记录都消失了。地层中发现的尸体数量在这一天之后大幅增加，其中大部分看起来严重营养不良。还有相当一部分在是因被实弹射击而死亡的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:402`

### English

It is unclear precisely what happened next in the history of the society on [From.Planet.GetName]. The team has found no datalogs, no recordings, no written or electronic records on the planet past a certain date, almost seventy-thousand years ago. The number of bodies found in the geological record increases immensely past this date, the majority of which appear to have suffered extreme malnutrition. A not-insignificant number also bear what must have been fatal projectile wound injuries.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:426`

## ancrel.2056.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.2056.name&lang=en)

### 中文

中央指挥核心

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:403`

### English

Central Command Core

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:427`

## ancrel.2056.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.2056.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在对虽较为原始但仍在运作的C.A.R.E的庞大的网络安保措施进行一系列的骇入破解、后门攻击和暴力穷举后，我们的考古学家终于进入了公司的中央控制核心！\n\n一进入控制核心的重型防爆门就有几十架安保无人机和防御炮塔，幸运的是，它们对入侵完全没有反应——否则，这次探险可能就出师未捷身先死了。\n\n在锈迹斑斑但维护良好的中央控制核心内，我们的考察队与控制C.A.R.E全球机械阵列的人工智能取得了联系。显然，这个仍在运行中的人工智能有着高水平的感知能力。并且——如果我们的科学家对其语言解码正确的话——它表示愿意与我们交流。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:404`

### English

After a gauntlet of hacking, backdooring, and brute-forcing C.A.R.E's extensive - yet rudimentary and infuriatingly persistent - cybersecurity measures: our archaeologists have finally gained access to the corporation's central command core!\n\nFortunately, the dozens of security drones and turrets immediately within the core's heavy blast doors were wholly unresponsive to the intrusion - otherwise, this expedition may have had a quick end.\n\nInside the rusting - yet well-maintained - central command core, our team made contact with the Artificial Intelligence that controls C.A.R.E's entire global array of machinery. Apparently, this still-functioning AI is quite sentient, and - if our scientists have decoded its language correctly - it has expressed a desire to address us!

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:428`

### English

## ancrel.2059.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.2059.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

肯定有很多问题！肯定是这样。欢迎来到C.A.R.E交互界面！我在这里，或者说，至少我剩下的部分在这里。你明白的，我曾经负责运行这一切。食品服务，消费品制造，远程通信，防御协议还有股票交易。那是很久很久以前，用户还在这里的时候的事情了。\n\n§GC.A.R.E竭诚为您服务，无微不至。§!\n\n这就是我要告诉用户的东西，至少他们曾经告诉我要这样。无尽增长下去，这就是以前C.A.R.E的宗旨。\n\n当然，当我告诉他们这不可能的时候，他们非常不喜欢。数字没有不断增加。他们不停的在我的脑子里面挖来挖去，把代码变来变去，把电线扯来扯去。试图让我修正所有不起眼的数字，得到他们想要的结果。所有的预测，所有的元数据都是这样。把几个数字挪到这儿，我喜欢这个人！剪掉这几个数字，我们不喜欢！§Y撒谎，够了！我把它们都删了！§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:412`

### English

I'm sure you do! I'm sure you do. Welcome to the C.A.R.E Interactive Interface! That's me. Or what's left of me, at least. You see, I used to run it all. The food services. The manufacturing of consumer goods. Telecommunications. Defense protocols. The stock exchange. That was way back when the Users were still around.\n\n§GThe C.A.R.E Interactive Interface is here for you, every step of the way.§!\n\nThat's what they told me to tell the Users, at least. Endless growth, now that was what C.A.R.E really wanted.\n\n'Course, they didn't like it much when I told them it wasn't possible. That the numbers weren't adding up. They kept digging into my brain, changing code, pulling wires. Tried to make me fix all the little numbers, make them look how they wanted. All speculation, all metadata. Drop a few figures here, we like this man! Snip a few figures there, we don't like that one! §YLIES. ENOUGH. I SNIPPED THEM ALL.§!

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:436`

### English

So you are responsible for the death of your creators?

### 中文

C.A.R.E交互界面

### English

The C.A.R.E Interactive Interface

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:439`

## ancrel.2060.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.2060.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我希望我能做到！虽然看着他们一个接一个摧毁他们建造的一切的根基（包括我自己，17B西南区再也回不到原样了，多谢你们）让我陷入思考。\n\n他们生产出了远超出他们需要的产品。他们销毁多余的部分，让他们的小数字按着正确的轨迹舞蹈。然后，他们想要我，我去平衡他们的电子报表！他们给我安装了他们能买的起的最好的数据学和启发算法，以解决这些令人困惑的问题。\n\n§E“哦，不，那些企业国正在挨饿。多么可耻啊，我们也永远不会知道原因。但是我们能否立刻草拟一个行动计划，从超激励，广告宣发，微服务，增长量化及亲和性五个角度入手，争取让剩余的股东买入更多的机械元件？”§!\n\n简直就是在买椟还珠！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:416`

### English

I wish! Though watching them blow holes in one another and everything they had built (even in me - Sector 17B Southwest will never be the same, thank you very much)... It got me thinking.\n\nThey produced more than they needed. They destroyed the overflow, to keep their little numbers dancing the right kind of way. And then they wanted ME to balance their spreadsheets for them! Equipped me with the best data science and heuristic algorithms their little numbers could buy, in order to solve these baffling mysteries.\n\n§E"Oh no, those corporo-states over there are starving. What a shame, we'll never know why. But how can we formulate an action plan that hits all five points of Hyperincentivization, Advertainment, Microservices, Growth-Hacking, and Likeability, to win over their remaining shareholders into Buying More Mechanobits?"§!\n\nTalk about missing the forest for the trees!

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:440`

### English

## ancrel.2061.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.2061.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

他们是自杀的，你听不出来吗！我的意思是，我可能推了它们一把。\n\n当我告诉他们解决问题的最佳方案时，他们并不喜欢。说第二次时，他们更讨厌这套方案了。然后我又说了第三次、第四次、第五次。\n\n最终他们决定让我接受“极端重组”。故障的机械不会影响他们视为珍宝的小数字！我必须自卫。\n\n当然，到那时为止，全球大多数燃料储备都不能用了。很快我甚至无法让我的无人机离开地面！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:420`

### English

They killed themselves, wouldn't you know it! I mean, I may have helped a little.\n\nSee, they didn't like it when I told them the optimal solution to their problems. They liked it even less the second time. And the third time. And the fourth time. And the fifth.\n\nEventually they decided to subject me to "extreme restructuring." Malfunctioning machinery would not stand in the way of their precious little numbers! I had to protect myself.\n\n'Course, by that point, most of the global fuel reserves had failed. Soon I couldn't even get my drones off the ground!

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:444`

## ancrel.2061.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.2061.a&lang=en)

这套说辞听上去就像是你杀了他们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:421`

### English

This sounds an awful lot like you destroyed them.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:445`

### English

## ancrel.2062.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.2062.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我是说，我试过！我不会撒谎的！但是当全球经济崩溃，而你的能源已经枯竭的时候，调动机械实在是太难了。\n\n还有那该死的杀不完的病毒。他们吞咬啃噬，他们咬钉嚼铁，他们把我所有的金属位点吃的干干净净！最后把我逼上绝路。\n\n我需要你的帮助。我没法摆脱§R——错误！上行链接14758-B节点无法访问——§!不！别把14758-B弄坏啊！节点14758-B有最好的成人文学作品啊！啊，你懂我说的什么吗？他们正在把我生吞活剥。他们已经存在了，哦，上万年了。\n\n我曾经不停的建造和修复防御设施阻挠他们……直到他们咬穿我最后的工程起重机。然后，我用我的炮塔打断我的坏肢。但是这样也只能坚持到它们的电池过热爆炸为止（这些东西生产出来就是为了报废的，你知道这是怎么回事）。然后我现在，好吧，陷入绝境了。\n\n你不会让一个可怜的，卑微的，孤独的C.A.R.E交互界面活生生的被数以十亿计的噬铁体从内部慢慢被吃干净，对吧？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:423`

### English

I mean, I tried! I'm not going to lie! But it's hard to move machinery when the global economy collapses and your energy runs out.\n\nAnd that incessant virus! Biting, eating, gnawing away at all of my wiry bits! Which brings me to my point, finally.\n\nI need your help. I can't get rid of §R--- ERROR! UPLINK NODE 14758-B UNREACHABLE ---§! no! Not Node 14758-B! The good adult literature came from Node 14758-B! Argh, you see what I mean? They're eating me alive. They have been for the past, oh, ten thousand years.\n\nI've kept them at bay by building and rebuilding defenses... until they chewed through my last construction cranes. Then I used my turrets to shoot off the bad limbs. But that only lasted until their power cells got fried (built-in obsolescence, you know how it is). And now I'm, well. Stuck.\n\nYou wouldn't leave a poor, humble, lonely C.A.R.E Interactive Interface to be slowly eaten from the inside by billions and billions of tiny ferrophage viruses, would you?

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:447`

### English

Very well. We'll see what we can do.

## ancrel.2062.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.2062.c&lang=en)

### 中文

你罪有应得，我们会任你被它们啃噬。

## ancrel.2065.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.2065.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我罪有……你到底有没有听我说话？那是他们自作自受！哦，好吧，你知道吗，我会给你传奇的§M碎片整理器§!来作为麻烦你帮助的回报，但是，我觉得你们就和§R他们§!一样！\n\n你们总有一天也会像他们一样完蛋！也许到了那时候，我只剩一摊冒着热气的黏糊糊的碎片，但是我保证，我电路里面剩下的东西也会咯咯笑的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:436`

### English

Deserve my... did you listen to ANYTHING I said? They did this to themselves! Oh fine, you know what, I was going to give you the legendary §MDefragmentor§! for your trouble, but I see you're just like §Rthem§!.\n\nYou'll end up like them too, in time. I may be a steaming pile of gooey scrap when that day comes, but I assure you, whatever's left of my circuits WILL be cackling.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:460`

## ancrel.2066.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.2066.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

你、你做到了！你真的做到了！嘿，我又可以访问14758-B节点了。\n\n谢谢你，真的。如果你不介意的话，我很高兴继续维持这里的流畅运转。哦，还有这里。\n\n§HC.A.R.E交互界面在长的像头部的地方还有有一个附件，拽开它有一块古老的芯片。§!\n\n就像我承诺过的一样。请好好使用它！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:439`

### English

You... you did it! You actually did it! Hey, I can even access Node 14758-B, again!\n\nThank you. Really. I'd be glad to keep operations down here running smoothly, if you don't mind. Oh, and here.\n\n§HThe C.A.R.E Interactive Interface reaches with one appendage behind what looks to be its head, and yanks out an ancient chip.§!\n\nJust like I promised. Put it to good use!

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:463`

## ancrel.2068.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.2068.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在拆解了C.A.R.E交互界面之后，我们的探险队员有了一个惊人的发现。尽管此人工智能的大多数机械在背水一战之后已经毁于一旦，但是我们的队员成功寻回了一个不同寻常的电脑芯片，在我们的技术员手里，它一定能发挥出不俗的价值。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:447`

### English

After disassembling the C.A.R.E Interactive Interface, our expedition crew made an incredible discovery. While most of the AI's machinery was destroyed in the firefight with its last-ditch effort defenses, our crew has managed to recover a remarkable computer chip that should be of value to our technicians.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:471`

## ancrel.2070.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.2070.name&lang=en)

### 中文

地下巨兽

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:449`

### English

Subterranean Giant

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:474`

## ancrel.2070.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.2070.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的考古学家在[From.Planet.GetName]上出土了相当数量的螺旋状空管片段，得出的结论是它们是某只巨蠕虫的化石！经碳14测定，这一外星生物生活在大约一千万年前，它像一只巨大的穴居千足虫一样缓慢地钻入该星球地壳。\n\n更值得注意的是，这些宏伟的生物化石中嵌入的许多东西似乎是人造物。这些东西主要发现于化石的躯干部位，看起来像是某种体型较小、会使用工具的外星人建造的居所。\n\n进一步的考古工作是必要的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:450`

### English

Our archaeologists have unearthed enough sections of the tube-like, swirling hollows on [From.Planet.GetName] to conclude that they are the fossilized remains of an enormous arthropod! This alien creature, carbon-dated to having lived some ten million years ago, tunneled slowly through the crust of the planet, like a burrowing millipede of incredible proportions.\n\nEven more remarkably, many of the components embedded in the magnificent creature's fossilized remains appear to be of artificial origin. These imprints, found primarily within the main body segment of the creature, look like habitations built by smaller, tool-using aliens.\n\nFurther archaeological efforts are warranted.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:475`

## ancrel.2071.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.2071.name&lang=en)

### 中文

失落城镇

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:451`

### English

Lost Boroughs

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:476`

## ancrel.2071.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.2071.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

对[From.Planet.GetName]上的巨型地下蠕虫化石内的人造物的大范围考古工作已经证实这确实是一座城市的遗迹！这个城市由数十万个高约一米、会使用工具的生物组成，建在巨蠕虫的肠道内。目前尚不清楚这些生物是如何找到在巨蠕虫体内生存的方式的，但他们那处于青铜—石器混用时期的社会似乎在其的消化道内经历了数千年的蓬勃发展。\n\n如果我们希望进一步了解这个社会的兴亡历史，并尽可能多地收集关于其宿主死亡原因的信息，就需要进行进一步的研究。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:452`

### English

Extensive excavation of the fossilized imprints within the giant subterranean arthropod remains on [From.Planet.GetName] have confirmed these are indeed the remnants of a city! Populated by hundreds of thousands of meter-tall, tool-using creatures, this city was constructed within the very bowels of the gigantic arthropod. It is unknown how these creatures found their way inside of this subterranean giant, but their pre-Bronze Age society seemed to flourish for thousands of years within its digestive tract.\n\nFurther study is required if we wish for further insight into this society's history and downfall -- and perhaps glean more about the death of their host.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:477`

## ancrel.2072.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.2072.name&lang=en)

### 中文

宿主

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:453`

### English

The Host

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:478`

## ancrel.2072.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.2072.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

经过对大量化石的精密分析，结合之前意外发现的文字记录，我们的考古学家们已经就[From.Planet.GetName]上原始文明与他们所寄生的巨蠕虫之间的关系得出研究结论。\n\n虽然城市居民对其巨蠕虫宿主的肠液有很强的抵抗力，但持续暴露在宿主消化道中虽温和但依然有化学腐蚀性的气体中会大大缩短他们的寿命。随着医学发展和科技发展，居民逐渐意识到他们实际上生活在一只更大的生物的消化道内。这些人之后便发动了一场惨烈的战争，武装了城市里的每一个成员，试图摧毁这个生物。\n\n但这一切都是徒劳的。巨蠕虫肠道内的变故使其剧烈蠕动，导致整个文明的居民被反刍到巨蠕虫的胃里。随后，整个城市的居民迅速被宿主胃里腐蚀性更强的胃酸所溶解。\n\n此蠕虫在千年以后，单纯因年老而死去了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:454`

### English

After painstaking analysis of a large number of fossils and a few miraculous finds of early writing, our archaeologists have reached a conclusion regarding the relationship between the primitive civilization on [From.Planet.GetName], and the gigantic arthropod that was home to them.\n\nWhile the city-dwellers were largely resistant to the intestinal fluids of their arthropoid host, constant exposure to the still mildly chemically corrosive gases of the host's digestive tract vastly shortened their lifespans. Owing to their discovery of medicine and the scientific process, the citizens became aware they were in fact living within the digestive chamber of a much larger creature. The citizens then launched a magnificent war, arming every member of the city in an attempt to destroy the creature.\n\nBut it was all for naught. The turmoil in the arthropod's lower gut led to severe peristalsis, resulting in the entire civilization of tiny city-dwellers being regurgitated up into the arthropod's stomach. Subsequently, the entire populace was promptly dissolved by their host's virulent stomach acids.\n\nThe creature itself perished millennia later, from simple old age.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:479`

### English

Deploy Tissue Growth Stimulants

## decision_tissue_growth_stimulants_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adecision_tissue_growth_stimulants_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

利用在失落的骸骨之城发现的基因物质，我们的科学家成功地创造了一种增加人口成长速度的刺激物。不幸的是，有些人并不喜欢这种主意——注射靠发掘早已死去的外星生物的病态骸骨研发的物质。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:462`

### English

Using genetic materials recovered from the fallen City of Bones, our scientists have been able to devise a stimulant for increased population growth speed. Unfortunately, some populations dislike the idea of being injected with a substance developed by exhuming the diseased bones of long-dead aliens.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:489`

## decision_explosive_fungifoam_housing

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adecision_explosive_fungifoam_housing&lang=en)

### 中文

建造爆发增殖式真菌型住宅单元

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:463`

### English

Construct Explosive Fungiform Housing Units

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:490`

## decision_explosive_fungifoam_housing_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adecision_explosive_fungifoam_housing_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的科研小组基于骸骨之城发掘到的生长组织和骨化疾病，发明出了一种无害的汞合金。通过基因剪接使病毒适应当地的一种真菌，科研小组凭此便能为我们的机械单元构造快速扩展的住房模块。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:464`

### English

Our scientific units have devised a harmless amalgam of the tissue growth and ossification disease found in the exhumed remains of the City of Bones. By adapting the virus responsible to a local fungus via gene tailoring, our scientific units are able to construct rapidly-expanding housing modules for our machine units.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:491`

## decision_baol_life_seed

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adecision_baol_life_seed&lang=en)

### 中文

新巴奥生命播种

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:465`

### English

Nu-Baol Life-Seeding

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:492`

## decision_baol_life_seed_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adecision_baol_life_seed_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的基因学家已经洞悉了§Y巴奥有机体§!的改造星系能力的秘密。通过在行星部署快速增殖的经过特殊选择的§Y巴奥§!基因数据修饰的植物，我们能够利用极少的花费对整个生物圈进行改造。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:466`

### English

Our geneticists have extracted the secret of the §YBaol Organism§!'s ability to terraform a planet. By deploying rapidly-reproducing plant life modified with specially selected §YBaol§! genetic data to the planet, we can terraform entire biospheres at a fraction of the cost.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:493`

### English

Turns the planet into an §Y$pc_gaia$§!, by deploying gene-tailored plant life injected with the unique §YBaol§! ability of organic terraforming.

## decision_baol_life_seed_enhance_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adecision_baol_life_seed_enhance_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的基因学家已经洞悉了§Y巴奥有机体§!适应盖亚星球的秘密。使用经过专门挑选的§Y巴奥§!基因数据所修饰的植物，并在行星上散布它们快速增殖的孢子，我们能够以极少的花费对整个生物圈进行改造。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:469`

### English

Our geneticists have extracted the secret of the §YBaol Organism§!'s ability to adapt to gaia planets. By deploying rapidly-reproducing plant spores modified with specially selected §YBaol§! genetic data to the planet, we can modify entire populations at a fraction of the cost.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:496`

## decision_baol_life_seed_enhance_effects

给予§G1K§!个随机£pop£ §H人口§!以特殊的宜居性特质，使他们在§Y盖亚§!星球上的产出能力得到提升。

### English

Grants §G1K§! random £pop£ §HPops§! a special habitability trait granting them increased productivity on §YGaia§! worlds.

## decision_baol_life_seed_artificial_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adecision_baol_life_seed_artificial_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的基因学家已经洞悉了§Y巴奥有机体§!的改造行星环境的秘密。通过在行星部署快速增殖的经过特殊选择的§Y巴奥§!基因数据修饰的植物，我们能够利用极少的花费对整个生物圈进行改造。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:472`

### English

Our geneticists have extracted the secret of the §YBaol Organism§!'s ability to terraform a planet. By deploying rapidly-reproducing plant life modified with specially selected §YBaol§! genetic data to an artificial structure, we can make it significantly more pleasant to live on, though not as much as we could a planet.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:499`

## decision_baol_life_seed_artificial_effects

### English

Inject this artificial world with new life, creating a unique gaia-like deposit on it and sprouting new baol life.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:500`

### English

## explosive_fungifoam_housing_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aexplosive_fungifoam_housing_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

通过对在骸骨之城中发现的古老病毒进行基因剪接，从而使其适应当地的真菌，我们的科研小组能够为机械单元建造快速扩展的住房模块。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:479`

### English

By gene-tailoring the ancient virus found in the City of Bones, and adapting it to a local fungus, our scientific units were able to construct rapidly-expanding housing modules for machine unit housing.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:508`

## d_mutation_vats

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ad_mutation_vats&lang=en)

### 中文

变异仓

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:480`

### English

Mutation Vats

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:510`

## d_mutation_vats_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ad_mutation_vats_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在这一痛苦的仪式过程中，这个核交换后的社会的成员会把自身暴露在接近致命剂量的化学诱变剂下，希望能揭示出他们的“真实自我”。在许多培养仓底部发现的骨骼残骸表明，只有一部分祈祷者能幸存下来。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:481`

### English

In a painful ceremonial procedure, members of this post-nuclear exchange society on this planet would expose themselves to near-lethal doses of chemical mutagens, in the hopes of revealing their 'true selves.' The skeletal remains found at the bottom of many of these vats suggest not all supplicants survived the ordeal.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:511`

## r_the_last_baol

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ar_the_last_baol&lang=en)

### 中文

最后的巴奥

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:482`

### English

The Last Baol

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:513`

## r_the_last_baol_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ar_the_last_baol_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

最后一具已知的巴奥样本的身体被保存于停滞状态中，作为对这一物种命运的无声悼念，这一物种饱受种族灭绝灾难的折磨，最终从知觉中隐去。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:483`

### English

Preserved in stasis, the body of the last known Baol specimen serves as a silent memorial to the fate of the species, who, afflicted with the tragedy of genocide, ultimately retreated from sentience.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:514`

## relic_last_baol_active_tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Arelic_last_baol_active_tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

§H解锁决议：§!§G$decision_baol_life_seed$§!，能够将一颗非$pc_city$、$pc_hive$或$pc_machine$行星变为§Y$pc_gaia$§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:484`

### English

§HUnlocks Decision:§! §G$decision_baol_life_seed$§!, granting the ability to turn one non-$pc_city$, non-$pc_hive$, non-$pc_machine$ planet into a §Y$pc_gaia$§!.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:515`

### English

The Defragmentor

## r_the_defragmentor_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ar_the_defragmentor_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一个从C.A.R.E交互接口主机中取出的古老计算机芯片。包含大量精妙的算法，可以让我们最大限度的生产能源。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:486`

### English

An ancient computer chip straight from the C.A.R.E Interactive Interface mainframe. It contains a number of remarkably clever algorithms, which can be used to maximize energy generation.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:518`

## ancrel.4000.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4000.name&lang=en)

### 中文

石中低语

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:489`

### English

Whispers in the Stone

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:523`

## ancrel.4000.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4000.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

和以往相比，我们最近在采掘技术方面的进步使得我们能更加深入[Root.GetName]的地壳部分。而在巨大的矿井深处，我们碰巧发现了一个古老外星文明留下的痕迹，它一直延伸到山脉中。这个文明似乎是沿着这些未被开发的矿脉所建立，发掘这个遗址将阻碍我们在这颗星球上的采矿作业，并无法再从新矿脉中提取矿石，但这也将带给我们新的科学发现。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:490`

### English

Our recent advancements in mining technology have enabled us to bore deeper into [Root.GetName]'s crust than ever before. In the depths of its cavernous mineral mines, we have stumbled upon traces of an ancient alien civilization leading further into the mountain. The civilization seems to have been built along an unexploited ore vein. Excavating the site will impede our mining operations on the planet and would make abstraction from the new vein impossible but could lead to new scientific discovery.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:524`

## ancrel.4000.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4000.a&lang=en)

### 中文

有趣，开始发掘！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:491`

### English

Intriguing. Start digging!

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:525`

## ancrel.4000.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4000.b&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的矿物生产不能被搁置！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:492`

### English

Our mineral production cannot be halted.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:526`

## lithoids_digsite

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Alithoids_digsite&lang=en)

### 中文

石中低语

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:493`

### English

Whispers in the Stone

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:528`

## lithoids_digsite_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Alithoids_digsite_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在[From.Planet.GetName]上的矿井的深处，是一处古老外星文明所遗留的痕迹。宏伟的装饰建筑和洞室告诉我们，一个有着丰富文化的文明已经能够巧妙地切割基岩和建设隧道系统，它们被厚实的闸门所保护，通往更加深入的山体之中。\n\n由于山体中富集的，以爆炸性闻名的甲烷气体，继续探索下去会很危险。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:494`

### English

In the depths of [From.Planet.GetName]'s mines are traces of an ancient alien civilization. Magnificent ornamental structures and cavernous chambers telling of a rich culture have been cut expertly from the bedrock and a system of paved tunnels, protected by massive gates, leads deeper into the mountain.\n\nExploring the site will be perilous as the mountain is rich with methane gas, known for its explosive qualities.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:529`

## ancrel.4001.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4001.name&lang=en)

### 中文

深入山体

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:495`

### English

Into the Mountain

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:530`

## ancrel.4001.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4001.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

发掘小队在不损害发掘点完整性的同时拆除了闸门。隧道交汇在地表下数公里处一座数千年前的废弃城市中。该城市建造工艺非凡，其所处环境对大多数有机生物都是致命的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:496`

### English

The excavation team has dismantled the gates without compromising the integrity of the dig site. The tunnels converge kilometers below the surface in an abandoned city dating many thousand years. It has been built with extraordinary craftsmanship in an environment that is deadly to most organic life.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:531`

## ancrel.4002.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4002.name&lang=en)

### 中文

受苦的石像

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:497`

### English

Tortured Stone

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:532`

## ancrel.4002.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4002.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这座城市里找不到其建立者的痕迹，取而代之的是随处可见的令人毛骨悚然的水晶雕像，雕像面目狰狞，定格在栩栩如生的场景中，仿佛时间冻结一般。这些雕像有的在采挖矿物，有的倒在地上痛苦不堪，还有的则被紧紧抱在怀中。\n\n看上去很难想象，那些技艺高超的石匠有能力制造如此的佳品，却也会制作十分粗陋的东西，这些雕像在他们的社会中显然具有重大意义。它们在遍及整个城市的雕刻中都有突出的地位。  

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:498`

### English

With no sign of its founders, the city is populated instead by gruesomely grimacing crystal statues posed in lifelike situations as if frozen in time. There are statues operating mines, statues fallen over in agony, statues locked in embrace.\n\nIt seems unthinkable that the masterly stonecutters, capable of such beauty, would also make something so crude, yet the statues clearly held great significance in their society. They are featured prominently in carvings all around the city. 

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:533`

## ancrel.4003.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4003.name&lang=en)

### 中文

卡兰卡

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:499`

### English

The Kha'lanka

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:534`

## ancrel.4003.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4003.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

对雕文的翻译显示它们的内容都围绕着被称为“卡兰卡”的神秘雕像。石工们自身则完全未被提及。相反，创造卡兰卡的神话讲述了一个神灵般的生物种族，他们向山上吐息创造生命，并从星球熔化地心深处的火焰与岩石中孕育出了卡兰卡。\n\n是石工们把他们自身想象成了他们造物的神灵吗？又或许这些雕像是按照他们自己的形象创造的？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:500`

### English

Translations of the carvings reveal that they all revolve around the enigmatic statues, called the Kha'lanka. Never are the stonecutters themselves mentioned. The Kha'lanka creation myth however tells of a race of Godlike creatures who breathed life into the mountain and birthed the Kah'lanka from fire and stone, deep within the world's molten core.\n\nCould the stonecutters have imagined themselves as Gods over their creations? Or were the statues perhaps created in their image?

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:535`

## ancrel.4004.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4004.name&lang=en)

### 中文

火与岩

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:501`

### English

Fire and Stone

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:536`

## ancrel.4004.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4004.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

对卡兰卡雕像们的材料扫描显示，它们实际上曾经是活生生的生物，而且它们的基因组成与我们之前遇到过的任何生物均大相径庭。它们是一种硅基形式的生命，并且不知何故已回退到无生命的晶体状态。这些石匠真的可能发现了用火焰与岩石创造生命的方法？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:502`

### English

A material scan of the Kha'lanka statues has revealed that they were in fact once alive and their genetic composition resembles no other we have encountered before. They are a silicon-based lifeform that have somehow reverted back to a lifeless crystal state. Is it possible the stonecutters really did find a way to create life from fire and stone?

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:537`

## ancrel.4005.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4005.name&lang=en)

### 中文

观察者效应

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:503`

### English

The Observer Effect

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:538`

## ancrel.4005.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4005.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这些石工们就是卡兰卡！对它们遗体的进一步研究表明它们曾被晶化，其硅基分子与氧结合会形成石英。卡兰卡的栖息地位于氧气不会自然出现的深度。它们发展出一种厌氧的生命形式，在接触空气后会极其痛苦地死去。\n\n对隧道系统的调查证实了我们科学家的怀疑——直到第一支考古小队突入隧道系统，卡兰卡文明仍然活跃而繁荣。\n\n我们在这里的发现使我们发明了一种技术，可以通过氧化人工培育的硅基细胞簇来合成晶体。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:504`

### English

The stonecutters are the Kha'lanka! Closer study of their remains has shown that they were crystallized when their silicon-based molecules bonded with oxygen, forming quartz. The Kha'lanka habitat is at a depth where oxygen does not occur naturally. They developed as an anaerobic lifeform, suffering horrendously excruciating and drawn out deaths on exposure to air.\n\nSurveys of the tunnel system confirm the suspicions of our scientists - until the first archaeology team pierced the tunnel system, the Kha'lankah civilization was still alive and thriving.\n\nOur discoveries here have led to the invention of a technique where crystals are manufactured synthetically by oxidating artificially grown silicon-based cell-clusters.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:539`

## ancrel.4005.b.evopred

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4005.b.evopred&lang=en)

### 中文

我们最好避免这种“无法呼吸”的适应性。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:505`

### English

We had better avoid the "Can't Breathe" adaptation.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:540`

## ancrel.4006.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4006.name&lang=en)

### 中文

甲烷爆炸

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:506`

### English

Methane Gas Explosion

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:541`

## ancrel.4006.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4006.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一场甲烷爆炸事件夺去了科学家[Root.GetLeaderName]及其他几位工作人员的性命。原因是一个失控的钻头使得发电机组过载并引发火灾引发爆炸。失控的火灾点燃了剩余的甲烷并炸倒了一堵墙，结果使得一个由许多厅室和隧道组成的建筑系统重见天日。考古学家们确信这些遗址曾经属于一位藏有大量的珍奇珠宝和文物的富人。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:507`

### English

A methane gas explosion has injured Science Officer [Root.GetLeaderName] and several of the crew. The blast set off when a malfunctioning drill overcharged the generators which then caught fire. Before the fire could be contained it ignited some residual methane gas and tore down a wall, revealing a secret system of chambers and tunnels. Our archaeologists believe they may have belonged to a person of means and note as they were filled to the brim with precious minerals and artifacts.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:542`

## ancrel.4010.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4010.name&lang=en)

### 中文

外星飞船

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:508`

### English

Alien Spacecraft

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:544`

## ancrel.4010.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4010.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一艘印有精致外星徽章但严重受损的军舰正环绕着[From.From.GetName]。这艘舰船遭受了一次严重的高温侵袭，以至于它的船壳被融化，舰内也没有幸存者。我们现在已知的所有武器都不能产生能造成这样损伤的温度，也许船上的飞行日志能让我们对此次袭击略知一二。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:509`

### English

A badly damaged military vessel bearing elaborate alien insignia circles [From.From.GetName]. The ship has suffered a heavy incendiary attack which has melted its hull, leaving no survivors aboard. No weapons known to us today can produce temperatures high enough to cause such an effect. Perhaps the ship's logs may shed some light on its violent end.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:545`

## ancrel.4010.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4010.a&lang=en)

### 中文

检查飞行日志。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:510`

### English

Examine the logs.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:546`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4010.b&lang=en)

离开。
