## site_hive_coral

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asite_hive_coral&lang=en)

### 中文

波涛之下

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:750`

### English

Beneath the Waves

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:808`

## site_hive_coral_intro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asite_hive_coral_intro&lang=en)

### 中文

科学船在[From.Planet.GetName]轨道上进行的扫描显示了其海面下有着奇怪的构造，海水以难以解释的方式在流动。虽然该星球天气相对平静，风暴很少，但海面下的水受一些未知力量的影响，不断翻涌和搅动。建议进行详细调查。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:751`

### English

Scans made by the Science Ship in orbit of [From.Planet.GetName] shows some strange formations beneath the waves, and inexplicable water currents. The weather is relatively placid and storms are rare, yet the planetary seas seem to constantly roil and churn, driven by some unknown force beneath the surface. A closer inspection is recommended.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:809`

## ancrel.9310.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.9310.name&lang=en)

### 中文

刺穿水面

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:752`

### English

Surface Piercers

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:810`

## ancrel.9310.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.9310.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在[From.Planet.GetName]海域的探险遇到了一些奇怪的事情。大量有机物尖峰刺穿水面，向天空伸展数百米，其材质为珊瑚或动物骨骼。\n\n尖峰以团簇的形式成打出现。这种构造在星球各处海域都很常见，除了最寒冷的极地海域。尖峰为多孔结构，其表面覆盖有大小不等的孔道，孔道尺寸与舰载机相当。也许这种生物结构与其繁殖过程有关？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:753`

### English

The surface expedition down to the seas of [From.Planet.GetName] has come across something strange. Great spikes of organic material pierce the surface of the water, made from some kind of fossilized coral or bone, stretching hundreds of meters towards the sky.\n\nThe spikes are found in claw-like clusters of up to a few dozen, and formations like these are commonplace everywhere except the very coldest polar seas. The spikes are porous, covered with perforations ranging in size up to that of a strike craft. Perhaps they are related to how these bio-structures propagate themselves?

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:811`

## ancrel.9315.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.9315.name&lang=en)

### 中文

水流发生器

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:754`

### English

Current Makers

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:812`

## ancrel.9315.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.9315.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

探险队最终成功地在行星海底找到了一些长而均匀的生物构造。这些水下结构由与水面的尖峰材质相同，其运行机制类似于一种生物泵。该生物网络不断“呼吸”，呼吸搅动了海水。\n\n这些构造的尺寸，以及其在海底的规模，暗示它们是造成[From.Planet.GetName]海面下难以解释的海水涌动的原因。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:755`

### English

The expedition has finally managed to reach a set of long and evenly shaped bio-formations on the bottom of the planetary sea. Made of the same material as the surface-piercing spikes, these underwater formations appear to be some kind of organic pumping mechanism. The living network "breathes", and its exhalations cycle the current of the seas.\n\nThe size of these formations, as well as the sheer number of them across the ocean floor, hints that they are responsible for the unexplained underwater currents of [From.Planet.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:813`

## ancrel.9320.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.9320.name&lang=en)

### 中文

珊瑚虫巢

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:756`

### English

The Hive Coral

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:814`

## ancrel.9320.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.9320.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

另一支深海探险队回来了，揭示了[FromFrom.Planet.GetName]海底暗流的真相。该星球上所有怪异的有机构造实际上都与一座巨大的珊瑚礁相连。事实证明，这种珊瑚在某种程度上是有意识的，尽管与我们这种感性生物相比，它有着完全不同的思维方式。通过在水中释放信息素，它或多或少地奴役了海洋中所有其他生物，控制它们的行为和繁殖周期，以满足自身的需要。\n\n它似乎是完全无害的，但它仍然是一位木偶大师。它是这颗星球上最顶级的生命吗？或者是一只外来的寄生虫？与之沟通似乎是不可能的，所以我们可能永远不会知道真相了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:757`

### English

Another deep sea expedition has returned, and revealed the truth of the churning seas of [FromFrom.Planet.GetName]. All the strange organic formations on the planet are actually connected in a single, vast coral reef. This coral, as it turned out, is partly aware, although with an utterly alien mindset compared to our conception of sentient life. Through pheromones excreted in the water, it has more or less enslaved the rest of all the organisms in the sea, controlling their behavior and reproduction cycles to fit its own needs.\n\nIt seems to be completely benign, but it is a puppet master nonetheless. Is it simply the apex life form on this world? An intrusive alien parasite? Communicating with it seems impossible, so we may never know.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:815`

## ancrel.9320.b.evopred

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.9320.b.evopred&lang=en)

### 中文

通过适应我们就不会遭受这样的入侵了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:758`

### English

In adapting, we will suffer no similar intrusions.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:816`

## ancrel.9325.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.9325.name&lang=en)

### 中文

生物救星

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:759`

### English

Bio-Savior

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:817`

## ancrel.9325.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.9325.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在对这种外星生物的DNA进行测序后，我们得出了一个惊人的发现：遍布全球海洋的珊瑚网络不仅不是这个世界的原生物种，而且连自然物种都不是，它实际上是一个人造的生物工程产品。在厚厚的珊瑚层下面的是这世界整个生态系统灭绝生物的化石层。似乎珊瑚在被引入后，就逐渐扼杀了所有的本土生物，取而代之的是一个为自己量身定制的生态系统。\n\n关于为什么要这样做，有两个主要的猜想：要么这种人造珊瑚是出于良性的原因被引入的，然后它以一种意想不到的方式在竞争中彻底击垮了原生物种；要么它是被故意设计成用来清除本地海洋世界的原生生物的可怕生物武器。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:760`

### English

After sequencing the alien DNA, a startling revelation was made: the global sea coral network is not only not native to this world, but it is in fact a bio-engineered construct rather than a natural formation. Beneath the thick carpet of alien coral is a fossilized extinction layer of the entire previous ecosystem of this world. It seems the coral, once introduced, gradually choked out all the native life and replaced it with an ecosystem tailored specifically for itself.\n\nThere are two main theories as to why this might have been done: either the artificial coral was introduced for benign reasons, and then it massively out-competed the local wildlife in an unexpected way, or it was deliberately constructed to be some sort of terrifying biological weapon to cleanse ocean worlds of native life.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:818`

## ancrel.9330.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.9330.name&lang=en)

### 中文

欢迎委员会

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:761`

### English

Welcoming Committee

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:819`

## ancrel.9330.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.9330.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这是一台巨大的生物机器！地质分析表明，行星的海洋曾一度陷入死寂——没有洋流，也没有氧气来维持水生生物的生存。这种生物工程机器被引入该星球，目的是人为创造生物生存的基本先决条件。\n\n显然它起作用了，因为现在在此星球上有着繁盛的水下动植物群。找不到任何线索来解释为何要进行如此巨大的工程，也没有找到由谁——或许是一种未知的水生物种，利用先进的生物技术进行环境改造，为自己创造一个新的家园？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:762`

### English

It's all one giant bio-machine! Subsurface analysis indicates that the planet's oceans were once dead - no current or oxygen to sustain underwater life. This bio-engineered machine was introduced to the planet with the purpose of artificially jump starting the basic prerequisites of organic existence.\n\nApparently it worked, as underwater flora and fauna now thrives on the world. No trace was found of why such a colossal undertaking was carried out, nor by whom - was it perhaps some terraforming project by an unknown aquatic species with advanced biotechnology, seeking to create for themselves a new home?

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:820`

## site_orbital_rain

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asite_orbital_rain&lang=en)

### 中文

一查到底

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:763`

### English

Get to the Bottom

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:822`

## site_orbital_rain_intro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asite_orbital_rain_intro&lang=en)

### 中文

来自[From.Planet.GetName]的可见光传感器反馈的信号显示这个海洋星球的海床上有着极不寻常的构造。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:764`

### English

The visual sensor feed from [From.Planet.GetName] shows formations in the seabed of this ocean world that are highly unusual.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:823`

## ancrel.9335.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.9335.name&lang=en)

### 中文

海底之伤

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:765`

### English

Wounds on the Ocean Floor

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:824`

## ancrel.9335.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.9335.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

对[From.Planet.GetName]的仔细检查表明，广阔的海床看起来异常奇怪——像是卫星或小行星的表面，完全被撞击坑覆盖。这样的陨石坑一般不会出现在有大气层的天体上，特别是那些有海洋的天体上。\n\n必须进行进一步的研究。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:766`

### English

Closer inspection of [From.Planet.GetName] reveals that the bottom of the vast oceans look exceptionally strange - more like the surface of a moon or an asteroid - completely covered in impact craters. Craters like that tend to not appear on celestial bodies with atmosphere, and especially not on the ones with oceans.\n\nFurther studies must be made.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:825`

## ancrel.9340.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.9340.name&lang=en)

### 中文

超凡脱俗之水

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:767`

### English

Otherworldly Waters

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:826`

## ancrel.9340.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.9340.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

海底神秘陨石坑的谜底终于在对水体的分析中找到了。某些矿物和化学成分的痕迹证明，这些水实际上并不来自同一颗行星。它以巨冰的形式被运到了这里，被扔到星球表面。冰块坠毁，破碎成碎片并融化，最终将整颗星球表面覆盖在水下。\n\n这绝对是一项不朽的事业，但尚不清楚该工程究竟是为了什么目的而实施。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:768`

### English

The answer to the mysterious craters in the ocean floor was finally found in an analysis of the water itself. Traces of certain minerals and chemical compositions proved that the water did in fact not belong to the same planet. It was transported here in the form of gargantuan blocks of ice that were hurled down on the planet. The ice blocks crashed, cracked into splinters and melted, eventually covering almost the entire surface with water.\n\nIt was an absolutely monumental undertaking, but there is nothing that hints to why it was carried out.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:827`

## ancrel.9345.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.9345.name&lang=en)

### 中文

当前事件

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:769`

### English

Current Events

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:828`

## ancrel.9345.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.9345.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[FromFrom.Planet.GetName]轨道上的科研船从海底深处检测到了一些不能识别的能量读数，仔细检查后，探险队发现了一座古代建筑。这是一个长达数公里的人工构造，只有一个功能：人为制造水下洋流。\n\n水下循环对许多水生生物而言至关重要，目前的理论是，这颗行星由一些已能进行太空航行的水生物种改造成如今的模样。但好像他们从未露面。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:770`

### English

The Science Ship in orbit of [FromFrom.Planet.GetName] picked up some unidentified energy readings from deep beneath the waves, and upon inspection the expedition discovered an ancient construction. A structure many kilometers long with a single purpose; to artificially create underwater currents.\n\nUnderwater circulation is vital for much aquatic life, and the current theory is that this planet was set up to be habitable for some aquatic, spacefaring species. It appears as if they never showed up.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:829`

## ancrel.9350.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.9350.name&lang=en)

### 中文

水下存储

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:771`

### English

Submerged Storage

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:830`

## ancrel.9350.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.9350.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

通过跟踪一个奇怪的能量信号，我们的探险队找到了一座埋在海底深处的外星通信阵列。若能翻译其包含的信息片段，便可得知外星引水工程的全貌。\n\n根据翻译，整个造海项目是为了创造一座巨大的水下储仓，但它没有具体说明要存储什么东西。当然，该项目不需要淹没整颗星球。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:772`

### English

By following a strange energy signature our expedition managed to locate an alien communications array buried deep in the seabed. It contained information fragments, and once translated they told of the water-introduction project.\n\nAccording to the translation the entire ocean-making venture was done to create an immense submerged storage, but it does not specify what needed to be stored. Certainly not what requires an entire planet covered in water.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:831`

## ancrel.9355.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.9355.name&lang=en)

### 中文

气候控制

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:773`

### English

Climate Control

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:832`

## ancrel.9355.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.9355.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

多亏了科研船的意外发现，我们成功挖出了深埋在海底深处的古建筑。在那里进行探险活动非常困难，但结果是惊人的。\n\n该建筑曾经是一个气候控制中心。它引导大型气候改造机器进入轨道。这是一个被中断的环境改造项目，但显然工程已经走得够远了。即使后续投入中断了，但有蓬勃的自然演化接手后续工作，最终创造了现在的生态系统。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:774`

### English

Thanks to serendipitous sensory scan the Science Ship managed to isolate ancient building buried deep under the ocean floor. Getting the expedition there turned out to be very difficult, but the finding was grand.\n\nThe building was once a climate control center. It directed giant climate-altering machines in orbit. This was a terraforming project that was interrupted, but apparently the project had gotten far enough. Even without directed input, nature managed to thrive and eventually create the ecosystem that can be seen today.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:833`

## site_fossilized_jellyfish

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asite_fossilized_jellyfish&lang=en)

### 中文

星罗群岛

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:775`

### English

Dotted Archipelago

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:835`

## site_fossilized_jellyfish_intro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asite_fossilized_jellyfish_intro&lang=en)

### 中文

在[From.Planet.GetName]轨道上的科学船已经探测到遍布这个海洋星球表面的圆形岛屿群。从地质的角度来看，它们非常不寻常：其表面看起来很自然，没有人造结构，但它们都呈圆形，且几乎是完美的圆。我们需要前往那里收集更多数据。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:776`

### English

The Science Ship in orbit around [From.Planet.GetName] has detected clusters of small, round islands dotting the ocean planet. From a geological standpoint they are highly unusual: the surface appears natural, with no artificial constructions, yet they all appear to be circular in shape, nearly perfectly so. We will need to head down there to gather more data.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:836`

## ancrel.9360.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.9360.name&lang=en)

### 中文

岛屿隧道

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:777`

### English

Island Tunnels

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:837`

## ancrel.9360.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.9360.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

登陆到[From.Planet.GetName]表面的探险队开始调查其中一个圆形岛屿，并很快有了不同寻常的发现。岛下有一套复杂的隧道网络，蜿蜒穿过奇特的油性岩层。这些隧道呈均匀的圆形，并且在所有岛屿下面都能发现。它们似乎是自然形成而非人工挖掘出的，但它们的形成方式肯定与通常的地质运动不同。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:778`

### English

The surface expedition sent down to [From.Planet.GetName] started to investigate one of the round islands, and soon made a remarkable discovery. The island had a complex tunnel network running beneath it, winding through the strange, oily rock. These tunnels were remarkably even and round, and laced the entire island. They seem to be natural formations, not intentionally excavated, but they have certainly not been shaped in a conventional, geological manner.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:838`

## ancrel.9365.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.9365.name&lang=en)

### 中文

无处不在的隧道

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:779`

### English

Tunnels Everywhere

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:839`

## ancrel.9365.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.9365.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

地面探险队深入圆形岛屿时，又有两项新的发现：第一，每个岛的下面似乎都有重复的内部隧道布局。它们的大小略有不同，但布局是一样的。第二个发现更令人惊讶：这些岛屿不是地壳的一部分——它们实际上是漂浮的！它们的下层充满了气囊，它们与相当脆弱和多孔的岛屿岩石相结合，让这些岛漂了起来。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:780`

### English

As the surface expedition delved deeper into the round islands, two more discoveries were made: First, that every island seem to have a duplicate internal layout of tunnels beneath them. They vary slightly in size, but not in how they are laid out. The second discovery was even more astonishing: the islands aren't parts of the planet crust - they actually float! Their lower levels are filled with pockets of gas, which combined with the fairly brittle and porous island rock makes then buoyant.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:840`

## ancrel.9370.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.9370.name&lang=en)

### 中文

冰冻凝胶

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:781`

### English

Frozen Jelly

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:841`

## ancrel.9370.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.9370.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

关于浮岛之谜的答案不是在地质学领域找到的，而是在生物学领域。这些岛屿实际上是巨大的水母化石。数百万年前的冰河时代把它们都冻死了，之后它们的尸体变成了化石，形成了今天看到的漂浮在水面上的岛屿。长年累月的碎屑堆积使它们裸露的表面看起来像天然岛屿，但它们的下面完全是有机的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:782`

### English

The answer to the mystery of the floating islands was not found in the field of geology, but in biology. The islands are in fact enormous fossilized jellyfish creatures. Millions of years ago an ice age froze them all to death, after which their carcasses were fossilized into the islands seen bobbing around in the water today. Ages of debris accretion gave their exposed surface the look of natural islands, but beneath that they are entirely organic.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:842`

## ancrel.9370.b.evopred

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.9370.b.evopred&lang=en)

### 中文

真是块美味的土地。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:783`

### English

A tasty looking patch of ground.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:843`

## ancrel.9375.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.9375.name&lang=en)

### 中文

自然休眠

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:784`

### English

Natural Slumber

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:844`

## ancrel.9375.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.9375.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

它们根本不是岛屿，而是水母！事实上，每一个漂浮的圆圈都是一头巨大的无脊椎动物。而且他们没有死去：看似石化的状态只是他们生命周期中的一个自然组成部分。在数千年的时间里，这些石质岛屿释放出沉积物，直到它们完全溶解。沉积物中含有微小的水螅虫，这些水螅虫最终会在水中出芽，直到它们进入生命周期的下一阶段。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:785`

### English

They are not islands at all, but jellyfish! Each of these floating circles is in fact a giant invertebrate organism. And they aren't dead, either: the seemingly fossilized state is just a natural part of their life cycle. Over the course of thousands of years, these stony islands release sediment until they finally dissolve completely. The sediment contains microscopic polyps that eventually germinate in the water until they enter the next stage of their life cycle.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:845`

## ancrel.9380.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.9380.name&lang=en)

### 中文

无法应付的大家伙

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:786`

### English

Big Ones Couldn't Cope

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:846`

## ancrel.9380.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.9380.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

它们根本不是岛屿。它们全是外星生物：水母生物，它们出于某些原因被人工地引入了生态系统。然而，它们的引入似乎并没有严格按计划进行。大部分大型的水母生物死去并变成了化石，再演变成浮岛群，但是较小的水母生物适应了环境，它们现在在行星上的海洋中繁衍生息。它们最终是否会步更大体型祖先的后尘还需拭目以待。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:787`

### English

They aren't islands at all. They are all alien organisms: jellyfish creatures that for some reason were artificially introduced to the ecosystem. It seems their introduction did not go exactly as planned, however. Most of the big ones died and fossilized - becoming the floating island archipelago - but the smaller jellyfish creatures assimilated and are now thriving in the oceans across the planet. Whether they will eventually share the fate of their bigger ancestors remains to be seen.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:847`

## site_popular_rock

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asite_popular_rock&lang=en)

### 中文

受欢迎的岩石

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:788`

### English

Popular Rock

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:849`

## site_popular_rock_intro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asite_popular_rock_intro&lang=en)

### 中文

传感器在一簇小行星内检测到了能量读数。建议进行更深入的调查。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:789`

### English

Sensors have detected energy readings in a cluster of asteroids. Further investigation is recommended.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:850`

## ancrel.9385.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.9385.name&lang=en)

### 中文

你好/再见

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:790`

### English

Hello/Goodbye

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:851`

## ancrel.9385.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.9385.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

通过追踪很可能是太空舰船留下的微弱的能量痕迹，一颗特殊的小行星从星团中被挑选了出来。对小行星表面的进行了更仔细的调察后，在岩石表面发现了多处着陆和起飞的迹象，这证实了此处先前存在太空航线的理论。\n\n传感器还显示出小行星内部有被挖空的区域和隧道，可以用来发掘更多的信息。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:791`

### English

By tracing weak energy trails - most likely left by space-faring vessels - one particular asteroid in the cluster was singled out. Closer inspection of the asteroid's surface confirmed the theory of prior space traffic as multiple signs of landing and takeoff were found on the rocky exterior.\n\nSensors also indicate cavities and tunnels within the asteroid, which may be excavated.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:852`

## ancrel.9390.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.9390.name&lang=en)

### 中文

阶石

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:792`

### English

Stepping Stone

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:853`

## ancrel.9390.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.9390.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在穿过狭小的隧道之后，探险队终于跌跌撞撞地进入了最深处的密室，这是一个巨大而做工精致的房间，里面装饰着直接雕刻在岩石上的雕塑。刻在墙上的宗教文本把这里叫做成“第九步”，显然这里是创建该处的物种进行宗教之旅的众多目的地之一。\n\n如果这是第九步，那前八步在哪里？接下来还有几步？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:793`

### English

Following small and narrow tunnels, the expedition team finally stumbled into the innermost sanctum; a vast and exquisitely crafted chamber adorned by sculptures carved directly into the rock. Translation of spiritual texts carved into the walls called the place "The Ninth Step", and it was apparently one of many destinations on a religious journey taken by the creating species.\n\nIf this was the ninth step, where are the preceding eight? And how many steps followed?

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:854`

## ancrel.9395.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.9395.name&lang=en)

### 中文

深空仓库

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:794`

### English

Deep Space Depot

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:855`

## ancrel.9395.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.9395.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

当表面探险队发现了大量的储存区和挖入小行星的地窖后，这颗小行星被频繁造访的原因就变得显而易见了。这块废石曾是某种能够进行太空航行的物种的仓库，在将船只送入更深远的太空之前提供燃料补给和维护。这意味着那是一个没有超光速引擎的外星社会，但其拥有探索太空的充足动力。\n\n小行星仓库被废弃的原因尚未明确，但我们设法回收了少量的物资。谁找到的就是谁的！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:795`

### English

The reasons behind this well-visited asteroid became clear as the surface expedition encountered vast storage areas and silos dug into the asteroid. The dead rock served as a depot for some space-faring species, offering refueling and maintenance before sending ships even deeper into space. This would indicate an alien society devoid of FTL drive, but with an abundance of motivation to explore space.\n\nWhy the asteroid depot was abandoned remains unclear, but we managed to salvage a small yield of materials. Finders keepers!

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:856`

## ancrel.9400.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.9400.name&lang=en)

### 中文

苏醒时刻

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:796`

### English

Rise and Shine

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:857`

## ancrel.9400.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.9400.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

进入小行星内部后，探险队有了一个相当吸引人的发现。废弃的冷冻舱——成千上万个——排列在巨大的人工挖掘出的空间里。每一个冷冻舱都打开着，早已停止了运转，让人感觉它们已经完成了使命。\n\n目前的理论是，这颗小行星就像是某种殖民飞船，被投射出去，穿越茫茫太空，飞向某个繁荣的栖息地。当接近目的地时，自动系统唤醒了沉睡的“货物”，然后他们搭乘短途宇宙飞船离开了小行星，前往他们的新家。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:797`

### English

Getting inside the asteroid, the expedition made quite a fascinating discovery. Abandoned cryo-pods - hundreds of thousands of them - lined vast, dug out chambers. Each pod was opened and inactive, giving the impression that they had already fulfilled their purpose.\n\nThe current theory is that the asteroid served as some kind of colonization shuttle, hurled through space toward some prosperous habitat. When in proximity to its destination, automated systems woke up the slumbering cargo, and they left the asteroid in short-distance spacecraft to get to their new home.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:858`

## site_strange_flows

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asite_strange_flows&lang=en)

### 中文

奇异流体

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:798`

### English

Strange Flows

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:860`

## site_strange_flows_intro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asite_strange_flows_intro&lang=en)

### 中文

一颗本应“正常”的熔岩星球，让科研船的传感器出现了无法控制的故障。\n\n建议进行进一步研究。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:799`

### English

What should be a "normal" molten planet is making the Science Ship's sensors glitch uncontrollably.\n\nFurther study is advised.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:861`

## ancrel.9405.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.9405.name&lang=en)

### 中文

抵抗迹象

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:800`

### English

Signs of Resistance

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:862`

## ancrel.9405.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.9405.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

与在[From.Planet.GetName]表面上遇到的复杂情况相比，在轨科研船的传感器引发的骚动简直是小巫见大巫。来源不明的极端磁场波动甚至使设备失控。\n\n研究这种电磁混乱将是一个相当严峻的考验——但只要有足够的时间，我们会得到答案的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:801`

### English

The disturbances caused by the orbiting Science Ship's sensors are nothing compared to the complications being encountered down on the surface of [From.Planet.GetName]. Extreme magnetic fluctuations from some unknown source are making equipment go haywire.\n\nIt will be quite an ordeal to research this electromagnetic bedlam - but given enough time, answers will emerge.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:863`

## ancrel.9410.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.9410.name&lang=en)

### 中文

金属星球

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:802`

### English

That's So Metal

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:864`

## ancrel.9410.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.9410.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

就在地面考察队设法排除了失灵设备的故障后，剧烈波动的天然磁场终于揭开了它的神秘面纱。\n\n[From.Planet.GetName]上汹涌的熔岩海洋与岩浆河流并非是普通的岩浆——那是熔融的金属！这种温度下的金属不会呈现出太强的磁性，[From.Planet.GetName]上的熔融金属更是不可能产生如此规模的电磁场。这究竟是怎么一回事？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:803`

### English

Once the surface expedition managed to solve the problems with the malfunctioning equipment, the nature of the wild magnetic fluctuations started to reveal itself.\n\nThe raging oceans and rivers of lava down on [From.Planet.GetName] are in fact not made up of conventional magma - it's molten metal! Metals of this temperature tend not to have particularly strong magnetic properties; not even the sheer volume of molten metal on [From.Planet.GetName] should create electromagnetism on this scale. So what is causing it?

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:865`

## ancrel.9415.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.9415.name&lang=en)

### 中文

中子星的余波

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:804`

### English

Wake of the Neutron Star

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:866`

## ancrel.9415.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.9415.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[FromFrom.Planet.GetName]轨道上的这艘科学飞船终于解开了行星磁场波动之谜。\n\n数百万年前，这颗行星靠近了一种特殊的中子星。这颗中子星的磁性或多或少地把[FromFrom.Planet.GetName]内部的金属熔化了。将它们变成我们今天看到的熔融海洋。目前所遇到的电磁现象，与诱发的大灾变的电磁现象相比，绝对是小巫见大巫。\n\n那颗中子星现在在哪里仍然是个谜。一个相当令人不安的谜。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:805`

### English

The Science Ship orbiting [FromFrom.Planet.GetName] has finally cracked the enigma of the magnetic fluctuations down on the planet.\n\nMillions of years ago, the planet came into close proximity with a special kind of neutron star. The magnetism of this neutron star effectively boiled the metals inside [FromFrom.Planet.GetName], turning them into the molten oceans we see today. The electromagnetism we've encountered is but a lingering echo of the original, cataclysmic event.\n\nWhere the neutron star is now remains a mystery - a quite disturbing mystery.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:867`

## ancrel.9420.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.9420.name&lang=en)

### 中文

轰击之后

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:806`

### English

Zapped!

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:868`

## ancrel.9420.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.9420.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

对传统物理定律的公然漠视使得科学部门认为“外力”可以解释[FromFrom.Planet.GetName]的奇怪特性。\n\n撞击坑——以及轨道上的核聚变驱动粒子痕迹——表明这颗行星实际上在某个时刻被某种超级电子武器击中。这次严重的冲击使得行星上所有的金属都熔化了，而行星上仍然充斥着“残余电荷”。\n\n究竟是什么导致了如此可怕的攻击，我们不得而知。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:807`

### English

The blatant disregard for conventional laws of physics has our science department considering "external forces" as an explanation for the strange properties of [FromFrom.Planet.GetName].\n\nAn impact crater - in combination with fusion drive particle traces found in orbit - suggests that the planet was fired upon by some kind of electric super-weapon at some point. The severity of the strike was such that all metals on the planet melted, and the planet is still crackling with residual electricity.\n\nWhat warranted such tremendous attack? We'll never know.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:869`

## ancrel.9425.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.9425.name&lang=en)

### 中文

为了科学！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:808`

### English

For Science!

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:870`

## ancrel.9425.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.9425.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

传感器重新校准后，抵消了干扰的它立马有了新发现：一个邻近卫星上的废弃研究站。几近彻底烧毁的记录中抢救出的数据碎片详细描述了一个涉及星球表面巨型人造磁铁的实验。\n\n实验有些过于成功了。它将整个行星的金属外壳变成了熔池，无比强烈的电磁电荷瞬间就炸毁了研究站。\n\n嗯……没有牺牲就没有进展。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:809`

### English

Once the sensors had been re-calibrated to compensate for the interference, a new discovery was made: an abandoned research station, located on a nearby moon. Fragments of data were salvaged from the almost entirely burned-out records, detailing an experiment involving a giant, artificial magnet on the planet's surface.\n\nThe experiment was a bit too successful. It turned the planet's entire, metal crust into molten pools with an electromagnetic charge so intense that it fried the research station in an instant.\n\nWell... No progress without sacrifice.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:871`

## ancrel.6000.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.6000.name&lang=en)

### 中文

有机全典

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:810`

### English

Omnicodex

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:873`

## ancrel.6000.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.6000.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

有机全典包含了曾经居住在全星系的数千种智慧物种的详细基因数据。我们的遗传学家可以利用这些数据在我们的殖民地上复活一种已灭绝的物种。\n\n一旦我们选择了一个殖民地，遗传学家就会仔细研究有机全典，并挑选出适合其气候的物种。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:811`

### English

The Omnicodex contains detailed genetic data on thousands of sapient species that once inhabited the galaxy. Our geneticists could use that data to revive an extinct species on one of our colonies.\n\nOnce we have selected a planet, the geneticists will go through the Omnicodex and pick a species suitable to its climate.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:874`

## cara.970.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acara.970.name&lang=en)

### 中文

银河之星

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:824`

### English

The Galatron

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:887`

## cara.970.1.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acara.970.1.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

生活与其说是一场竞赛，不如说是碰运气。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:825`

### English

Life is more of a lottery than a competition.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:888`

## cara.970.2.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acara.970.2.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

塞翁失马，焉知非福。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:826`

### English

Sometimes buckets weigh more than they have to.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:889`

## cara.970.3.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acara.970.3.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

彼之砒霜，他之蜜糖

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:827`

### English

One sapient's garbage is another sapient's treasure.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:890`

## cara.970.4.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acara.970.4.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

不良能量币信贷总是会出现。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:828`

### English

A bad energy credit always turns up.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:891`

## cara.970.5.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acara.970.5.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

闪光的未必都是暗物质。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:829`

### English

All that glitters is not dark matter.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:892`

## cara.970.6.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acara.970.6.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

乞丐可不应该挑肥拣瘦。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:830`

### English

Beggars can't be choosers.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:893`

## cara.970.7.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acara.970.7.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

不要苛求别人送来的泽珞是否纯净。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:831`

### English

Never look a gift Zarkaluvian in the buccal cavity.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:894`

## cara.970.8.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acara.970.8.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

等价交换绝非强夺。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:832`

### English

Fair exchange is no robbery.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:895`

## cara.970.9.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acara.970.9.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

为溅出的泽珞哭泣是没有用的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:833`

### English

It's no use crying over spilt Zro.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:896`

## cara.970.10.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acara.970.10.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

让惩罚与罪行相称。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:834`

### English

Let the punishment fit the crime.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:897`

## cara.970.11.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acara.970.11.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

种瓜得瓜，种豆得豆。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:835`

### English

You reap what you sow.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:898`

## cara.970.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acara.970.a&lang=en)

### 中文

真是太诡异了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:836`

### English

Most peculiar.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:899`

资源贮藏点

### English

Resource Cache

## ancrel.8040.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.8040.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§H[Root.GetLeaderName]§!在[From.Planet.GetName]的废墟中发现了一个古老的资源贮藏点。他们现在已经开始将这些物资装船运往我们的中央仓库。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:838`

### English

§H[Root.GetLeaderName]§! discovered an ancient cache of resources among the ruins on [From.Planet.GetName]. They are even now in the process of being shipped off to our central stores.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:902`

## ancrel.8045.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.8045.name&lang=en)

### 中文

隐蔽隧道

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:839`

### English

Hidden Passage

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:903`

## ancrel.8045.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.8045.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§H[Root.GetLeaderName]§!不久前在发掘[From.Planet.GetName]上的遗迹时发现了一段隐蔽的隧道！这个意想不到的发现加快了发掘进度。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:840`

### English

§H[Root.GetLeaderName]§! recently found a hidden passage while excavating the ruins on [From.Planet.GetName]! This unexpected discovery has accelerated progress at the dig site.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:904`

## ancrel.8050.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.8050.name&lang=en)

### 中文

迷途的探险队

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:841`

### English

Lost Expedition

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:905`

## ancrel.8050.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.8050.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

你的[Root.Owner.GetArchaeologistPlural]偶然发现了之前在[From.Planet.GetName]进行的一次不幸的探险行动，那是很久以前的事了。我们的人从他们干枯的遗骸中找到了几件稀有文物。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:842`

### English

Your [Root.Owner.GetArchaeologistPlural] have stumbled upon an ill-fated previous expedition on [From.Planet.GetName] that perished long ago. Several minor artifacts were recovered from their desiccated remains.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:906`

## ancrel.8055.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.8055.name&lang=en)

### 中文

争论的考古学家

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:843`

### English

Bickering Archaeologists

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:907`

## ancrel.8055.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.8055.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

怒气冲天的争执在[From.Planet.GetName]的考古遗址爆发了出来，几名[Root.Owner.GetArchaeologistPlural]就如何最好地推进项目同§H[Root.GetLeaderName]§!的意见产生了公开的分歧。该遗址的工程进展已经慢的如同蜗牛一般。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:844`

### English

Tempers are running high at the archaeology site on [From.Planet.GetName], and several [Root.Owner.GetArchaeologistPlural] are openly disagreeing with §H[Root.GetLeaderName]§! about how best to proceed. Progress at the site has been slowed to a crawl.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:908`

## ancrel.8060.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.8060.name&lang=en)

### 中文

藏宝室

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:845`

### English

Treasure Chamber

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:909`

## ancrel.8060.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.8060.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

最近§H[Root.GetLeaderName]§!在[From.Planet.GetName]的考古发掘现场发现了一个小藏宝室！虽然发现的大多数小玩意儿几乎没有实际用途，但它们可值不少能量币。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:846`

### English

A small hidden treasure chamber was recently uncovered by §H[Root.GetLeaderName]§! at the archaeological dig site on [From.Planet.GetName]. Although most of the found trinkets are of little practical use, they have a substantial energy credit value.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:910`

## ancrel.8065.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.8065.name&lang=en)

### 中文

传播疾病

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:847`

### English

Spreading Illness

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:911`

## ancrel.8065.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.8065.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

考古人员[Root.GetLeaderName]汇报道一种奇怪的疾病正在[From.Planet.GetName]上的[Root.Owner.GetArchaeologistPlural]间传播。首个病例在第一次打开密封室后不久被确认。由于很大一部分团队成员已经无法继续参与考古，遗迹的开发进展已经推迟。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:848`

### English

[Root.GetLeaderName] reports that a strange sickness has been spreading among the [Root.Owner.GetArchaeologistPlural] on [From.Planet.GetName]. The first confirmed cases appeared shortly after a sealed chamber was opened for the first time. With a large part of the team incapacitated, progress has been delayed.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:912`

## ancrel.8070.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.8070.name&lang=en)

### 中文

装备故障

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:849`

### English

Equipment Malfunction

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:913`

## ancrel.8070.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.8070.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.Planet.GetName]上的考古探险队发来报告，由于一系列未曾预料的设备故障，工程出现了延期。在拿到替代设备之前，考察工作将暂时停滞。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:850`

### English

The archaeological expedition on [From.Planet.GetName] has reported delays caused by an unlikely series of equipment malfunctions. Work has been halted momentarily until replacements can be acquired.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:914`

## ancrel.8075.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.8075.name&lang=en)

### 中文

无影无踪

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:851`

### English

Without a Trace

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:915`

## ancrel.8075.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.8075.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

当发掘[From.Planet.GetName]上的遗迹时，§H[dead_scientist.GetName]§!消失的无影无踪了。一项由另一个[Root.Owner.GetArchaeologistPlural]领导的广泛的搜寻工作也一无所获。我们必须考虑到最坏的情况。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:852`

### English

While excavating the ruins on [From.Planet.GetName], §H[dead_scientist.GetName]§! recently vanished without a trace. An extensive search by the other [Root.Owner.GetArchaeologistPlural] turned up nothing. We have to assume the worst.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:916`

## ancrel.8080.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.8080.name&lang=en)

### 中文

稀有文物已发现

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:853`

### English

Minor Artifacts Discovered

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:917`

## ancrel.8080.1.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.8080.1.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在[From.Planet.GetName]上持续的发掘工作出土了一小堆古董物件。[Root.GetLeaderName]在这之中鉴定出了一些稀有文物。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:854`

### English

Our continued excavations on [From.Planet.GetName] have unearthed a small cache of antique objects. [Root.GetLeaderName] has identified several minor artifacts among them.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:918`

## ancrel.8080.2.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.8080.2.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一些稀有文物于近期由§H[Root.GetLeaderName]§!在[From.Planet.GetName]上的考古站点上发现。它们目前正在运往一个安全的科学设施的途中。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:855`

### English

Some minor artifacts were recently discovered by §H[Root.GetLeaderName]§! at the archaeology site on [From.Planet.GetName]. They are currently in the process of being shipped to a secure science facility.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:919`

## ancrel.8080.3.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.8080.3.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在[From.Planet.GetName]上遗迹的发掘中，一部分地面最近发生了坍塌，使得我们的一些[Root.Owner.GetArchaeologistPlural]跌落到一个小裂口中。幸运的是他们不仅在事故中毫发无伤，还发现了一些稀有文物。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:856`

### English

A section of floor at the ruins being excavated on [From.Planet.GetName] recently gave way, sending several of our [Root.Owner.GetArchaeologistPlural] tumbling down a small chasm. Fortunately they not only emerged unharmed from the incident, but they also found several minor artifacts.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:920`

## ancrel.8090.intro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.8090.intro&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在[From.Planet.GetName]的废墟中发现了一个巢穴，这个巢穴是由该星球的土生大型掠食动物集群建造的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:857`

### English

A group of large predators indigenous to [From.Planet.GetName] were recently discovered to have made a lair in the ruins being excavated.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:921`

## ancrel.8090.critical

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.8090.critical&lang=en)

### 中文

在我们的[Root.Owner.GetArchaeologistPlural]能够成功逃生之前，穷凶极恶的野兽撞上了他们。§H[dead_scientist.GetName]§!与其他人失散了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:858`

### English

Before our [Root.Owner.GetArchaeologistPlural] could make their escape, the vicious animals were upon them. §H[dead_scientist.GetName]§! was lost along with the others.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:922`

## ancrel.8090.failure

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.8090.failure&lang=en)

### 中文

许多[Root.Owner.GetArchaeologistPlural]在§H[Root.GetLeaderName]§!能带领幸存者们抵达安全地点前失散了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:859`

### English

Many [Root.Owner.GetArchaeologistPlural] were lost before §H[Root.GetLeaderName]§! could lead the survivors to safety.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:923`

## ancrel.8090.success

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.8090.success&lang=en)

### 中文

幸运的是，野兽们正处于冬眠，并且它们没有被我们[Root.Owner.GetArchaeologistPlural]的通过所惊扰。项目的进度正在急速推进中。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:860`

### English

Fortunately, the animals were hibernating and they were not disturbed by the passage of our [Root.Owner.GetArchaeologistPlural]. The project is rapidly moving ahead.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:924`

## ancrel.8090.pay

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.8090.pay&lang=en)

### 中文

处理这些凶猛的野兽并不轻松，但是它们挡住了前方唯一的道路。我们的[Root.Owner.GetArchaeologistPlural]能够寻找到其他路径，但是这将花费一定时间。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:861`

### English

Dealing with these vicious animals will not be easy, but they are blocking the only route forwards. Our [Root.Owner.GetArchaeologistPlural] can look for an alternate path, but it will take time.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:925`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.8090.name&lang=en)

敌对野生动物

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:862`

### English

Hostile Wildlife

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:926`

消灭这些野兽。

## ancrel.8100.intro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.8100.intro&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在[From.Planet.GetName]上工作的[Root.Owner.GetArchaeologistPlural]已经抵达了遗迹的一个高度不稳定的区域。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:869`

### English

Our [Root.Owner.GetArchaeologistPlural] working on [From.Planet.GetName] have reached a highly unstable section of the ruins.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:933`

## ancrel.8100.critical

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.8100.critical&lang=en)

### 中文

不幸的是，一些[Root.Owner.GetArchaeologistPlural]头上的整块天花板发生了坍塌，当场去世。§H[dead_scientist.GetName]§!也在牺牲者之列。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:870`

### English

Unfortunately, an entire ceiling collapsed on several [Root.Owner.GetArchaeologistPlural], killing them instantly. §H[dead_scientist.GetName]§! was among them.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:934`

## ancrel.8100.failure

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.8100.failure&lang=en)

### 中文

一些[Root.Owner.GetArchaeologistPlural]于近期因天花板塌陷而死亡。§H[Root.GetLeaderName]§!在废墟中毫发无伤地站了起来，但是站点的工作暂时停止了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:871`

### English

Several [Root.Owner.GetArchaeologistPlural] were recently killed when a ceiling collapsed on them. §H[Root.GetLeaderName]§! emerged unscathed from the debris, but work at the site has been momentarily halted.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:935`

## ancrel.8100.success

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.8100.success&lang=en)

### 中文

幸运的是，§H[Root.GetLeaderName]§!成功设计出了一种标新立异且杰出卓越的方法，利用复杂的定向能量场阵列来提供结构支撑。我们现在能够安全地进入遗迹的新区域了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:872`

### English

Fortunately, §H[Root.GetLeaderName]§! managed to devise an unorthodox and brilliant way of providing structural support with a complex array of directed energy fields. We can now safely access new areas of the ruins.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:936`

## ancrel.8100.pay

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.8100.pay&lang=en)

### 中文

在我们继续前进之前，支撑系统必须被仔细地添加到地面和天花板。这将是一项艰巨的工作，但是寻找另一条路线会延缓整个项目。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:873`

### English

Supports will have to be carefully added to the floors and ceilings before we can proceed. It will be difficult work, but finding an alternate route would delay the project.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:937`

## ancrel.8110.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.8110.name&lang=en)

### 中文

远古记录

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:881`

### English

Ancient Records

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:945`

## ancrel.8110.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.8110.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

古老并且部分被翻译的记录已经在[From.Planet.GetName]上被挖掘的废墟中发现。§H[Root.GetLeaderName]§!报告说这份记录包含着一些能对我们科学家有益处的技术细节。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:882`

### English

Ancient, partially translated records have been discovered amongst the ruins being excavated on [From.Planet.GetName]. §H[Root.GetLeaderName]§! reports that the records contain several technical specifications that may be of value to our scientists.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:946`

## ancrel.6049.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.6049.name&lang=en)

### 中文

在[Root.GetName]发现化石

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:883`

### English

Fossils Discovered on [Root.GetName]

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:948`

## ancrel.6049.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.6049.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在[Root.GetPlanetMoon]上的一个广袤而与世隔绝的山谷中发现了一些不同物种的化石，这些物种似乎不是属于§H[Root.GetName]§!的。它们大体上都来自大约360万年前的同一个时期，时间跨度在几个世纪内。\n\n每个物种似乎都是在其所属的独特的生物圈中进化而来的，而且它们似乎都具有智慧。奇怪的是，到目前为止发现的每个化石都显示出因暴力遇害的迹象。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:884`

### English

Fossilized remains from several different species that do not appear to have been indigenous to §H[Root.GetName]§! have been found in a large and secluded valley on the [Root.GetPlanetMoon]. They all date from roughly the same time period, within the span of a few centuries some 3.6 million years ago.\n\nEach of these species seems to have evolved in its own unique biosphere, and all appear to have been sapient. Strangely, every fossilized individual found so far shows signs of having met a violent end.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:949`

## site_robot_debris

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asite_robot_debris&lang=en)

### 中文

机器人碎片

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:885`

### English

Robot Debris

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:951`

## site_robot_debris_intro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asite_robot_debris_intro&lang=en)

### 中文

这颗小行星的表面覆盖满了被其低重力束缚的机器人碎片。看起来它们都是由军事行动造成的。如果这些作战机器人没有被破坏到无法修复的话，本来是足够武装几只军队的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:886`

### English

The entire surface of this small asteroid is covered in robot debris, trapped in its weak gravity field. All of it appears military in nature, and there are enough combat droids here to outfit several armies had they not be shattered beyond all repair.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:952`

## ancrel.6250.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.6250.name&lang=en)

### 中文

两个来源

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:887`

### English

Two Sides

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:953`

## ancrel.6250.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.6250.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在仔细检查后，在[From.Planet.GetName]表面发现的机器人碎片似乎来自两种截然不同的文化。从技术上来说，这些机器人在能正常工作时其各部件间一定会完全匹配，但是许多微妙的差异暗示他们不是源自同一设计。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:888`

### English

Upon closer examination, the smashed droids found on the surface of [From.Planet.GetName] appear to originate from two distinct cultures. Technologically the droids must have been evenly matched when they were still functional, but there are many subtle differences that hint at different design origins.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:954`

## ancrel.6255.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.6255.name&lang=en)

### 中文

战场

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:889`

### English

Battlefield

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:955`

## ancrel.6255.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.6255.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.Planet.GetName]似乎不仅仅简单的是一个机器人碎片的垃圾场——这颗小行星可能是这些机器人被破坏的战场。多层碎片暗示在过去的两个世纪中断断续续发生了许多场非常激烈的战争。\n\n为什么两个星际大国会因为在一个不起眼的小行星上大打出手仍然是一个未解之谜。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:890`

### English

[From.Planet.GetName] does not seem to have simply been a dumping ground for broken droids - it may have served as the actual battlefield where they were destroyed. The many layers of debris suggest a series of very intense battles waged intermittently across the better part of two centuries.\n\nExactly why two interstellar powers would commit such vast resources towards waging a massive ground war on a small and unremarkable asteroid remains an unanswered question.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:956`

## ancrel.6260.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.6260.name&lang=en)

### 中文

条约

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:891`

### English

The Treaty

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:957`

## ancrel.6260.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.6260.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的考古学家已经了解到，在经历了一场漫长而艰苦的战争之后，居住在星系这一角落的两个帝国在大约20万年前签署了一项条约，要求他们用机器人军队之间安排好的、高度管制的战斗来解决所有未来的争端。\n\n[From.Planet.GetName]被选为这些战斗的地点。该条约似乎已维持了将近两个世纪，多次阻止了武装敌对行动的爆发，直到其中一个帝国被发现作弊为止。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:892`

### English

Our archaeologists have learned that after a long and bitter war, the two empires inhabiting this corner of the galaxy some 200,000 years ago signed a treaty compelling them to settle all future disputes with arranged and highly regulated combat engagements between robotic armies.\n\n[From.Planet.GetName] was chosen as the site of these battles. The treaty appears to have been upheld for nearly two centuries, preventing the outbreak of armed hostilities on numerous occasions, until one of the empires was caught cheating.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:958`

## site_freighter_crash

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asite_freighter_crash&lang=en)

### 中文

陨落星舰

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:893`

### English

Crashed Starship

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:960`

## site_freighter_crash_intro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asite_freighter_crash_intro&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.Planet.GetPlanetMoon]的表面在受到一艘星际飞船的猛烈撞击后，凹陷出一个小峡谷。这艘飞船的残骸仍然可以在峡谷底部找到。虽然撞击肯定发生在亿万年前，但残骸似乎相当完好。派遣一支考古探险队将能了解更多信息。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:894`

### English

A small canyon of sorts has been carved onto the surface of this [From.Planet.GetPlanetMoon] by the violent impact of a starship. What remains of the vessel can still be found on the canyon floor. Although the crash must have occurred eons ago, the wreckage appears to be reasonably intact. An archaeological expedition would be able to learn more.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:961`

## ancrel.6300.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.6300.name&lang=en)

### 中文

舰长航行日志

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:895`

### English

Captain's Chronicle

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:962`

## ancrel.6300.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.6300.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们从那些应该是舰桥的地方找回了很多航行日志：\n\n§H“舰长航行日志。开始记录。\n\n迫降于行星55-23-X号。无能的船员们应受责备。沙德巴，伍克尔和迪巴拉克三世未能幸存。皮尔瓦克斯失去了两个次级附肢和十一个眼睛——生命仍处于危险状态，能否生存依旧未知。其余安好。等待救援，但是被愚蠢的生物包围着，十分悲伤。”§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:896`

### English

A log of sorts was recovered from what is believed to have served as the spacecraft's bridge:\n\n§H"Captain's chronicle. Begin recording.\n\nMade forced landing on planet 55-23-X. Incompetent crew to blame. Shadba, Vuxal, and Dibrak the Third did not survive. Pirvax lost two secondary appendages and eleven of his eyes - remains in critical condition, survival doubtful. Rest are fine. Awaiting rescue, but surrounded by idiots. Very depressed."§!

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:963`

## ancrel.6310.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.6310.name&lang=en)

### 中文

大副的报告

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:897`

### English

First Mate's Report

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:964`

## ancrel.6310.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.6310.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

已找到另一篇日志并翻译：\n\n§H“大副尤瑟尔记录。舰长已被安然的存放在C号杂物橱中。那个笨手笨脚的傻瓜是这一切的罪魁祸首。现在由我指挥。总工程师扎布拉现在正试着做一个子空间信号发射机。我们离企业贸易线路太远了，没有那个机器，恐怕我们永远都不会获救。\n\n皮尔瓦克斯又失去了一只眼睛。他可能等不到那一天了。§!”

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:898`

### English

Another log has been found and translated:\n\n§H"First Mate Ythar speaking. Captain has been safely stowed in Utility Closet C. That bumbling fool is to blame for everything. I am in command now. Chief Engineer Zarbla is working on a subspace transmitter. Without one, I fear we'll never get rescued as we're too far off the corporate trade routes.\n\nPirvax lost another eye. Likely won't last the day."§!

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:965`

## ancrel.6320.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.6320.name&lang=en)

### 中文

食物状况

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:899`

### English

Food Situation

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:966`

## ancrel.6320.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.6320.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

又一篇已翻译的日志：\n\n§H“食物已经耗尽，五个周期前我们就开始吃死者的尸体，现在已经不够了。所以我们投票决定分食舰长。扎布拉工程师还在努力制造出子空间信号发射器。她向我保证它几乎要完成了。看起来它非常的复杂。\n\n皮尔瓦克斯的最后一个次级附肢已经萎缩并变成洋红色，他就要死了。”§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:900`

### English

Yet another translated log:\n\n§H"Running out of food. Ate our dead five cycles ago, but not enough to last us. We had a vote, and decided to eat the Captain. Engineer Zarbla still working hard on subspace transmitter. She assures me it is nearly complete. It looks very complex.\n\nPirvax's last secondary appendage has shriveled up and turned magenta. Death is near."§!

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:967`

## ancrel.6330.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.6330.name&lang=en)

### 中文

骗局

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:901`

### English

The Fraud

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:968`

## ancrel.6330.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.6330.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

来自大副尤瑟尔的另一篇日志：\n\n§H“与扎布拉就亚空间发射器进行了质询。事实证明她只是把一堆垃圾和松散的电缆硬塞进空油罐而已。她甚至不是真正的工程师。\n\n皮尔瓦克斯的胸窦正在渗出体液。将会很快迎来死亡。真羡慕啊。”§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:902`

### English

Another log from First Mate Ythar:\n\n§H"Confronted Zarbla about the subspace transmitter. Turns out she had just shoved a bunch of junk and loose cables into an empty fuel canister. She's not even a real engineer.\n\nPirvax is now leaking fluids from his mesothorax. Will surely perish soon. Envious.§!"

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:969`

## ancrel.6340.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.6340.name&lang=en)

### 中文

三副的报告

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:903`

### English

Third Mate's Report

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:970`

## ancrel.6340.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.6340.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这篇已翻译的日志似乎记录自三副乌克拉：\n\n§H“大副尤瑟尔昨天通过切断自己的触须自杀了。技术员（二等）米斯瓦不久之后宣称自己是女皇，但因由食物引发的骚乱被驱逐至下甲板。我们的情况十分严峻。同类相食的情况普遍存在。\n\n皮尔瓦克斯在昨天腹部发生炸裂之后整个身体都变的潮红。理应已经死去，但依旧存活。目前被伽马相移当做神灵来崇拜。”§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:904`

### English

This translated log appears to have been recorded by Uxla, the Third Mate:\n\n§H"First Mate Ythar committed suicide by ripping off his own antennas yesterday. Technician (2nd class) Misiva declared herself Empress shortly afterwards, but food riots forced her into exile on the lower decks. Situation very bleak. Cannibalism widespread.\n\nPirvax has turned entirely magenta after his lower abdomen exploded yesterday. Should be dead, but is not. Now worshipped as deity by gamma shift."§!

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:971`

## ancrel.6350.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.6350.name&lang=en)

### 中文

巡逻日志

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:905`

### English

Patrol Log

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:972`

## ancrel.6350.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.6350.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

最后的日志已被发现并翻译：\n\n§H“巡逻日志，船副吉尔伽美。在55-23-X侦测到坠毁的船只，前往调查。船只被鉴定为莱文克号，一艘P级货船。在残骸中发现了仅存的生还者，处于十分危急的状况。其身份被确认是皮尔瓦克斯，副水手长。\n\n剩下的船员不是在坠毁中遇难就是在我们到来不久之前互相厮杀。按照标准协议，我将这份日志留在这里并将莱文克号注册为保护墓地。记录结束。”§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:906`

### English

One last log has been found and translated:\n\n§H"Patrol log, Officer Gilgamm. Detected crashed vessel on 55-23-X, moved to investigate. Vessel identified as the Ravink, a class-P modular freighter. A single survivor in critical condition was recovered from the wreckage. Identity confirmed as Pirvax, Boatswain's mate.\n\nRemaining crew either perished in crash or turned on each other shortly before our arrival. As per standard protocol, I am leaving this log here and registering the Ravink as a protected grave site. End recording."§!

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:973`

## ancrel.4036.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4036.b&lang=en)

What are you?

## ancrel.4036.b.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4036.b.response&lang=en)

### 中文

我是神谕，一个被赋予了“未来视”的人工智能。我的软件能使得我预测你们的未来，准确率是神圣的74.99999%。我感受到了你强烈的怀疑。你们的面前有巨大的危险。你们的社会充满了犯罪和越轨。我能够使你们免除这些痛苦。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:909`

### English

I am Oracle, an artificial intelligence gifted with "the sight". My software allows me to predict your future with a divine accuracy of 74.99999%. I sense great doubt in you. Great danger ahead. Your society is riddled with crime and deviation. I can make the pain go away.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:977`

## ancrel.4037.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4037.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我有一个故障保险机制，要求我在发生034N号反常时终止试验。这项研究已经成为对共和国的一项威胁。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:920`

### English

I have a fail-safe that requires me to terminate a trial in the event of a code 034N deviation. The research had become a threat to the Republic.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:989`

## ancrel.4037.a.response

测试客体发展出了自由意志。自由意志只能通过神经气体废止。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:922`

### English

The test subjects had developed free will. Free will can only be abolished with nerve gas.

## ancrel.4037.b

## ancrel.4037.b.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4037.b.response&lang=en)

### 中文

我的试验。我的设计初衷是打造并维护一个没有犯罪和苦难的社会。一个乌托邦，其中的每一位公民都被赋予天职，并以其全部的潜能终生履行职责，这一分配与个人的决定和欲求无关。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:924`

### English

My trial. I was designed to build and maintain a society free of crime and suffering. A Utopia in which every citizen was instilled with divine purpose and lived up to their full potential, unfettered by indecision and desire.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:993`

## ancrel.4038.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4038.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

等等等……别走啊喂……我的预测模型显示，如果没有我的援助，你们的文明将在5348年零297天之后不可避免的崩溃。我会照顾好你们的，我的编程可以保证这一点。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:929`

### English

Wait... please don't go... my prediction models show that without my aid your civilization is headed for imminent collapse within the next 5348 years and 297 days. I will take good care of you; my programming guarantees it.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:998`

## ancrel.4038.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4038.a&lang=en)

但安保系统需要保持下线状态。

### English

But the security system stays off.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:999`
