### English

Our models predict your bright future as a toaster. Dismantle her!

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4039.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我很乐意优化你的殖民地。我在过去的几千年里一直非常孤独。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:936`

### English

I shall enjoy optimizing your colonies. It has been dreadfully lonely these past millennia.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1006`

## endless_expanse_digsite

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aendless_expanse_digsite&lang=en)

### 中文

无边无垠

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:939`

### English

The Endless Expanse

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1009`

## endless_expanse_digsite_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aendless_expanse_digsite_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

乍一看，[From.Planet.GetName]上只有冰雪。雪白的大地、玻璃般晶莹的湖泊和锯齿状的松绿色冰川广袤无垠，火红的橘色天空映衬着地面，宛如裂开的伤口，将[From.Planet.Solar_System.GetName]中夕阳西下的红色光芒倾注在海面。如果世界有终结，那这里一定就是了。海面风平浪静，积雪一尘不染，唯一能听到的，只有冰层崩裂的咔嚓雷动和平原上呼啸的狂风。\n\n然而，[From.Planet.GetName]曾经是某族的家园。在一片广阔而又贫瘠的旷野之中，一座黑色的金属尖塔从苍白的大地上突兀而起。积雪之下，究竟隐藏了怎样的被遗忘的秘密?

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:940`

### English

At first sight there is only snow and ice on [From.Planet.GetName]. Vast expanses of impenetrable white, glass lakes and jagged turquoise glaciers against fiery orange skies torn open like a wound, pouring red into the seas as [From.Planet.Solar_System.GetName]'s stars set. If the world had an end this would be it. Here the seas lie still and the snow untouched. The only sounds are the thunder of the ice calving and the wind howling across the plains.\n\nYet, [From.Planet.GetName] was once someone's home. In the midst of a wide barren field the black metal framework of a steeple protrudes from the white masses. What forgotten secrets could be buried beneath the snow?

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1010`

## ancrel.4040.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4040.name&lang=en)

### 中文

教堂现身无垠之地

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:941`

### English

Church of the Endless Expanse

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1011`

## ancrel.4040.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4040.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

考古队在一座中型神庙的断壁残垣之处着陆。神庙的外墙自某种外星有机材料精雕细琢而成，在过去曾有着斑斓的色彩，如今却早已褪成沉闷的灰色。一个功能完备的全系图像祭司依旧驻留在教堂。在神庙处于使用的年代，她听取旅者的忏悔，并提供心灵上的治愈，同时也成为宇宙中环境恶劣之一隅的调解与庇护之地。\n\n形态奇怪的机械告解椅看上去形似拷打装置，这让我们的考古学家坚信，这座神庙暗藏的秘密远在于目光所及之外。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:942`

### English

The archaeological team has laid bare the remains of a modest temple. Delicately carved from an organic alien material the exterior was once brightly colored but has faded to a dull gray. A fully operational holographic priest still presides over the church. While the temple was in use she took confessions from wayfaring penitents offering healing, reconciliation and sanctuary to this otherwise inhospitable nook of the universe.\n\nUnusual mechanical confessional chairs akin to torture devices have led the archaeologists to believe there may be more to the temple than meets the eye.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1012`

## ancrel.4041.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4041.name&lang=en)

### 中文

忘却堂

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:943`

### English

Oblivion

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1013`

## ancrel.4041.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4041.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在圣器收藏室的地砖底下，我们的考古学家发现了一个隐藏的地窖，其墙壁上陈列满了一排又一排的蓝色数字球体，其上的电路在黑暗中闪烁不定。每一个球体都容纳着一次告解的记忆，只要将其放入房间中央的秘密探查器就能浏览其中的内容。\n\n那些前来告解罪行的人们会被赐予忘却之福，和其罪行相关的记忆会由告解椅抽取出来并贮存在数字球体中。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:944`

### English

Hidden under the floor tiles in the sacristy, the archaeologists have discovered a secret crypt where the walls are lined with rows upon rows of tiny blue digital orbs, their circuitry glittering in the dark. Each orb contains the memory of a single confession and can be plugged into a cryptic looking machine at the center of the room to reveal its contents.\n\nThose who confessed their sins were blessed with oblivion, the memories of their deeds extracted by the confessional chairs and stored within the digital orbs.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1014`

## ancrel.4041.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4041.a&lang=en)

### 中文

真是实用。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:945`

### English

How practical.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1015`

## ancrel.4041.b

## ancrel.4042.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4042.name&lang=en)

### 中文

累累罪行

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:948`

### English

Sin

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1018`

## ancrel.4042.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4042.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

记录的罪行可谓见怪不怪。记忆球体里面容纳了各种各样鸡毛蒜皮一样的无聊透顶的罪过；夫妻不忠，穿戴渎神的装束，暴露自己的蝇拍，装饰自己的触须，以及不小心生吞了邻居的宠物、小孩或者配偶……不过，其中一人的忏悔罪迹格外引人注目——那就是祭司本人。\n\n全息祭司，从结果来看，是这个教会的创始人，启明使萨阿里亚的虚拟化身。显然，她遭受了严重的记忆创失，并因为过去所实行的一桩极其邪恶的行动而饱受愧疚心折磨。这位身材娇小的软体动物祭司提取并储存了超过一百段告解，企图明白她过去曾行之恶。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:949`

### English

Sin is unoriginal. The memory orbs contain countless accounts of tedious little transgressions; infidelity, bearing sacrilegious garb, exposing your flappers, adorning your feelers, ingesting your neighbor's pet, child or spouse... The deeds of one penitent alone stand out - those of the priest herself...\n\nThe holo-priest, as it turns out, is a virtual representation of the church's founder, Illuminate Saariah. She appears to have suffered from severe memory loss and was haunted by the conviction that she had committed a heinous act in the past. The tiny molluscoid priest extracted and stored over a hundred of her own confessions in an attempt to figure out what she had done.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1019`

## ancrel.4047.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4047.name&lang=en)

### 中文

遗忘之事无从释怀

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:950`

### English

Forgotten But Not Forgiven

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1020`

## ancrel.4047.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4047.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

启明使萨阿里亚的工作台四处散落着潦草的手稿，里面提及一个叫“埃克斯-柏楞”的地方，以及那个地方她感受到的四散的恐怖气氛。根据她的日记，她曾频繁于理智清醒和记忆丢失的状态之间交替。她写道，如果她没法认出自己的手抄，她会觉得那只是一个疯女人的胡言乱语。看上去，频繁出现的记忆提取导致她的精神状况不断恶化，不过由于没有记录主体什么也说不准。\n\n从她零碎的告解手札中拼凑出一个完整连贯的事件流程对于我们的考古学家来说是毫无希望的任务，正如她自己曾经打算尝试的一样。好在，考古学家们从她的一个记忆球体中发现了可能是埃克斯-柏楞的坐标。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:951`

### English

Illuminate Saariah's work station is littered with obsessive scribbles about a place called Ix Belèn and the horrors she fears transpired there. Judging from her journal she had intermittent moments of clarity followed by complete memory loss. She writes that if she did not recognize her own handwriting she would have thought they were the ramblings of a madwoman. It seems likely that her deteriorating mental health was a side-effect of repeated memory extraction but without a body it is impossible to say.\n\nPiecing together a coherent course of events from her many fragmented confessions has proven as hopeless for the archaeologists as it was for her, but they have uncovered what could be the coordinates to Ix Belèn from one of her many memory orbs.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1021`

设好前往埃克斯-柏楞的航线。

### English

Set course for Ix Belèn.

让漫天飞雪埋葬这个渎圣之地吧。希望这处地方能被很快遗忘。

### English

Let the snow bury this unholy establishment. Pray it will soon be forgotten.

我们可没功夫关注死掉的外星人犯的小罪。

### English

We cannot concern ourselves with the petty crimes of dead aliens.

## endless_expanse_digsite_2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aendless_expanse_digsite_2&lang=en)

### 中文

埃克斯-柏楞

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:956`

### English

Ix Belèn

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1027`

## endless_expanse_digsite_2_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aendless_expanse_digsite_2_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

被[From.Planet.GetName]大气圈笼罩着的是一处鬼影幢幢的巨大居住站——埃克斯-柏楞居住区。这里曾是一处日喧夜嚣的太空城，居民们享受着科技带来的巨大便利，过着如同神仙一般的生活，如今此处已成为一座寂静的墓碑。软体类的外星人横七竖八地倒在走道上，腐败程度各不相同。验尸报告表明，这些外星人由于居住站的维生系统失效最后死于窒息。\n\n他们的家里装备有模样奇怪的机械座椅，其顶端连接到了一个形状是蓝色巨大球状体的中央数据库——眠梦仪。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:957`

### English

Shrouded within [From.Planet.GetName]'s gaseous atmosphere is the dark looming form of an enormous habitat - the Ix Belèn Residence. Previously a bustling space city where the residents enjoyed a pampered lifestyle with a multitude of comforts made available through technology, the habitat is now a silent tomb. Molluscoid aliens lie slumped across the floor in various states of decay. A postmortem reveals that they died from asphyxiation as the habitat's life support system was disabled, intentionally.\n\nTheir homes are fitted with unusual mechanical chairs that are hooked up to a central database in the shape of a gigantic blue orb - the Memorex.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1028`

## ancrel.4043.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4043.name&lang=en)

### 中文

眠梦仪

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:958`

### English

The Memorex

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1029`

## ancrel.4043.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4043.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

此处的居民研发出了一个增强学习程式，当他们每晚在机械座椅上入眠时，这个程式就会运行。它被用于移除居民脑海里不愉快的记忆，并训练新技能。氧气耗尽或者约会时被放鸽子这种埃克斯-柏楞人天天都要经受的恐惧就没有必要遭受第二次。这一程式最开始是有好处的。\n\n居住站的领导层强制要求实行系统性的心智增强，并由眠梦仪对全体居民进行训练。然而，这个系统被滥用了。有人曾暗中在未经当事人同意的情况下尝试删除居民的记忆和特质，这一行为最终自食恶果。这些外星人失去了生存必须的重要技能，并进入了植物状态。不久，居住站的维生系统就停摆了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:959`

### English

The inhabitants had developed an enhanced learning program that ran every night as they slept in their mechanical chairs. It was used to remove unpleasant memories and train new skills. Being picked last for spaceball or stood up on a date were little day-to-day horrors the Ix Belènnians did not have to suffer to twice. And for a while it was bliss.\n\nSystematic mind enhancements were mandated by the habitat leadership and distributed to all residents from the Memorex. But the system was exploited. There were clandestine efforts to delete memories and traits collectively from the population without their consent and eventually it backfired. The aliens unlearned important skills for their survival and entered a vegetative state. Shortly thereafter the habitat life support was disabled.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1030`

## ancrel.4044.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4044.name&lang=en)

### 中文

我者何为？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:960`

### English

Who am I?

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1031`

## ancrel.4044.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4044.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

对居住站日志的扫描显示，其控制系统在维生系统下线的几十年后还曾被多次激活。有人重载了安保系统，进入一个维修舱室并接入了眠梦仪。一个数据板和一个小型的蓝色记忆球体——眠梦仪的袖珍版本——被遗弃在了这个舱室。\n\n数据板内写满了某人濒临疯狂时迸发出了混乱思绪。一遍又一遍，充满绝望的喊叫声重复着同样的问题：“我是谁？埃克斯-柏楞人发生了什么？”最后一条条目写道“现在，我知道我要在最后忘记这一切。”可能那个球体能够告诉我们忘记的东西是什么。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:961`

### English

A scan of the habitat's logs reveals its control system was activated again on multiple occasions decades after the life support was disabled. Someone overrode security, entered a maintenance hatch and accessed the Memorex. A datapad and a tiny blue memory orb - a smaller version of the Memorex - lie discarded by the hatch.\n\nThe datapad is filled with the frenzied thoughts of someone on the brink of madness. Again and again the same questions are repeated with growing despair: "who am I? What happened on Ix Belèn?" The final entry reads "now that I know I must forget one last time." Perhaps the orb will reveal what was forgotten.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1032`

## ancrel.4045.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4045.name&lang=en)

### 中文

萨阿里亚最后的记忆球体

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:962`

### English

Saariah's Last Memory Orb

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1033`

## ancrel.4045.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4045.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

球体的第一条记忆显示萨阿里亚博士在眠梦仪的开发中起到了绝对的领导作用。另一条记忆是几年之后，显示她私下秘密地抹除了眠梦仪的数据，原因不明。然后，又有一条记忆显示，萨阿里亚正绝望地努力工作，希望能够恢复居民的记忆，然而收效甚微。居民们空洞的双眼看着前方，头耸拉着，除了大脑的自给功能其他一切机能都消失了。\n\n最终，在承认自己失败之后，她隐匿了自己的罪行证据。当埃克斯-柏楞居住区被其环绕的气态巨行星的重力井拽入其中时，她形单影只，目视着这一切。触角分泌的粘液，似乎在诉说着名为绝望的情绪。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:963`

### English

The orb's first memory shows Dr. Saariah spearheading the development of the Memorex to great acclaim. Another memory from years later shows her secretly wiping Memorex data for unknown reasons. Yet another memory is of Saariah working frantically on restoring the residents' memories but to no avail. They stare emptily ahead, heads lolling, all but their autonomic brain functions lost.\n\nFinally admitting defeat she hides the evidence of her crimes. She watches, alone, as the Ix Belèn Residence is pulled into the gravity well of the gas giant it once orbited, feelers secreting large amounts of mucus in what is believed to be an alien expression of great distress.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1034`

## ancrel.4046.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4046.name&lang=en)

### 中文

灵光透进浊镜台

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:964`

### English

The Temporary Sunshine of the Spotless Mind

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1035`

## ancrel.4046.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4046.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

最后的记忆显示，年岁更高的萨阿里亚身着祭司袍在一片慌乱中苏醒，触须不断拍打，耳朵扇动个不停。她从床上摔倒下来，并开始慌乱地记下她的回忆，然而这段回忆不过是另一段梦魇——双目空洞的外星人倒在居住站的走廊上，不停地抽搐。\n\n接着，她的脸因为恐惧而扭曲，因为她意识到这一切都是真实的——梦魇与闪回乃是被抽取的记忆重新回到了她的大脑。如果被抽取的记忆能够返回，那么埃克斯-柏楞的居民也本可以被治愈恢复。维护舱室看上去最后被打开过一次，但是萨阿里亚的飞船仍旧处于停泊状态。如果我们愿意，我们可以将眠梦仪带回[Root.Owner.Capital.GetName]。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:965`

### English

The final memory shows a much older Saariah in a priest's robe waking up in a state of panic, tentacles flailing and ears flapping. She stumbles out of bed and begins to frantically jot down her recollections of yet another nightmare in which empty-eyed aliens convulse on a habitat floor.\n\nThen her face contorts with horror as it dawns upon her that it is all real - the nightmares and the flashbacks are extracted memories coming back to her. And if extracted memories can return, the inhabitants of Ix Belèn could have recovered. The maintenance hatch appears to have opened one last time but Saariah's ship still remains docked at the landing. We could bring the Memorex with us back to [Root.Owner.Capital.GetName], if we wish.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1036`

## ancrel.4046.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4046.a&lang=en)

### 中文

眠梦仪是我们的了。

### English

The Memorex is coming with us.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1037`

## ancrel.4046.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4046.b&lang=en)

### 中文

记忆不是可以随便把玩的东西，我们最好忘了“眠梦仪”这件事。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:967`

### English

Memories are not to be tampered with. The Memorex is best forgotten.

## ancrel.4046.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4046.c&lang=en)

异种的脑袋被门夹坏了！我们[Root.Owner.GetSpeciesName]会把这个“眠梦仪”完善好的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:968`

### English

Xeno squish-brains! The [Root.Owner.GetSpeciesName] will perfect this "Memorex".

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1039`

## ancrel.4051.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4051.name&lang=en)

### 中文

羽翼雕像

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:970`

### English

Winged Statues

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1041`

## ancrel.4051.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4051.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

九个巨型雕像，在其下的景物上投下了冰冷而高大的阴影。这些雕像带有羽翼而又通体苍白，散发着来自异世界的气质。它们并不像这颗行星上的任何生命形式。或许它们是依某种早被遗忘的神祗或偶像的形象建造的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:971`

### English

are nine massive statues, casting dark, tall shadows over the landscape. Winged and pale they exude an aura of otherworldliness. Perhaps they were created in the image of a deity or idol that has since passed into oblivion. They do not resemble any lifeforms on this planet.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1042`

## ancrel.4051.desc_jungle

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4051.desc_jungle&lang=en)

### 中文

队员们清理了[Root.GetName] 上的雨林后，无意中有了一个不寻常的发现，在一丛古老枯萎的树林下覆盖着一处[Root.Owner.GetSpeciesName] 似乎从未涉足的地方，$ancrel.4051.desc$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:972`

### English

The crew clearing the jungle on [Root.GetName] has stumbled upon an unusual find. Previously obscured deep within a tangle of old withered trees, where it would seem no [Root.Owner.GetSpeciesName] has ventured before, $ancrel.4051.desc$

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1043`

## ancrel.4051.desc_kelp

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4051.desc_kelp&lang=en)

### 中文

队员们清理了[Root.GetName]上的剧毒海藻之后，无意中有了一个不寻常的发现，在浑浊水体里一团随波逐流的海藻之中隐藏着$ancrel.4051.desc$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:973`

### English

The crew clearing the toxic kelp on [Root.GetName] has stumbled upon an unusual find. Hidden in murky waters among a forest of swaying seaweed $ancrel.4051.desc$

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1044`

## sentinels_digsite

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asentinels_digsite&lang=en)

### 中文

哨兵

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:979`

### English

The Sentinels

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1050`

## sentinels_digsite_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asentinels_digsite_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这些巨型雕像矗立在[From.Planet.GetName]上，静静注视着什么。九个堪称不朽的雕像沿着漫长的边沿一一排开，位于这片区域的重要战略点，似乎在守护着它们中心的某种物品。\n\n他们涂有精致的表面镀层，警觉的姿势好像时刻准备着战斗，不会移动的眼球也似乎紧随着你的每个动作，然而这里显然已经没有什么东西需要保护了。也许他们要保护的东西没能挨过岁月的流逝，又或是他们的任务早已失败。其上的累累伤痕表明，这些哨兵中的许多已经快要被物理损害彻底侵蚀了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:980`

### English

Silent giants stand watch on [From.Planet.GetName]. Nine monumental statues placed along a wide perimeter at strategical points in the landscape appear to be guarding something at its center.\n\nTheir skin is covered in fine scale plating, their postures vigilant, ready to spring to action, and their unmoving eyes follow your every step. Yet there is nothing in the area in apparent need of protection. Perhaps their ward did not survive the passing of time. Many of the Sentinels themselves bear marks of assault.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1051`

## ancrel.4052.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4052.name&lang=en)

### 中文

哨兵作祟

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:981`

### English

The Sentinels Did It

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1052`

## ancrel.4052.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4052.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

考古学家们有些异样。迄今为止，发掘一直受到离奇事故、伤害和不幸情况的阻碍，现在即使是具有数十年实地经验的高级考古学家也认为哨兵们是这些情况的祸源。\n\n他们满怀迷信和无端的恐惧，并开始相信这些雕像中存在着某种不希望被打扰的恶质实体。许多人声称，在没有人在注视时雕像会移动，但没有任何迹象表明它们的无关节肢体有任何移动能力。事态随后进一步失控，有人在哨兵肢体末端的细微伤口里发现了生物质的痕迹，或者说，明显的出血现象。\n\n是哨兵们成了受人利用的替罪羊，还是有其他更加险恶的情形，情况尚有待明确。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:982`

### English

Something has gotten into the archaeologists. The excavation thus far has been impeded by freak accidents, injury and misfortune and now even senior researchers, with decades of field experience, are blaming the Sentinels.\n\nOvercome with superstition and paranoia they claim the statues move about when no one is watching yet there is nothing to suggest their jointless limbs are even capable of motion. Matters spun further out of control when traces of biomatter, indicative of heavy bleeding, was found lodged within a series of tiny cuts on the terminal portions of a Sentinel.\n\nWhether the Sentinels are just a convenient scapegoat or there is a more sinister explanation remains to be seen.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1053`

## ancrel.4053.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4053.name&lang=en)

### 中文

捷足先登

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:983`

### English

They Were Here First

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1054`

## ancrel.4053.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4053.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

对哨兵们的成分分析显示雕像是由一种未知的杂合陶金复合物制成的。这种材料不可能被史前社会铸造出来，但初步读数表明它们的年代早于[From.Planet.GetName]的所有已知文明。\n\n不幸的是，扫描仪无法穿透哨兵们的金属涂层。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:984`

### English

A compositional analysis of the Sentinels has revealed that they are made from an unknown hybrid metal ceramic composite. Such a material could not have been forged by a prehistoric society, yet preliminary readings suggest they predate all known civilizations on [From.Planet.GetName]. \n\nUnfortunately the scanners cannot yet pierce the Sentinels' metal coating.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1055`

## ancrel.4054.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4054.name&lang=en)

### 中文

辐照核心

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:985`

### English

Radiant Core

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1056`

## ancrel.4054.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4054.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

考古学家们已重构了[missing_archaeologist.GetName]的改进型扫描仪。穿透雕像的复合材料后，扫描仪检测到了从哨兵核心发出的微弱辐射。为进一步进行调查，有必要剖开其中一个哨兵；在此过程中很可能对其产生破坏。鉴于其中一座雕像已缺失了部分翅膀，我们的考古学家建议从那个开始。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:986`

### English

The archaeologists have reconstructed [missing_archaeologist.GetName]'s modified scanner. Set to pierce deeper within the statues' hybrid composite material it has picked up on weak radiation emanating from their cores. To investigate further it would be necessary to break one of the Sentinels open, very likely destroying it in the process. Since one of the statues has already lost part of its wing our archaeologists recommend starting with that one.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1057`

## ancrel.4054.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4054.a&lang=en)

### 中文

小心地拆开哨兵，这样我们还能把它装回去。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:987`

### English

Open the Sentinel carefully so that we can reassemble it.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1058`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ADESTROY_SENTINELS&lang=en)

哨兵

### English

The Sentinels

## DESTROY_SENTINELS_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ADESTROY_SENTINELS_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

哨兵厚实的鳞状外层经证实无法被普通工具穿透，考古学家需要工程部门的协助才能有所进展。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:994`

### English

The Sentinels' thick, scale-plated outer layer has proven impenetrable with ordinary tools and the archaeologists need assistance from the engineering department to proceed further.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1065`

## SECRET_TOMB

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASECRET_TOMB&lang=en)

深入地下

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:995`

### English

Into the Earth

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1066`

## SECRET_TOMB_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASECRET_TOMB_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

在一座先前的哨兵的底座下隐藏着一块有复杂图样的圆形石板。我们相信这可能是通向一处地下复合建筑体的入口。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:996`

### English

Previously hidden below the base of one of the Sentinels is an intricately patterned circular slate. We believe it may be the entrance to an underground complex.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1067`

## ancrel.4055.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4055.name&lang=en)

### 中文

哨兵苏生

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:997`

### English

They're Alive!

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1068`

## ancrel.4055.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4055.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

雕像全身轻微颤动了一下，随之重获生命。它们身体低鸣，皮肤发光。从核心脉动而出的辐射使它们变得可塑而透明，并显示出一个数字节点网格。\n\n他们发出金属在扭曲时的怪异尖啸，眼中露出仇恨，从营地中切割而过。现在他们飞向了最近的定居点。\n\n他们的离去显露出地面上的圆形石板，先前隐藏在其中一个哨兵的底座之下。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:998`

### English

when a gentle shiver passed through the statues and they sprung to life, bodies humming and skin glowing. Radiation pulsating from their cores has made them malleable and transparent, revealing a digital grid of nodes.\n\nWith an unearthly screech of tortured metal they charged at the archaeologists, overrunning the camp. Now they are flying towards the nearest settlement.\n\nTheir departure has revealed a round slate in the ground, previously hidden under the base of one Sentinel.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1069`

## ancrel.4055.desc.phobe

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4055.desc.phobe&lang=en)

### 中文

这些哨兵苏醒了。工程团队正准备把哨兵砸烂捣碎，碾成齑粉，突然$ancrel.4055.desc$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:999`

### English

The Sentinels have woken. The engineering team were just preparing to smash, bash and mash the Sentinels down $ancrel.4055.desc$

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1070`

## ancrel.4055.desc.other

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4055.desc.other&lang=en)

这些哨兵苏醒了。工程团队正准备把雕像拆解，突然$ancrel.4055.desc$

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1071`

### English

Kill the Automatons!

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1072`

## ancrel.4056.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4056.name&lang=en)

### 中文

“永恒”

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1002`

### English

The Evermore

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1073`

## ancrel.4056.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4056.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

石盘下的确隐藏着入口。打开后可以进入隧道夹层，隧道本身则通向[event_target:sentinels_planet.GetName]地壳深处。在那里，这颗行星上某种开智的人型土著生物在一组华丽的仪式性房间内涤净自身，踏上最后的旅程。在他们终将结束的生命的最后，他们抛下自己的肉体，获取永恒的来生，在一处电脑模拟的天堂中以数字形式存在。谁曾想到外星天堂居然可以放进一个还没有护卫舰转向模块大的硬盘里呢？\n\n在某个时间点，全体人口都决定以数字化的形式继续存在，只留下寥寥数人。这些人将思维上传入哨兵当中，守护他们的安息之地直至永远。\n\n这一虚拟模拟或许可以用来鼓舞民众的精神。它看起来一定有某种娱乐价值。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1003`

### English

The Sentinel was indeed hiding an entryway. It opens on a mezzanine of underground tunnels leading deep into [event_target:sentinels_planet.GetName]'s crust. There a sapient species of humanoids, native to the planet, prepared for their final journey in a set of beautiful ceremonial chambers. At the end of their mortal existence they abandoned their fleshy bodies for a virtual afterlife within a simulation of paradise contained on a single drive.\n\nAt some point the entire population decided to move on to a digitized existence, leaving only a few behind, their minds uploaded to the Sentinels, condemned to guard their resting place forever.\n\nPerhaps this virtual simulation could lift the spirits of the masses. It certainly seems to hold some entertainment value.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1074`

## ancrel.4056.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4056.a&lang=en)

### 中文

净化外星人并重新设定这个虚拟世界的用途。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1004`

### English

Purge the aliens and repurpose the simulation for our needs.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1075`

## ancrel.4056.b

一个虚拟猎场……现在这是我们的娱乐世界了……

### English

A virtual hunting ground... Now that's entertainment...

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1076`

## ancrel.4056.c

### English

Replicate the technology. Let the aliens live in peace.

## ancrel.4056.d

我们将研究它。开启我们自己的疯狂表演。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1007`

### English

The aliens will make excellent lab rats. Commence the freak show.

## ancrel.4057.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4057.name&lang=en)

### 中文

击败哨兵

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1008`

### English

Sentinels Defeated

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1079`

## ancrel.4057.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4057.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们击败了最后一名哨兵。我们一次精准的攻击炸到了哨兵的腹部，哨兵随即倒地，身体渐渐僵硬，能量核心也随之黯淡。雕像用尽最后的能量颤巍巍地握拳，伸向靠过来的士兵，恳求我们“让他们安息吧”。随后，它的身躯便冰冷下来，眼神变得空洞，面部也放松起来。\n\n在哨兵之下，士兵们发现了一处秘密入口，先前被哨兵的力场所掩盖。对这些哨兵的完整检查发现，其并非是简单的自动化机器人，而是有着完整有机生物的数字化意识。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1009`

### English

We have defeated the last Sentinel. It collapsed after a well-aimed blast to its abdomen, body solidifying in rigor mortis as its power core dimmed. The statue closed a trembling fist around the nearest soldier and, with its last energy, pleaded for us to "let them rest in peace". Then it froze, eyes empty, face cold.\n\nBelow it the soldiers discovered what appears to be a secret entrance, previously hidden by the Sentinel's force field. A thorough examination of the Sentinels has revealed that they are no simple automatons, but contain the digitized spirits of organic beings.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1080`

## ancrel.4057.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4057.a&lang=en)

### 中文

封锁入口。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1010`

### English

Seal the entrance.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1081`

## ancrel.4075.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4075.name&lang=en)

### 中文

来自[sentinels_planet.GetName]的幸存者

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1011`

### English

Survivors from [sentinels_planet.GetName]

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1082`

## ancrel.4075.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4075.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一艘载着从[sentinels_planet.GetName]上的种族屠杀中逃离的幸存者的舰船抵达了[sentinels_new_home.GetName]。他们隐藏在旷野之中直至哨兵离去，奇迹般地逃脱了屠杀。很可能是这些自动机器人，自以为它们“将我们从肉体的束缚中解放出来”，在工作完成后离去了。\n\n在它们消失后，一个通往地底深处的秘密入口从力场下显现。可能哨兵们把它们自己封锁在里面了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1012`

### English

A ship of survivors from the genocide on [sentinels_planet.GetName] has arrived to [sentinels_new_home.GetName]. They miraculously escaped by hiding in the wilderness until one day the Sentinels were just gone. It is likely the automatons, who themselves believed they were 'liberating us from the confines of our own flesh', took off once their work was done.\n\nUpon their disappearance a secret entranceway leading deep into the underground revealed itself from behind a force field. It is possible the Sentinels sealed themselves inside.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1083`

## ancrel.4075.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4075.a&lang=en)

### 中文

进行彻底的搜索。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1013`

### English

Leave no stone unturned.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1084`

## ancrel.4075.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4075.b&lang=en)

### 中文

把入口埋掉。

### English

## ancrel.4076.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4076.name&lang=en)

### 中文

“永恒”

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1015`

### English

The Evermore

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1086`

## ancrel.4076.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4076.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们已经进入了[event_target:sentinels_planet.GetName]上的秘密入口。入口内是一处近期才被遗弃的寺庙复合体，一种人型生物在此踏上他们最后的旅程。在他们终将结束的生命的最后，他们抛下自己的肉体，在一处被称作“永恒”的模拟天堂中获取虚拟的来生。\n\n在某个时间点，全体人口都决定以数字化的形式继续存在，只留下寥寥数人。这些人将思维上传入机器人中，哨兵们由此诞生。\n\n现已了无踪迹的哨兵们为我们留下了一份临别赠礼——一块包含着[event_target:sentinels_planet.GetName]全体人口的硬盘，以数字化的形式来获取永恒无尽的无聊时光。外星天堂原来是一种永无止境的抽象桌游，其核心内容是通过积累欢乐豆来统治星河。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1016`

### English

We have breached the secret entrance on [event_target:sentinels_planet.GetName]. It opens on a recently abandoned temple complex where a species of humanoids prepared for their final journey. At the end of their mortal existence they abandoned their flesh bodies for a virtual afterlife within a simulation of paradise called the Evermore.\n\nAt some point it was decided the entire population would move on to a digitized existence leaving only a few behind to guard their resting place. Condemned to an eternity of servitude their minds were uploaded to robotic bodies and so the Sentinels were created.\n\nNow gone without a trace, the Sentinels have left us with a parting gift - a single drive containing the entire population of [event_target:sentinels_planet.GetName], digitized for all eternity and infinitely bored. As it turns out alien heaven consists of a never-ending abstract board game centered on galactic dominance through the accumulation of legumes.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1087`

## ancrel.4076.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4076.a&lang=en)

### 中文

利用他们的思维从事科研工作。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1017`

### English

Put their minds to work with some science.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1088`

## ancrel.4076.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4076.b&lang=en)

### 中文

对“永恒”进行调整，供大众娱乐之用。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1018`

### English

Modify the Evermore to entertain the masses.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1089`

## ancrel.4077.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4077.name&lang=en)

### 中文

“永恒”

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1019`

### English

The Evermore

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1090`

## ancrel.4077.desc.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4077.desc.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们做到了！这些居住在“永恒”之内的数字化人口已被分派去协助[from.owner.capital_scope.GetName]上的科学研究。他们几乎不需要休息，并且因为终于能干点儿别的事情而十分开心……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1020`

### English

We have done it! The digitized population residing within the Evermore have been assigned to assist with scientific research on [from.owner.capital_scope.GetName]. They need very little rest and are quite happy to be doing something different...

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1091`

## ancrel.4077.desc.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4077.desc.b&lang=en)

### 中文

我们做到了！“永恒”模拟已经被重新调校，以更好地适合我们民众的品味。我们的全体人口现在可以与“永恒”天堂中的数字化人口一道在幻想中游玩，没有网络延迟。这已极大地鼓舞了他们的精神，并缩减了对物质材料的需求。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1021`

### English

We have done it! The Evermore simulation has been remodeled to better cater to the particular taste of our people. All our population can now live out their fantasies together with the digitized population in the Evermore paradise without latency. This has greatly improved their spirits and lessened their need for material things.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1092`

## ancrel.4058.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4058.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

哨兵们被唤醒了。当工程队正打算拆解破损的哨兵时，雕塑轻柔的颤动起来，他们涌动出生命的迹象，身体低鸣，皮肤发光。从核心辐射出来的脉冲使得他们具有着多变性与透明性，显现出一种由数字虚拟构成的节点网络。\n\n在我们做出反应之前，他们便包围了考古学家，并且把我们吊在了一个力场之中。现在他们想要和我们谈谈。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1022`

### English

The Sentinels have woken. The engineering team was just preparing to disassemble the broken Sentinel when a gentle shiver passed through the statues and they sprung to life, bodies humming and skin glowing. Radiation pulsating from their cores has made them malleable and transparent, revealing a digital grid of limbs.\n\nBefore anyone could react they had surrounded the archaeologists and suspended them within a force field. Now they wish to speak with us.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1093`

## ancrel.4058.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4058.a&lang=en)

### 中文

听听他们要说什么。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1023`

### English

Hear them out.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1094`

## ancrel.4058.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4058.b&lang=en)

### 中文

让他们永远闭嘴！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1024`

### English

Silence them forever!

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1095`

## ancrel.4059.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4059.name&lang=en)

### 中文

哨兵

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1025`

### English

The Sentinels

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1096`

## ancrel.4059.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4059.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§H那名折翼的哨兵原来也是它们中的一员，这名哨兵说道：\n\n§!迪瓦纳，请放下他们。我希望从这个监狱里解脱。我已经数不清我们为主人服侍过多少时日了。我们在地上苦守，而他们却在下面享受着天堂！我还活着但却已经死了！他们早已死去却依旧如同活着一般！你仍旧不肯放我离开吗？\n\n§H迪瓦纳朝她投去憎恶的目光。§!\n\n埃克西亚，以神之名，团结一心吧。我们所爱的人都在下方安息，我们也承诺过要永远守护他们的来生。这是我们唯一的救赎。\n\n§H她又把我们举到了空中。§!\n\n你们对我们没有威胁，[Root.owner.GetSpeciesNamePlural]。让我们重归宁静，我们会永远守护这颗行星的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1026`

### English

§HThe Sentinel with the broken wing turns to one of its peers and speaks.\n\n§!Please Divana, set them down. I wish to be freed from this prison. I can no longer count the days we have served our masters, toiling on this earth while they enjoy the heavens below. I am alive but dead, they are dead but alive. Will you not let me go?\n\n§HDivana looks at her with disgust.§!\n\nExcelsia, by the gods, keep it together. Our loved ones rest below. Have you forgotten our sin? We made a promise to guard their afterlives eternally. That is our only redemption.\n\n§HShe sizes us up.§!\n\nYou are no threat to us, [Root.owner.GetSpeciesNamePlural]. Leave us in peace and we will protect this planet eternally.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1097`

## ancrel.4059.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4059.a&lang=en)

等等……你们是谁？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1027`

### English

What...are you?

### English

We are guardians of the gates to heaven - keepers of our people's immortality.

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1030`

### English

§HExcelsia approaches the archaeologists.§!\n\nPlease, I beg of you to end my suffering.

No. We need you.

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4059.c&lang=en)

### 中文

天堂？

Heaven?

## ancrel.4059.c.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4059.c.response&lang=en)

### 中文

天堂，是的……尽管我怀疑你们的天堂概念与我们主人的是否一致。\n\n§H她抬起了一个眉毛，暗声窃笑。§!\n\n我们的主人可是有着精致的品味……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1036`

### English

Heaven, yes... Though I doubt your concept of heaven matches that of our masters. \n\n§HShe raises one eyebrow and snickers.§!\n\nOur masters are of a refined taste...

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1107`

## ancrel.4060.a.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4060.a.response&lang=en)

### 中文

那你就得死。\n\n§H其他哨兵挥动着他们的武器，但埃克西亚只是微笑，脸上透露着平静。§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1037`

### English

Then you will die.\n\n§HThe other Sentinels brandish their weapons but Excelsia only smiles, a peaceful look upon her face.§!

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1108`

## ancrel.4060.name

信息传输

### English

Transmission

## ancrel.4060.desc

### 中文

我们不能让你打搅我们所爱之人休息的土地。让他们安息吧，你也永远不必担忧外来的威胁。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1039`

### English

We cannot let you disturb the ground where our loved ones lie. Let them rest in peace and you will never have to worry about outside threats.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1110`

## ancrel.4060.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4060.a&lang=en)

### English

You will vacate this land instantly.

我们可以利用你们的保护。

### English

We could use your protection.

向此行星添加哨兵防御部队。

### English

Adds Sentinel defensive armies to this planet.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1113`

## ancrel.4061.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4061.name&lang=en)

### 中文

再造“永恒”

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1043`

### English

Reinventing the Evermore

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1114`

## ancrel.4061.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4061.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们成功复制了“永恒”，并将其改造以适合我们的需要。我们的居民可以在一片虚拟游乐场中实现他们的幻想，没有网络延迟，这已极大地鼓舞了居民的精神，并缩减了对物质材料的需求。\n\n同时，那些人形外星人还继续在原本的“永恒”中生活，用一种略有不同的方法享受着他们的天堂。他们被锁定在一盘永无止境的抽象桌游之中，其核心内容是通过积累欢乐豆来统治星河。很难拿得准他们到底幸不幸福。他们眉头紧蹙，或许这就是外星欢乐的表现方式，又或许不是。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1044`

### English

We have replicated the Evermore and tailored it to our needs. Living out their fantasies in a virtual playground, without latency, has greatly heightened our inhabitants spirits and lessened their needs for material things.\n\nMeanwhile the humanoid aliens live on in the original Evermore, in a somewhat different take on paradise. They are locked in an abstract never-ending board game centered on galactic domination through the accumulation of legumes. It is impossible to say with certainty if they are happy. Their brows are deeply furrowed in what may, or may not, be an expression of alien joy.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1115`

## ancrel.4061.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4061.a&lang=en)

### 中文

每个人都有自己的选择……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1045`

### English

Each to their own...

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1116`

## ancrel.4063.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4063.name&lang=en)

### 中文

失去[sentinels_planet.GetName]

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1046`

### English

[sentinels_planet.GetName] Lost

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1117`

## ancrel.4063.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4063.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

意想不到的事情发生了。我们失去了[sentinels_planet.GetName]。哨兵们在一阵猛烈的旋风中爆发出能量，朝着我们的安保力量直落而下。我们不可能与不会流血也无需休息的敌人进行一场公平的战斗。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1047`

### English

The unthinkable has happened. We have lost [sentinels_planet.GetName]. The Sentinels descended upon our security forces in an infernal tornado of wings and force-blasts. There is no such thing as a fair fight with a foe that neither bleeds nor sleeps.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1118`

## ancrel.4063.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4063.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们会来取回本应属于我们的东西的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1048`

### English

We will come back for what is rightfully ours.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1119`

## ancrel.4064.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4064.name&lang=en)

### 中文

[sentinels_planet.GetName]已解放

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1049`

### English

[sentinels_planet.GetName] Liberated

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1120`

## ancrel.4064.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4064.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

哨兵们已被击倒，[sentinels_planet.GetName]重新回到了我们手中。最后一台自动机器人倒下时，其能量核心逐渐黯淡，它握着颤抖的拳头伸向靠近的士兵，恳请她“让他们安息吧”。随即不再移动，眼神空洞，面色冰冷。\n\n在哨兵之下，士兵们发现了一处秘密入口，先前被哨兵的力场所掩盖。对这些哨兵的完整检查发现，其并非是简单的自动化机器人，而是有着完整有机生物的数字化意识。\n\n不论这里究竟埋藏着何种秘密，这一胜利之日都将被人们长久铭记。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1050`

### English

The Sentinels have fallen and [sentinels_planet.GetName] is ours again. As the last automaton collapsed, its energy core dimming, it closed a trembling fist around the nearest soldier and pleaded with her to 'let them rest in peace'. Then it froze, eyes empty, face cold.\n\nBelow it the soldiers discovered what appears to be a secret entrance, previously hidden by the Sentinel's force field. A thorough examination has revealed that the statues are no simple automatons, but contain the digitized spirits of organic beings.\n\nRegardless of what secrets lie buried below, this triumphant day will not soon be forgotten.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1121`

## ancrel.4064.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4064.a&lang=en)

### 中文

对入口进行调查

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1051`

### English

Investigate the entrance.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1122`

## ancrel.4064.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4064.b&lang=en)

### 中文

埋掉它吧。我们终于回家了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1052`

### English

Bury it. We are home at last.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1123`

## ancrel.4065.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4065.name&lang=en)

### 中文

考古学家失踪

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1054`

### English

Archaeologist Missing

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1125`

## ancrel.4065.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4065.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

科学专员[missing_archaeologist.GetName]失踪了。[missing_archaeologist.GetSheHeCap]最后一次被看到离开营地是在日落时分，[missing_archaeologist.GetSheHe]希望找到安静的地点考虑哨兵问题。[missing_archaeologist.GetSheHe]在消失前的几天，出于事故数量的不断增加，提出了关于营地安全的担忧，我们担心[missing_archaeologist.GetSheHe]也许已经抛下了[missing_archaeologist.GetHerHis]的任务，以示抗议。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1055`

### English

Science Officer [missing_archaeologist.GetName] has gone missing. [missing_archaeologist.GetSheHeCap] [missing_archaeologist.GetWasWere] last seen leaving the camp at sunset to find a tranquil spot to consider the Sentinels. In the days leading up to [missing_archaeologist.GetHerHis] disappearance [missing_archaeologist.GetSheHe] raised concerns about the safety at camp following a growing number of accidents and we fear that [missing_archaeologist.GetSheHe] may have abandoned [missing_archaeologist.GetHerHis] mission in protest.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1126`

## ancrel.4065.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4065.a&lang=en)

### 中文

令人不安。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1056`

### English

Disconcerting.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1127`

## ancrel.4066.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4066.name&lang=en)

### 中文

失踪的考古学家

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1057`

### English

Missing Archaeologist

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1128`

## ancrel.4066.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4066.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[missing_archaeologist.GetName]的个人物品，包括[missing_archaeologist.GetHerHis]的意念集中仪——一种用来将思维与外部干扰隔绝的仪器——被发现在一处能俯瞰哨兵的边沿上。雕像旁边用木棍画出来的半完工草图，暗示[missing_archaeologist.GetSheHe]或许离开[missing_archaeologist.GetHerHis]的工作岗位是为了标记其位置。\n\n[missing_archaeologist.GetHerHisCap]的手电和扫描仪在找到时被一块哨兵翅膀的碎片压坏了。这台扫描仪被以一种奇怪的方式调整过了。也许它可以帮助我们穿透哨兵的保护层。\n\n如果我们希望活着找到[missing_archaeologist.GetName]，我们必须向搜索任务分派额外的资源，因为[missing_archaeologist.GetHerHis]在旷野中生还的几率每天都在急剧下降。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1058`

### English

[missing_archaeologist.GetName]'s belongings, including [missing_archaeologist.GetHerHis] MindGazer - a device used to isolate the mind from outside interferences - have been found on a ledge overlooking the Sentinels. Sticks forming a half-finished outline around one of the statues suggests [missing_archaeologist.GetSheHe] may have diverted from [missing_archaeologist.GetHerHis] exercise to mark its position.\n\n[missing_archaeologist.GetHerHisCap] flashlight and scanner were discovered crushed under a shattered piece of the Sentinel's wing. The scanner had been modified in a peculiar manner. Perhaps it could help us pierce the Sentinels protective layer.\n\nIf we wish to find [missing_archaeologist.GetName] alive, we must divert extra resources to the search as [missing_archaeologist.GetHerHis] chances for survival in the wilderness diminish drastically every day.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1129`

## ancrel.4066.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4066.a&lang=en)

### 中文

不惜一切代价。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1059`

### English

Spare no expenses.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1130`

## ancrel.4066.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4066.b&lang=en)

我们不会在逃兵身上浪费时间。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1060`

### English

We are not wasting time on deserters.

分派我们的一些考古学家进行研究。

### English

Divert some of our archaeologists to the search.

## ancrel.4067.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4067.name&lang=en)

### 中文

失踪考古学家已找到

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1062`

### English

Missing Archaeologist Found

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1133`

## ancrel.4067.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4067.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[missing_archaeologist.GetName]的尸体已被找到，被埋在一块巨型石板之下。死因已被确定为严重的头部创伤。有人在[missing_archaeologist.GetHerHim]安息的地点上环绕摆放了一小圈宝石以表敬意。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1063`

### English

[missing_archaeologist.GetName] has been found dead, buried under a huge slab of stone. The cause of death has been determined to be severe head trauma. Someone has made an effort to honor [missing_archaeologist.GetHerHim] resting place by adding a small circle of precious stones around it.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1134`

## ancrel.4067.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4067.a&lang=en)

### 中文

愿她安息。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1064`

### English

May she rest in peace.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1135`

## ancrel.4068.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4068.name&lang=en)

### 中文

失踪考古学家已找到

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1065`

### English

Missing Archaeologist Found

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1136`

## ancrel.4068.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4068.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[missing_archaeologist.GetName]被发现时不省人事，受伤严重，躲在一块自然生成的岩结构下的灌木丛中，[missing_archaeologist.GetHerHis]胳膊下塞了一块沾满[missing_archaeologist.GetBloodType]的数据板。[missing_archaeologist.GetSheHeCap]被火速送往医院，接受治疗由上方钝器打击造成的严重脑损伤。\n\n尽管目前[missing_archaeologist.GetHerHis]的情况已经稳定，但[missing_archaeologist.GetSheHe]仍有失忆症状，不记得自己是如何受到这种损伤的。我们从[missing_archaeologist.GetHerHis]的颅顶骨中取出了一些碎屑，材质与哨兵雕塑所用的独特合金相同。[missing_archaeologist.GetSheHe]所记得的一切就是曾经躲避某样东西，然后摔落在了什么东西上面。好在[missing_archaeologist.GetHerHis]的笔记中包含了一些有用的信息。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1066`

### English

[missing_archaeologist.GetName] has been found unconscious and badly injured, hiding in the underbrush below a natural rock formation, with a datapad covered in [missing_archaeologist.GetBloodType] tucked under [missing_archaeologist.GetHerHis] arm. [missing_archaeologist.GetSheHeCap] [missing_archaeologist.GetWasWere] rushed to the hospital and treated for severe brain injury indicative of blunt trauma by impact from above.\n\nThough [missing_archaeologist.GetHerHis] condition is now stable, [missing_archaeologist.GetSheHe] still suffers from memory loss and has no recollection of how [missing_archaeologist.GetSheHe] sustained [missing_archaeologist.GetHerHis] wounds. Splinters of the unique metal-alloy from which the Sentinels are sculpted were lodged in [missing_archaeologist.GetHerHis] parietal cranial bone. All [missing_archaeologist.GetSheHe] remember[missing_archaeologist.GetSXX] is hiding from someone and subsiding off of what little the land had to offer. However, [missing_archaeologist.GetHerHis] notes contain some helpful information.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1137`

## ancrel.4068.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4068.a&lang=en)

不祥之兆。

### English

Ominous.

## ancrel.4078.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4078.name&lang=en)

### 中文

火灾

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1068`

### English

Fire

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1139`

## ancrel.4078.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4078.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

入夜后，正当考古学家们酣眠之时，营地的食品模块突然因食物烧糊而发生火灾。此外没有找到有关火灾发生原因的线索。幸而无人受伤，可考古学家们空着肚子是无法好好工作的……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1069`

### English

Overnight, as the archaeologists slept peacefully, a fire broke out in the camp's provisions module overcooking the food somewhat. There are no indications as to how the fire started. Luckily no one was harmed, but archaeologists do not work well on an empty stomach...

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1140`

## ancrel.4078.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4078.a&lang=en)

### 中文

送口粮过去。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1070`

### English

Send Provisions

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1141`

## ancrel.4078.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.4078.b&lang=en)

### 中文

真正的[owner.GetSpeciesName]都会荒野求生。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1071`

### English

A true [owner.GetSpeciesName] lives off the land.

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Athe_evermore_modifier&lang=en)

廉价的兴奋感

Cheap Thrills

## the_evermore_modifier_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Athe_evermore_modifier_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

“永恒”原本是一个虚拟世界，外星人的意识可以以数字化的形式在这里永远幸福地存在下去，现已被重新设定用途，成为大众的游乐园。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1073`

### English

The Evermore, originally a virtual realm in which the digitized consciousnesses of aliens lived in eternal bliss, has been repurposed into a playground for the masses.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1145`
