### 中文

“永恒”

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1076`

### English

The Evermore

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1148`

## study_the_sentinels_modifier_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astudy_the_sentinels_modifier_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在“永恒”文明版本的天堂中，每个人都被锁在一个永无止境的抽象桌游中，其核心是通过积累欢乐豆来统治星河。这为人类学数据提供了一个无限无聊，却也信息丰富的来源。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1077`

### English

In the Evermore paradise everyone is locked in a never-ending abstract board game centered on galactic domination through the accumulation of legumes. It makes for an endlessly dull, albeit informative, source of anthropological data.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1149`

## sentinels_planet_modifier

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asentinels_planet_modifier&lang=en)

### 中文

哨兵

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1078`

### English

The Sentinels

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1150`

## sentinels_planet_modifier_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asentinels_planet_modifier_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

监视着这颗行星的远古守护者激发了我们民众的凝聚力和灵性。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1079`

### English

Ancient guardians watch over the planet inspiring unity and spirituality in our people.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1151`

## REPLICATE_THE_EVERMORE

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AREPLICATE_THE_EVERMORE&lang=en)

### 中文

复制“永恒”

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1080`

### English

Replicate the Evermore

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1152`

## REPLICATE_THE_EVERMORE_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AREPLICATE_THE_EVERMORE_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

我们重建的“永恒”将使群众们获得更为廉价的娱乐和休闲方式，外星人们也能永远享受他们的数字乐园。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1081`

### English

A reconstruction of the Evermore will give the masses access to cheap entertainment and relaxation, leaving the aliens to enjoy their digital paradise eternally.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1153`

## REPURPOSE_THE_EVERMORE

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AREPURPOSE_THE_EVERMORE&lang=en)

### 中文

重新设定“永恒”

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1082`

### English

Repurposing the Evermore

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1154`

## REPURPOSE_THE_EVERMORE_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AREPURPOSE_THE_EVERMORE_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

我们可不能让自己受到永远挣欢乐豆的处罚。我们必须重新设定“永恒”，以便使之更好地符合我们对有意义活动的理解。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1083`

### English

We cannot sentence our own to an eternity spent counting legumes. We must repurpose the Evermore to better fit our idea of a meaningful activity.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1155`

## modern_trench_war

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amodern_trench_war&lang=en)

### 中文

现代堑壕战争

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1085`

### English

Modern Trench War

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1160`

## modern_trench_war_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amodern_trench_war_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在一颗曾被巨型蜗牛状生物包围的行星上的考古发现已经使我们对如何利用环境进行防护有了新颖而有效的洞见。这会提升我们的陆军对抗地面进攻时在防御战役中的生存率——即使是对抗巨型太空蛞蝓也不例外！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1086`

### English

Archaeological discoveries from a planet once besieged by gargantuan snail-like creatures has led to new and effective insight on how to utilize the environment for protection. This will improve the survivability of our armies in defensive combat against all ground assaults - even from giant space slugs!

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1161`

## forced_mindfulness

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aforced_mindfulness&lang=en)

### 中文

正念减压

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1088`

### English

Forced Mindfulness

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1164`

## forced_mindfulness_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aforced_mindfulness_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在被困在一个致命的、充满熔岩的迷宫中时，我们的考古团队制定了一个例行的心灵放松程序。这项实践的基本原则现已在整个帝国广为人知。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1089`

### English

During their time trapped in a deadly, lava-filled maze, our archaeology team developed a routine of mindful relaxation. The fundamental principles of this exercise is now commonly known throughout the empire.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1165`

## debug_subroutine

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adebug_subroutine&lang=en)

### 中文

调试子例程

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1090`

### English

Debug Subroutine

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1167`

## debug_subroutine_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adebug_subroutine_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

当被暂时孤立在充满熔岩的迷宫中时，我们的考古单位与网络断开，无法执行其功能。他们开发了一种算法，允许他们在睡眠模式下运行调试子例程。此子例程后来已被上载并增补入主系统中。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1091`

### English

While temporarily isolated in a maze full of lava, our archaeology units were disconnected from the network and unable to perform their function. They developed an algorithm that allowed them to run a debug subroutine while in sleep mode. This subroutine was later uploaded and patched into the main system.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1168`

## glory_to_the_many

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aglory_to_the_many&lang=en)

### 中文

荣归集体

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1092`

### English

Glory to the Many

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1170`

## glory_to_the_many_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aglory_to_the_many_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一支探险队在充满熔岩的迷宫中被困，暂时与蜂巢思维失去了联系。不能依靠集体意识，他们经历了与糟糕的自我概念的短暂遭遇；这是一次恐怖的经历，只会加强对个人主义的蔑视。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1093`

### English

An expedition marooned inside a maze full of lava temporarily lost touch with the Hive Mind. Unable to rely on the collective consciousness they suffered a brief encounter with the dreaded notion of Self; a terrifying experience that only strengthened the contempt for individualism.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1171`

## anthem_of_aurora

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanthem_of_aurora&lang=en)

### 中文

极光韵律

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1095`

### English

Anthem of Aurora

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1174`

## anthem_of_aurora_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanthem_of_aurora_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

科学部门已成功重现了曾遭遇过的北极光实体所“吟唱”的空灵赞美诗。所有能源生产设施现在都有谐波系统以增加产量。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1096`

### English

The science division has managed to reproduce the ethereal hymn "sung" by an encountered aurora borealis entity. All Energy production facilities now have harmonics systems that amplifies the yield.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1175`

## ancrel.6500.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.6500.name&lang=en)

### 中文

在[planet.GetName]上缴获遗珍

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1097`

### English

Relic Seized on [planet.GetName]

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1177`

## ancrel.6500.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.6500.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

随着我们牢牢掌控[planet.GetName]，我们得知我们的军队在战斗中取得了一个名为§H[this.GetLastReceivedRelic]§!的无价遗珍的控制权。显然，[victim.GetName]正准备将这个遗珍撤离到安全的地方，但是我们在[planet.GetPlanetMoon]上战线的快速推进使得这个选择不再现实。\n\n遗珍现已被我们安全转移到位于[Root.Capital.GetName]上的中央档案馆。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1098`

### English

With [planet.GetName] firmly under our control, we have learned that our forces came into possession of a priceless relic, known as §H[this.GetLastReceivedRelic]§!, during the fighting. The [victim.GetName] was evidently in the process of evacuating this relic to a safe location, but the rapidly shifting front lines on the [planet.GetPlanetMoon]'s surface made this impossible.\n\nThe relic has been safely transferred to our central archives on [Root.Capital.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1178`

## ancrel.6500.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.6500.a&lang=en)

### 中文

[this.GetLastReceivedRelic]是我们的了！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1099`

### English

[this.GetLastReceivedRelic] is ours!

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1179`

## stole_relic

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astole_relic&lang=en)

### 中文

我们已经从§Y[victim.GetName]§!手里取得§Y[this.GetLastReceivedRelic]§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1100`

### English

We have acquired §Y[this.GetLastReceivedRelic]§! from the §Y[victim.GetName]§!

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1180`

## ancrel.6505.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.6505.name&lang=en)

### 中文

于[planet.GetName]失去遗珍！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1101`

### English

Relic Lost on [planet.GetName]!

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1181`

## ancrel.6505.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.6505.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

祸不单行，继[planet.GetName]沦陷之后，紧接便是我们伟大的首都[From.GetName]。我们努力撤离§H[this.GetLastLostRelic]§!，可惜却失败了，其专用的运输穿梭机在中央档案馆附近的混战中被击落。\n\n我们现已确定[From.GetSpeciesNamePlural]将该遗珍运送至[From.Capital.GetName]，即他们的首都。我们已经失去它了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1102`

### English

Yet another tragedy has followed on the heels of the recent fall of [planet.GetName], our great capital, to the [From.GetName]. Our efforts to evacuate §H[this.GetLastLostRelic]§! failed when its designated transport shuttle was brought down in the confused fighting near the central archives.\n\nWe now have confirmation that the [From.GetSpeciesNamePlural] have moved the relic to [From.Capital.GetName], their capital. It is lost to us.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1182`

## ancrel.6505.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.6505.a&lang=en)

### 中文

[this.GetLastLostRelic]不再属于我们……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1103`

### English

[this.GetLastLostRelic] is no longer ours...

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1183`

## relic_lost

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Arelic_lost&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[this.GetLastLostRelic]§!现在属于§Y[From.GetName]§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1104`

### English

§Y[this.GetLastLostRelic]§! now belongs to the §Y[From.GetName]§!

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1184`

## site_the_library

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asite_the_library&lang=en)

### 中文

图书馆

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1106`

### English

The Library

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1187`

## site_the_library_intro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asite_the_library_intro&lang=en)

### 中文

一层厚厚的玻璃和金属覆盖在这颗原本贫瘠的行星上，或许暗示着它过去发生了某种灾难性事件。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1107`

### English

A thick layer of glass and metal covers this otherwise barren planet, perhaps hinting at some sort of cataclysmic event in its past.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1188`

## ancrel.12020.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.12020.name&lang=en)

### 中文

被遗忘已久的废墟

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1108`

### English

Ruins Long Forgotten

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1189`

## ancrel.12020.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.12020.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们发现了明显的迹象，迹象表明[From.Planet.GetName]曾经人口稠密。然而，尽管不知道是什么摧毁了他们文明，摧毁都做得非常彻底：几乎没有发现有用的遗骸。\n\n我们的扫描仪在地表深处探测到几个可能曾经支持生命的大洞。也许我们会在那里有更多的运气。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1109`

### English

We have discovered clear signs that [From.Planet.GetName] was once densely populated. However, whatever destroyed their civilization did a very thorough job of it: very few useful remains have been found.\n\nOur scanners have detected several large cavities deep below the surface that may once have supported life. Perhaps we will have more luck there.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1190`

## ancrel.12025.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.12025.name&lang=en)

### 中文

图书馆

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1110`

### English

The Library

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1191`

## ancrel.12025.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.12025.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们对[From.Planet.GetName]上的洞穴系统的挖掘发现了保存完好、规模宏大的地下高科技社会遗迹。许多废墟的状况出奇的好，我们设法找到了[Owner.GetResearchers]所描述的加密数据水晶“图书馆”。希望这些可以对这里发生的事情有所了解。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1111`

### English

Our excavation of the cave systems on [From.Planet.GetName] has found well-preserved remnants of an underground, high technology society built on a vast scale. Many of the ruins are in surprisingly good condition, and we have managed to find what our [Owner.GetResearchers] are describing as a "library" of encrypted data crystals. Hopefully, these can shed some light on what happened here.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1192`

## ancrel.12030.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.12030.name&lang=en)

### 中文

暗影中潜伏

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1112`

### English

Lurking in the Shadows

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1193`

## ancrel.12030.desc.base

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.12030.desc.base&lang=en)

### 中文

一个在[FromFrom.Planet.GetName]上探索废墟的[Owner.GetTeamMember]最近失踪了。经过短暂的搜索，我们找到了一具可能属于[Root.Owner.Species.GetName]的[Owner.GetSpeciesRemnantName]。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1113`

### English

A [Owner.GetTeamMember] exploring the ruins on [FromFrom.Planet.GetName] recently disappeared. After a short search, we found a [Owner.GetSpeciesRemnantName] that seems to have belonged to a [Root.Owner.Species.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1194`

## ancrel.12030.desc.normal

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.12030.desc.normal&lang=en)

### 中文

$ancrel.12030.desc.base$我们的[Owner.GetResearchers]毫不怀疑这些遗骸是我们失踪的[Owner.GetTeamMember]的遗骸，但他们对尸体如何如此迅速地分解感到困惑。他们敦促我们在接下来的调查中保持谨慎。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1114`

### English

$ancrel.12030.desc.base$ Our [Owner.GetResearchers] are in no doubt that these remains are what's left of our missing [Owner.GetTeamMember], but they are baffled about how the body decayed quite this quickly. They urge caution as we continue our investigations.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1195`

## ancrel.12030.desc.lithoid

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.12030.desc.lithoid&lang=en)

### 中文

$ancrel.12030.desc.base$我们的[Owner.GetResearchers]毫不怀疑这些遗骸是我们失踪的[Owner.GetTeamMember]的遗骸，但他们对究竟是什么杀死了他们感到困惑——这个过程似乎相当地迅速。他们敦促我们在接下来的调查中保持谨慎。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1115`

### English

$ancrel.12030.desc.base$ Our [Owner.GetResearchers] are in no doubt that these remains are what's left of our missing [Owner.GetTeamMember], but they are baffled as to what exactly killed them - the process seems to have been fairly instantaneous. They urge caution as we continue our investigations.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1196`

## ancrel.12030.desc.machine

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.12030.desc.machine&lang=en)

### 中文

$ancrel.12030.desc.base$我们的[Owner.GetResearchers]毫不怀疑这些遗骸是我们失踪的[Owner.GetTeamMember]的遗骸，但他们对他们的单元如何如此迅速地腐蚀感到困惑。他们敦促我们在接下来的调查中保持谨慎。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1116`

### English

$ancrel.12030.desc.base$ Our [Owner.GetResearchers] are in no doubt that these remains are what's left of our missing [Owner.GetTeamMember], but they are baffled as to how their unit corroded away quite this quickly. They urge caution as we continue our investigations.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1197`

## ancrel.12035.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.12035.name&lang=en)

### 中文

乐园的烦恼

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1117`

### English

Troubles in Paradise

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1198`

## ancrel.12035.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.12035.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们解析来自[From.Planet.GetName]洞穴中的数据水晶时，拼凑出了一个陷入困境的星球的景象。\n\n这个星球曾经是一个繁荣的文明的中心，被称为伊兰尼基金会。在那个文明的后期，伊兰尼人越来越倾向于囤积知识，而不关心物质问题，比如生育。结果，他们的人口迅速减少。他们的领袖对他们不断减少的人数感到震惊，他们试图找到永生的技术解决方案。在这一点上，记录变得越来越稀少——尽管它似乎早于迫使他们进入地下的某种灾难——目前尚不清楚他们的努力有什么结果。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1118`

### English

As we interpret the data crystals from the caves on [From.Planet.GetName], we begin piecing together a picture of a troubled planet.\n\nThe planet was once the center of a prosperous civilization known as the Ilanii Foundation. During that civilization's latter years, the Ilanii turned more and more to the hoarding of knowledge instead of more material matters such as procreation. As a result, their population fell rapidly. Alarmed by their dwindling numbers, their leaders attempted to find a technological solution for immortality. Records become scarcer around this point - though it appears to predate whatever cataclysm forced them underground - and it is not clear yet what results their efforts yielded.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1199`

## ancrel.12040.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.12040.name&lang=en)

### 中文

文明的终结

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1119`

### English

Civilization's End

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1200`

## ancrel.12040.desc.base

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.12040.desc.base&lang=en)

### 中文

我们现在可以拼凑出伊兰尼尝试永生的结果了。看来，他们远没有永生，而是制造了一种可怕的威胁——一群能够快速繁殖并且几乎立即吞噬任何有机物的微生物……就像我们团队失踪成员身上发生过的事情那样。\n\n由此产生的大灾难消灭了大部分人口。幸存者分裂成两组：其中一组，不愿放弃[From.Planet.GetName]，遁入深处，在我们发现的那个密不透风的洞穴中重建他们的文明。然而，从长远来看，这种解决方案被证明是不可持续的，最终微生物找到了进入它们的栖息地的方法。洞穴成了他们的坟墓。\n\n另一组流亡星海，通过彻底摧毁地表使其无法居住的方法来强制执行行星隔离。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1120`

### English

We can now piece together what became of the Ilanii's attempt at immortality. It appears that, far from bringing them everlasting life, they created a terrible menace - a swarm of microorganisms capable of multiplying rapidly and almost instantly devouring any organic matter... as must have happened to our team's lost member.\n\nThe resulting cataclysm wiped out much of the population. The survivors splintered into two groups: one, unwilling to give up [From.Planet.GetName], took to the depths, recreating their civilization in air-tight pockets such as the one we found. However, this solution proved unsustainable in the long run, and eventually the microorganism found its way into their habitats; the caves became their tombs.\n\nThe other group instead went into exile, enforcing a planetary quarantine by utterly obliterating the surface, rendering it uninhabitable.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1201`

## ancrel.12040.desc.curators

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.12040.desc.curators&lang=en)

### 中文

$ancrel.12040.desc.base$ 从此以后，他们将生活在小飞地中，并致力于保存他们文明中失落的知识，最终演变成现代§H[curator_enclave_country.GetName]§! 的祖先之一。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1121`

### English

$ancrel.12040.desc.base$ They would henceforth live in small enclaves and dedicate themselves to preserving the lost knowledge of their civilization, eventually morphing into one of the ancestors of the modern §H[curator_enclave_country.GetName]§!.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1202`

## ancrel.12040.desc.meet_curators

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.12040.desc.meet_curators&lang=en)

### 中文

$ancrel.12040.desc.base$从今以后，他们将生活在小飞地中，致力于保护他们文明中失落的知识。我们不知道他们的最终命运，但其中一块数据水晶表明他们正前往§H[curator_poi.GetName]§! 恒星系。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1122`

### English

$ancrel.12040.desc.base$ They would henceforth live in small enclaves and dedicate themselves to preserving the lost knowledge of their civilization. Their ultimate fate is unknown to us, but one of the data crystals indicates they were headed for the §H[curator_poi.GetName]§! system.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1203`

## ancrel.12040.desc.no_curators

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.12040.desc.no_curators&lang=en)

### 中文

$ancrel.12040.desc.base$ 他们的最终命运遗失在历史的长河中，但似乎他们曾打算将他们毕生奉献于保存他们文明失落的知识中。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1123`

### English

$ancrel.12040.desc.base$ Their ultimate fate is lost to time, but it seems they intended to dedicate the remainder of their existence to preserving the lost knowledge of their civilization.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1204`

## killer_librarian_organism_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Akiller_librarian_organism_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的陆军装备了一种有机微生物，能够以凶残的速度吞噬任何生物的生命组织。当然，使用这种武器不能完全保证周围无辜群众的安全……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1130`

### English

Our armies go into battle armed with a microorganism capable of devouring the life tissue of any being with ferocious speed. Of course, they cannot entirely guarantee the safety of innocent bystanders as a result of its use...

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1213`

### English

Ruined Station

## site_percussive_maintenance_intro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asite_percussive_maintenance_intro&lang=en)

### 中文

一个与中子星非常接近的大型空间站正在发出奇怪的读数。尽管其当前位置稳定，但显示出相当大的损坏。焦痕的扩散表明它在过去曾被几次大爆炸所摧毁。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1137`

### English

A large station orbiting in alarmingly close proximity to the neutron star is giving off strange readings. Although its current position is stable, it shows considerable wear and tear. A proliferation of scorch marks indicates that it was rocked by several large explosions in the past.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1222`

## ancrel.12000.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.12000.name&lang=en)

### 中文

实验型装备

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1138`

### English

Experimental Weaponry

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1224`

## ancrel.12000.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.12000.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.Leader.GetName]与一组研究人员登上了空间站，并设法恢复了某些部分的供电。似乎在 [Root.Owner.GetSpeciesNamePlural]进入太空之前仅仅几个世纪就已经有人居住了。乍一看，它似乎是一个武装空间站，占其大部分体积的超大大炮证明了这一点。然而，其余大部分区域似乎都专门设计为研究设施，这表明该武器很可能是高度实验性的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1139`

### English

[Root.Leader.GetName] has boarded the station with a team of researchers and managed to restore power to some sections. It appears that it was inhabited mere centuries before [Root.Owner.GetSpeciesNamePlural] took to space. At first sight, it appears to have been a weapons station, as evidenced by the exceptionally large cannon that takes up the majority of its volume. However, most of the remaining sections seem to have been dedicated to research facilities, indicating that the weapon was likely highly experimental.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1225`

## ancrel.12005.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.12005.name&lang=en)

### 中文

系统失效

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1140`

### English

System Failures

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1226`

## ancrel.12005.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.12005.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.Leader.GetName]不禁注意到，即便考虑到空间站的年龄，其明显未损坏的系统绝大部分似乎以过高的频率出现故障。\n\n在无数次重新激活它们后，团队的计算技术人员发出痛苦的尖叫：“这是啥？为啥？咋整？！”一时间，痛苦和怀疑的声音有增无减，[Root.Leader.GetSheHe]摇了摇[Root.Leader.GetHerHis]的头，宣布失败。“似乎整个设施都是用导管胶带糊在一起的，”[Root.Leader.GetSheHe]说。“我很震惊这玩意儿曾经能用。”

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1141`

### English

[Root.Leader.GetName] can't help but notice that an abnormally large number of apparently undamaged systems in the station seem to be failing with excessive frequency, even allowing for their age.\n\nUpon reactivating them for the umpteenth time, agonized screams are heard from the team's comp tech: "What? Why? How?!" The stream of distressed and incredulous noises continues unabated for some time before [Root.Leader.GetSheHe] shakes [Root.Leader.GetHerHis] head, defeated. "It seems this entire facility was cobbled together with duct tape," [Root.Leader.GetSheHe] says. "I'm surprised it ever worked."

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1227`

## ancrel.12005.desc.gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.12005.desc.gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

我们注意到，这个空间站上大量看似完好的系统似乎都在发生灾难性故障，其频率远超预期——即便考虑到它们的年代也是如此。\n\n在几次艰难的系统重启尝试后，我们的一名维修工[root.GetIndividualNamePlural]发生了严重崩溃：\"超出理性参数范围，\"它开始不停重复这句话。这种状态持续了一段时间，最终[root.GetIndividualName]被迫完全放弃了尝试，让我们不得不得出结论：这个设施的程序设计根本不具备可用性。我们甚至无法理解这个设施曾经是如何正常运转的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1142`

### English

We note that a large number of apparently undamaged systems aboard this station seem to be suffering catastrophic failures, far more frequently than expected - even allowing for their age.\n\nFollowing several arduous attempts to reactivate these systems, one of our repair [root.GetIndividualNamePlural] suffered a critical meltdown: "Context exceeds parameters of reason," it began bleating. This continued for some time before the [root.GetIndividualName] was forced to abandoned the attempt altogether, resigning us to a conclusion that the facility programming is not fit for purpose. We cannot fathom how this facility ever functioned properly.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1228`

### English

The Rudari Gambit

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1232`

## ancrel.12010.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.12010.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

尽管过程经常中断，[Root.Leader.GetName]已在破译该空间站的用途方面取得重大进展。日志表明，它的创造者“鲁达里”一直在与一个被称为“波格-莫里”灭杀种族进行长期斗争——一场他们即将输掉的战争。为了在绝望的最后一搏中扭转局势，一群激进的鲁达里政治家得出结论，保证他们生存的唯一方法是彻底消灭敌人。由于无法通过常规手段击败波格-莫里舰队，他们资助了一个绝密项目，旨在制造一种能够利用恒星的力量在跨恒星系空间发射的激光武器。然而，虽然他们可以在这个问题上投入大量资源，但该项目成立地较为仓促，并且不断有危险发生——无论是被敌人还是被他们自己政府内不愿屈服于此类措施的派系。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1147`

### English

Despite the frequent outages, [Root.Leader.GetName] has been able to make significant progress in deciphering the purpose of the station. Logs indicate that its creators, the "Rudari," had been engaged in a long struggle with a genocidal race known as the "Pogg-Mory" - a war they were losing. In a desperate last-ditch effort to turn the tide, a radical group of Rudari politicians concluded that the only way to guarantee their survival was through the total annihilation of the enemy. Unable to defeat the Pogg-Mory fleet by conventional means, they funded a top secret project to build a laser weapon capable of firing across interstellar space by harnessing the power of a star. However, it appears that while they could throw considerable resources at the problem, the project was hastily set up and in constant danger of discovery - either by the enemy or by factions within their own government unwilling to stoop to such measures.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1233`

## ancrel.12010.A

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.12010.A&lang=en)

### 中文

这听起来也太冒险了……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1148`

### English

This sounds risky...

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1234`

## ancrel.12015.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.12015.name&lang=en)

### 中文

梦的破碎

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1149`

### English

The Failure of a Dream

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1235`

## ancrel.12015.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.12015.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.Leader.GetName]发现了鲁达里首席科学官的个人日志，这些日志看起来是一连串对该项目看起来不可能的要求以及迅速下降的质量和安全标准的抱怨。“没有时间测试任何东西，”最后一篇文章中写道。“这是我族人的最后一搏，敌人已经察觉到了我们。我们必须希望到此为止了。他们来了。”\n\n日志表明由于该武器在那不久之后进行测试，所有记录都将停止于此。有证据表明，超级武器仅仅是成功摧毁了空间站。鲁达里似乎将其位置的秘密带到了他们的坟墓中——阻止我们找到他们的遗族。\n\n空间站程序和总体状况都不足以让我们冒险尝试重新启动它并完成这个项目。然而，“死亡激光”中使用的一些创新概念为我们自己的武器专家提供了宝贵的见解。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1150`

### English

[Root.Leader.GetName] has uncovered the Rudari Chief Science Officer's personal logs, which read as a litany of complaints about the seemingly impossible demands of the project and their rapidly falling quality and safety standards. "There is no time to test anything," reads one of the last entries. "This is the last gambit of my people, and the enemy has our scent. We must hope that this is enough. They are coming."\n\nThe logs indicate that the weapon was due to be tested soon after, at which point all records cease. Evidence suggests that the superweapon succeeded only in destroying the station. The Rudari seemingly took the secret of its location to their graves - allowing us to find its remains undisturbed.\n\nNeither the programming nor the general condition of the station are good enough for us to risk trying to reactivate it and complete the project. However, some of the innovative concepts used in the "Death Laser" have provided our own weapons specialists with valuable insights.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1236`

鲁达里死亡激光

Rudari Death Laser

## rudari_death_laser_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Arudari_death_laser_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

当他们的文明在敌军的猛攻下崩溃时，鲁达里人试图制造一种能够跨恒星系部署的激光武器。尽管他们在这方面的努力失败了，但他们的研究对我们自己的武器专家非常有用。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1157`

### English

As their civilization crumbled under the onslaught of enemy forces, the Rudari attempted to create a laser weapon capable of being deployed at interstellar range. Although they failed in this endeavor, their research has proved extremely useful to our own weapons specialists.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1244`

## abandoned_weapon_station

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aabandoned_weapon_station&lang=en)

### 中文

废弃武装空间站

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1158`

### English

Abandoned Weapons Station

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1246`

## abandoned_weapon_station_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aabandoned_weapon_station_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一个废弃武装空间站在[star.GetName]近轨道环绕。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1159`

### English

An abandoned weapons station orbiting in close proximity to [star.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1247`

## ancrel.12060.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.12060.name&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.GetName]恒星系

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1160`

### English

The [From.GetName] System

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1249`

## ancrel.12060.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.12060.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.GetName]恒星系笼罩着诡异的光芒。[Root.GetName]突然出现在星系的某个区域，我们确信这里此前数代人甚至数千年间从未被星系文明造访过。\n\n但等一下，我们的传感器发现了一个能量信号！该信号主要集中在星系中的第一颗行星上，这颗行星似乎不太可能支持生命，毕竟它距离中子星如此接近。但好像还有其他信号正在移动。它们直奔[Root.GetName]而去，武器随时准备开火！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1161`

### English

An eerie glow lies over the [From.GetName] system. The [Root.GetName] has emerged in a sector of the galaxy surely not visited by any galactic civilization for generations - for millennia... if ever.\n\nBut wait - our sensors are reporting energy signatures! Most are concentrated on the first planet of the system, an unlikely candidate for life, considering its proximity to a neutron star. But there are others, mobile ones. They are careening towards the [Root.GetName], weapons charged!

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1250`

## ancrel.12065.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.12065.name&lang=en)

### 中文

[fromfrom.System.GetName]大捷

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1162`

### English

Victory in [fromfrom.System.GetName]

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1251`

## ancrel.12065.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.12065.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们已经击败了[fromfrom.System.GetName]的防御者。这个恒星系里的景象真是光怪陆离：固态物质几乎不存在，即便是小行星带都残破不堪，就像是被人彻底地开采枯竭了一般。一系列巨型装置按照特定的间距环绕着行星，而信号显示的那一颗孤零零的行星也许是人为制造的。其中释放出了强大的能量信号。如果有什么线索的话一定能在那里找到。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1163`

### English

We have defeated the defenders of [fromfrom.System.GetName]. The system is a curious sight: there is very little solid matter remaining in it - even its asteroid belts have a bedraggled look, as if they have been extensively mined. A series of large installations placed at regular intervals around the star, and there is a single planet that our readings suggest may be artificial in origin. Intense energy signals have been detected emanating from it. If answers are to be found, they will be there.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1252`

## site_ancient_robot_world

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asite_ancient_robot_world&lang=en)

### 中文

远古机器人星球

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1164`

### English

Ancient Robot World

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1254`

## site_ancient_robot_world_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asite_ancient_robot_world_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.Planet.System.GetName]恒星系中孤零零的这颗行星上住满了无人机。它们对我们毫不关心，各自匆匆忙忙做着自己的事情。如果关于这个奇怪的星系有什么线索的话一定能在那里找到。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1165`

### English

The sole planet in the [From.Planet.System.GetName] system is bustling with drones. Heedless of us, they continue to perform their tasks. If the answers to this curious system are to be found anywhere, it is here.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1255`

## ancrel.12070.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.12070.name&lang=en)

### 中文

攒动的人工生命

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1166`

### English

Teeming with Artificial Life

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1256`

## ancrel.12070.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.12070.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

目前为止，我们的[Owner.GetResearchers]所能推断的是，这个被他们乌鸦嘴般称为“§Y观测终端§!”的行星上从未有过生命的存在。但其上并不缺活动迹象。地表上有稠密的建筑物，大量人造建筑鳞次栉比地排列在荒芜的基岩上，这些规整的阵列几乎覆盖了行星四分之三的地表。数以亿计的无人机正在对其进行维护，执行的任务从简单的清扫到运行高度复杂的装配线皆包含在内。\n\n然而它们的真正作用目前尚不明确。我们的[Owner.GetResearchers]准备派遣一支队伍下到地表以进行进一步调查。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1167`

### English

As far as our [Owner.GetResearchers] can tell, there has never been life on the planet they have ominously dubbed the "§YObservation Terminal§!". But there is no shortage of activity. The surface is densely built up, massive artificial structures placed upon the bare bedrock in neat, orderly rows that cover almost three quarters of the planet. Billions of mechanical drones maintain them, performing tasks ranging from menial clean-up to running highly-complex assembly lines.\n\nTheir exact purpose remains, however, unclear. Our [Owner.GetResearchers] are preparing to send a team to the surface to investigate further.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1257`

## ancrel.12075.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.12075.name&lang=en)

### 中文

无人机调查

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1168`

### English

Investigating the Drones

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1258`

## ancrel.12075.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.12075.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这些机器人对我们的[Owner.GetResearchers]毫不关心，即使从它们的身边路过也继续执行着各自的命令。与这些无人机的一切沟通尝试都失败了，即便是尝试打断他们的工作也毫无收获，这些无人机毫不躲闪直接强行冲撞挡路的[Owner.GetResearchers]，造成了严重的损伤。\n\n打那以后，我们的[Owner.GetResearchers]转而将目光放在操控这些无人机的程序上。通过分析拦截到的信号，我们推断其中有一个主宰一切的系统在操控这些无人机。然而，对其进行解码非常困难，可能很难再有进一步的发现了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1169`

### English

As our [Owner.GetResearchers] pass amongst them, the drones on the planet ignore their presence and continue going about their tasks. All efforts to communicate with the drones have failed, and even attempts to disrupt them produced no reactions - the drones simply rode roughshod over the interfering [Owner.GetResearchers], causing serious injury.\n\nSince then, our [Owner.GetResearchers] have shifted their focus to the programs steering the drones. Analysis of intercepted signals suggests there is an overarching system steering them. However, the encryption it uses is extremely sophisticated, and it has proven hard to find out more.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1259`

## ancrel.12080.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.12080.name&lang=en)

### 中文

恒星系探索

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1170`

### English

Systemic Exploration

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1260`

## ancrel.12080.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.12080.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

尽管我们的[Owner.GetResearchers]已经难以进一步深入调查这颗行星，但还有一支队伍还在继续研究散布在整个恒星系中的物体阵列。总共约有超过一万个这样的物体，它们大部分是长圆柱体外形的装置，似乎仍在运行，持续不断地向行星发送信号。鉴于它们的形状以及它们似乎无一例外地指向我们星系中的各个点的事实，我们无可避免地得出这是一个出奇巨大的传感器阵列的结论。但它会是由谁建造的呢？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1171`

### English

While our [Owner.GetResearchers] have been unable to make further progress investigating the planet, a team has been investigating the array of objects spread across the system. There are over ten thousand of them, all told. Most of them are long and cylindrical devices that still seem to be operating - they are transmitting a signal towards the planet. Given their shape and the fact that they seem to be without exception pointed towards various points in our galaxy, we cannot escape the conclusion that this is an absurdly huge sensor array. But who could have built it?

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1261`

## ancrel.12085.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.12085.name&lang=en)

### 中文

钓鱼执法

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1172`

### English

Time to Phish

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1262`

## ancrel.12085.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.12085.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在一次大胆的行动中，我们的[Owner.GetResearchers]趁着附近没有其他无人机存在时，成功地向一处无人机主机的接入终端植入了窃听装置。该入侵目前还没有被发现，我们对其的印象是这是一个高度复杂的系统，甚至可能远超我们的网络，但完全没有考虑到自卫的需求。毫无疑问他们认为这些防御舰队的武力吓止就已经非常充分了。尽管如此，我们入侵的这个系统庞大得难以置信，甚至庞大这个词用来描述这个涵盖整颗行星的系统来说都有些苍白无力，所以我们需要一些时间来获取更多信息。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1173`

### English

In a daring move, our [Owner.GetResearchers] managed to implant spyware into an access terminal to the drones' mainframe in a moment while no drones were nearby. The intrusion does not appear to have been detected yet. Our impression is of a highly sophisticated system - beyond even our own networks, probably - but one which did not anticipate the need to protect itself. No doubt the physical deterrence of the defense fleet was deemed sufficient. Nevertheless, the systems we are infiltrating are vast - a term which seems a gross understatement for this planetary system - so it will take some time to find out more.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1263`

## ancrel.12090.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.12090.name&lang=en)

### 中文

超级监察计算机

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1174`

### English

The Surveillance Supercomputer

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1264`

## ancrel.12090.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.12090.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

位于$NAME_Ultima_Vigilis$的自动化系统似乎比我们所能想象的还要古老。我们黑入这个系统取得了数据，可是其中并没有能够帮助我们确认其建造者身份或最终意图的信息。关于系统的建造者，我们唯一可以确定的事情就是他们至少在12亿年前就已经来到了这里，在建造完系统并将其设定为监测整个星系的观测站后就离开了。\n\n值得一提的是，经历如此漫长的岁月后，这个系统仍在运作。遍布整个系统的装置阵列实际上是高度精密的传感器，它们会向该星球发送实时数据，而在星球上的中央处理系统则会将这些数据转化为无人机可以保存并长期存储的数据。由于受到存储空间与物资短缺的影响，他们似乎下决心改进了数据压缩技术。这个星球上的巨型结构自称其存储了自建造以来的所有观测数据，不过我们却无法证实这一点，毕竟这些数据被过度压缩，已经超出我们的解压能力了。\n\n令人不安的是，尽管发送频率下降，但其依旧在向外界广播“重点”数据流。不过都过了这么久了，应该也没人在监听了吧？不管怎样，我们已经准备好程序接管这些无人机，只要有胆量，我们马上就可以根据我们自己的设想改造这个系统。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1175`

### English

It seems the automated systems in $NAME_Ultima_Vigilis$ are older than we can imagine. The data systems we have hacked do not give us any clues as to the identity or ultimate intentions of its creators, but the one thing we have managed to ascertain is that they arrived no less than 1.2 billion years ago, and departed soon after setting up the system as an observation post to surveil our entire galaxy.\n\nRemarkably, the system is still operating even after all these years: the array of installations spread across the system are, it turns out, highly sophisticated sensors. They send a live feed to the planet, where the central operating system processes it into information the drones can save and store. Spurred on by shortages of space and matter, it appears that they have improved their data compression techniques with determination: the massive structures on the planet purport to contain the sum of all observations ever recorded. However, we cannot test this assertion, since the data is compressed well beyond our ability to decompress it.\n\nDisturbingly, a reduced stream of "highlights" is still being broadcast into empty space. But surely no one is still listening, after all this time? Regardless, our programs are poised to take control of the drones. We may now shape the system to our imagination - if we dare.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1265`

## ancrel.12095.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.12095.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

“你们一点也不顾念——”\n\n§B传输数据流乱码了一段时间，无法输出有效信息。§!\n\n“你们难道没有想过有人正在接受那个你漫不经心就切断掉的数据流？行吧，既然你们已经证明自己是一群危险而具有攻击性的害虫……那么是时候叫§R害虫防治§!过来了。准备好迎接末日吧。”

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1184`

### English

"You inconsiderate-"\n\n§BThe transmission feed is garbled for a time, and no sense can be made of the noises heard.§!\n\n"Did you never consider that someone might be watching that signal feed you so carelessly cut off? Well, since you have proven yourselves to be dangerously aggressive vermin... it's time to call the §RPEST CONTROL§!. Prepare to face your doom."

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1275`

### 中文

爆炸性减压

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1189`

### English

Explosive Decompression

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1281`

## ancrel.8200.intro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.8200.intro&lang=en)

### 中文

我们对 [System.GetName] 恒星系中空间站进行调查的期间，团队无意中导致船体的一部分区段失去完整性，引起了爆炸性减压。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1190`

### English

During our survey of the station in the [System.GetName] system, our team inadvertently caused a section of the hull to lose integrity, triggering an explosive decompression.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1282`

## ancrel.8200.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.8200.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$ancrel.8200.intro$\n\n所有人都无能为力 —— 一瞬间，§H[dead_scientist.GetName]§! 和其他几个 [Root.Owner.GetArchaeologistPlural] 被卷入了裂口，直接导致他们死亡。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1191`

### English

$ancrel.8200.intro$\n\nThere was nothing anyone could have done - within moments, §H[dead_scientist.GetName]§! and several other [Root.Owner.GetArchaeologistPlural] were swept through the breach to their deaths.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1283`

## ancrel.8201.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.8201.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$ancrel.8200.intro$\n\n一瞬间，几个[Root.Owner.GetArchaeologistPlural]被卷入了裂口，直接导致他们死亡。幸运的是，§H[Root.GetLeaderName]§!当时在空间站的不同区段，但这对调查工作来说仍是一个重大挫折。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1192`

### English

$ancrel.8200.intro$\n\nWithin moments, several [Root.Owner.GetArchaeologistPlural] were swept through the breach to their deaths. Fortunately, §H[Root.GetLeaderName]§! was in a different section of the station at the time, but this is nevertheless a significant setback for the investigation effort.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1284`

## ancrel.8202.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.8202.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$ancrel.8200.intro$\n\n幸运的是，由于我们的团队能够逃到空间站未受影响的区域，事故中无人丧生。似乎也没有重要设备损坏，因此调查不会因此面临任何不应有的挫折。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1193`

### English

$ancrel.8200.intro$\n\nFortunately, no lives were lost in the incident, as our team was able to escape to unaffected areas of the station. It seems no vital equipment was damaged either, so the investigation will not face any undue setbacks as a result.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1285`

## ancrel.8202.desc.robot

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.8202.desc.robot&lang=en)

### 中文

$ancrel.8200.intro$\n\n幸运的是，我们的[Owner.GetArchaeologistPlural]没有受到伤害，因为它们的建造出来就可以在太空中生存一段时间。因此，他们能够不受阻碍地继续他们的工作。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1194`

### English

$ancrel.8200.intro$\n\nFortunately, our [Owner.GetArchaeologistPlural] were unharmed, as they have been built to survive in open space for a time. They were therefore able continue their work undeterred.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1286`

## ancrel.8203.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.8203.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$ancrel.8200.intro$\n\n幸运的是，由于我们的团队能够逃到空间站未受影响的区域，事故中无人丧生。然而，减压的力量损坏了他们一直在工作的几个控制台，需要花费大量资金进行维修。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1195`

### English

$ancrel.8200.intro$\n\nFortunately, no lives were lost in the incident, as our team was able to escape to unaffected areas of the station. However, the force of the decompression damaged several of the consoles they had been working on, and will cost a considerable amount to be repaired.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1287`

## ancrel.8203.desc.robot

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.8203.desc.robot&lang=en)

### 中文

$ancrel.8200.intro$\n\n幸运的是，我们的[Owner.GetArchaeologistPlural]没有受到伤害，因为它们的建造出来就可以在太空中生存一段时间。然而，减压的力量损坏了他们一直在工作的几个控制台，需要花费大量资金进行维修。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1196`

### English

$ancrel.8200.intro$\n\nFortunately, our [Owner.GetArchaeologistPlural] were unharmed, as they have been built to survive in open space for a time. However, the force of the decompression damaged several of the consoles they had been working on, and will cost a considerable amount to be repaired.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1288`

我们将提供他们需要的所有资金。

## ancrel.8210.1.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.8210.1.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们对[fromfrom.planet.system.GetName]恒星系中空间站的遗迹开展了持续性研究，并在这个过程中发现了少量密藏的古董物品。首席考古学家[root.leader.GetName]在其中发现了几件稀有文物。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1199`

### English

Our continued study of the remnants of the station in the [fromfrom.planet.system.GetName] system has unearthed a small cache of antique objects. Chief archaeologist [root.leader.GetName] has identified several minor artifacts among them.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1291`

## ancrel.8210.2.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.8210.2.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

当我们的[Root.Owner.GetArchaeologistPlural]正在调查附近的控制台时，空间站的一部分墙壁突然倒塌。然而，事实证明我们撞了好运，收纳有几个稀有文物的隐藏隔间随即被发现了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1200`

### English

A section of the station's walls suddenly collapsed as our [Root.Owner.GetArchaeologistPlural] were investigating a nearby console. This proved a fortuitous event however, as a hidden compartment concealing several minor artifacts was revealed.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1292`

## ancrel.8215.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.8215.name&lang=en)

### 中文

系统关闭

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1201`

### English

System Lockout

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1294`

## ancrel.8215.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.8215.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

查看空间站的电子设备时，我们的[Owner.GetArchaeologistPlural]之一意外触发了其创建者留下的一些仍然有效的安全措施。这导致了在部署正确的对策之前，我们的团队在很长一段时间内都被排除在空间站系统之外。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1202`

### English

As they were poking through the station's electronics, one of our [Owner.GetArchaeologistPlural] accidentally triggered some still-functional security measures left by its creators. As a result, our team were locked out from the station's systems for a considerable time before the right countermeasures could be deployed.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1295`

## polaris_seed_vault_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apolaris_seed_vault_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

漂浮在 [From.Planet.GetName] 行星残骸水晶的般清澈的海洋上，包裹在微小冰盖的，似乎是一个人造结构。\n\n需要一个团队来挖掘这个结构。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1205`

### English

Encapsulated in what remains of the planet's tiny ice cap, floating on the crystal clear oceans of [From.Planet.GetName], is what appears to be an artificial structure. \n\nA team is required to excavate this structure.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1299`

## ancrel.13000.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.13000.name&lang=en)

冷酷的招待会

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1206`

### English

A Cool Reception

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1301`

## ancrel.13000.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.13000.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在勾陈一的最后一个侵略者倒下后，我们的舰船在返回港口之前完成了对恒星系的最后一次扫描，并发现了一些奇怪的东西。在勾陈一 II 的星球上，我们的传感器显示有重金属被排列成了人造结构。有趣的是，这些构造中中似乎只有一个漂浮在行星海洋上，而其余的则在海面以下。我们怀疑这是外星生命的杰作。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1207`

### English

After the last of the aggressors in Polaris fell, our ships completed a final scan of the system before heading back to port and discovered something curious. On the world of Polaris II, heavy metals are arranged in artificial structures on our sensors. Interestingly only one of these formations appears to be floating on the planetary ocean while the rest are below the surface. We suspect this is the work of alien life.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1302`

## ancrel.13005.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.13005.name&lang=en)

### 中文

冰山

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1208`

### English

Iceberg

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1304`

## ancrel.13005.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.13005.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

漂浮的冰山本身内置了一个巨大的黑色长方体，由与上方太空舰队残骸相同的合金制成。我们的冰川专家表示，尽管据称“很难用外星大气来判断”，冰山形成依然可以追溯到至少一千年前。结构内部的扫描显示有相当多的生物读数，因此不建议使用破坏性工具。\n\n团队将不得不穿越冰冻的悬崖，到达似乎是建筑物入口的地方。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1209`

### English

Built into the floating iceberg itself is a hulking black cuboid made out of the same alloys as the wreckage of the space fleet above. Our glacial specialists state that analysis of the iceberg dates its formation to least a thousand years ago, though it's supposedly "hard to tell with alien atmospheres". Scans of the interior of the structure show quite a few biological readings, therefore using disruptive tools is inadvisable.\n\nThe team will have to traverse the frozen cliffs to what appears to be an entrance into the building.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1305`

## acnrel.13005.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aacnrel.13005.a&lang=en)

### 中文

那就一路向下吧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1210`

### English

Down we go.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1306`

## ancrel.13010.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.13010.name&lang=en)

### 中文

霜冻之门

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1211`

### English

Frost Gate

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1308`

## ancrel.13010.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.13010.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在[From.Planet.GetName]的设施入口处建立了大本营。我们已经发现了一些看起来像是刻在建筑物墙壁上的文字。现在我们将开始在不会损坏里面任何东西的情况下打开这扇门的漫长而艰巨的过程。与此同时，我们的异种语言学家尝试翻译铭文中已失传的外星语言。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1212`

### English

We have set up base camp at the supposed entrance to the facility here on [From.Planet.GetName]. Already we have discovered something that looks like writing inscribed on the walls of the building. Now we will begin the long and arduous process of opening this door without damaging whatever is inside. At the same time, our xeno-linguists attempt to translate the dead alien language of the inscriptions.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1309`

## ancrel.13015.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.13015.name&lang=en)

### 中文

盗墓

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1213`

### English

Grave Robbing

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1311`

## ancrel.13015.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.13015.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

是时候了，我们已经准备好解封黑色结构，根据墙上铭文的初步翻译，它被称为“避难所”。这个避难所的创造者勾陈一星人将其作为保护财宝安全的最后手段。当我们打开门时，迎接我们的是——根据我们的传感器的数据——一种类似于外界空气的受控大气，只是二氧化碳要少得多。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1214`

### English

The time was upon us, and we stood ready to unseal the black structure, dubbed "The Vault" according to preliminary translations of the wall inscriptions. The creators of this vault, the Polarians, made it as a last resort to keep their treasures safe. As we opened the doors, we were greeted by - according to our sensors - a controlled atmosphere similar to the air outside, just with quite a bit less carbon dioxide.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1312`

## ancrel.13020.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.13020.name&lang=en)

### 中文

藏宝

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1215`

### English

Treasure

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1314`

## ancrel.13020.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.13020.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

远古种子！植物胚胎——那是勾陈一星人藏在这个金库里的宝藏。进一步的翻译告诉我们，这些外星人怪异地不断用过量二氧化碳填充他们的大气层。一旦气候发生不可逆转的变化，这个星球的古代居民就会收集他们所能找到的所有不能在新环境下生存的植物物质，并将它们全部冻结在这里。\n\n那时，整个星球都会被冰覆盖；现在，只剩下这片孤独的小冰山了。然而，他们的努力不会白费——拿到星球上的整个植物生态的编目和分类，将极大地帮助我们的科学家研究未来的生物问题。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1216`

### English

Ancient seeds! Plant embryos - that was the treasure the Polarians hid away in this vault. Further translations tell us that these aliens curiously kept filling their atmosphere with too much carbon dioxide. Once the atmosphere was irreversibly changed the ancient inhabitants of this world gathered all the plant matter they could find that might not survive their new status quo and froze them all in here.\n\nBack then, the whole planet would have been covered in ice; now, all that remains is this small lonely iceberg. However, their efforts will not have been wasted - having a planet's whole plant ecology already cataloged and sorted will significantly help our scientists in researching future biological matters.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1315`

## ancrel.13020.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.13020.a&lang=en)

### 中文

把这些样本发送给我们的科学家。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1217`

### English

Send these samples to our scientists.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1316`

## ancrel.13020.b.evopred

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.13020.b.evopred&lang=en)

### 中文

不仅编目了而且还便于克隆？这个地库简直是为我们建的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1218`

### English

Cataloged and ready for cloning? This vault was made for us.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1317`

## ancrel.13025.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.13025.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

有机微生物！所有有害和有益的细菌。所有真核生物和原核生物。所有这些都在高级存储设施中标记和分类：这是勾陈一星人藏在这个金库中的巨大财富。进一步的翻译告诉我们，这些外星人怪异地不断用过量二氧化碳填充他们的大气层。一旦大气发生了不可逆转的变化，这个星球的古代居民就会收集他们所能找到的所有不能在新环境下生存的微生物，并将它们全部冻结在这里。\n\n那时，整个星球都会被冰覆盖；现在，只剩下这片孤独的小冰山了。然而，他们的努力不会白费——拿到星球的整个微生物生态的编目和分类，将极大地帮助我们的[Root.Owner.getscientistplural]开发新星球以及改造殖民地。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1219`

### English

Microorganisms! Every harmful and helpful bacterium. Every eukaryote and prokaryote. All in an advanced storage facility labeled and cataloged: this was the great treasure the Polarians hid away in this vault. Further translations tell us that these aliens curiously kept filling their atmosphere with too much carbon dioxide. Once the atmosphere was irreversibly changed, the ancient inhabitants of this world gathered all the microbial life they could find that might not survive their new status quo and froze them all in here.\n\nBack then, the whole planet would have been covered in ice; now, all that remains is this small lonely iceberg. However, their efforts will not have been wasted - having a planet's whole microbial ecology already cataloged and sorted will significantly help our [Root.Owner.getscientistplural] develop new and improved colonies for new worlds.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1319`

## ancrel.13025.desc.machine

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.13025.desc.machine&lang=en)

### 中文

有机微生物。所有有害和有益的细菌，所有真核生物和原核生物。标记和编目：这是勾陈一星人藏在这个金库中的宝藏。进一步的翻译告诉我们，这些外星人怪异地不断用过量二氧化碳填充他们的大气层。一旦大气发生了不可逆转的变化，这个星球的古代居民就会收集他们所能找到的所有不能在新环境下生存的微生物，并将它们全部冻结在这里。\n\n那时，整个星球都会被冰覆盖；现在，剩下的就是这座孤独的小冰山。然而，感谢他们的努力——预先分类的生物将使提取相关信息变得更加容易。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1220`

### English

Microorganisms. Every harmful and helpful bacterium, every eukaryote and prokaryote. Labeled and cataloged: this was the great treasure the Polarians hid away in this vault. Further translations tell us that these aliens curiously kept filling their atmosphere with too much carbon dioxide. Once the atmosphere was irreversibly changed, the ancient inhabitants of this world gathered all the microbial life they could find that might not survive their new status quo and froze them all in here.\n\nBack then, the whole planet would have been covered in ice; now, all that remains is this small, lonely iceberg. However, their efforts are appreciated- pre-sorted biologicals will make it easier to extract relevant information.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1320`

## ancrel.13030.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.13030.name&lang=en)

令人不安的景象

### English

Disturbing Vision

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1322`

## ancrel.13030.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.13030.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

大前天晚上，首席考古学家[From.Leader.GetName]报告说看到了令人不安的景象，并且一直被关在自己的房间里直到现在。他们认为是我们这里的某种东西引发的……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1222`

### English

Three nights ago, chief archaeologist [From.Leader.GetName] reported having a disturbing vision and has been shut away in their quarters until now. They believed the source to be whatever is down here with us...

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1323`

## d_polaris_city_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ad_polaris_city_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

勾陈一星人的城市曾经在寒冰地基融化之前覆盖了冰冻的海洋。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1227`

### English

The cities of the Polarians once covered the frozen ocean before their icy foundations melted away.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1329`

## site_fumongus_dig

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asite_fumongus_dig&lang=en)

### 中文

脱水

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1229`

### English

Desiccated

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1332`

## site_fumongus_intro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asite_fumongus_intro&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在星球各地都发现了水的迹象。据我们所知，这些水应该还在这颗星球上。星球温度在液态水存在的正常范围内，并且星球仍然存在大气，尽管很脆弱。然而，这里的一切都是干燥的，存在的植被也枯死了。\n\n此外，扫描还揭示了行星上另一个星际文明的踪迹。考古团队将有两个目标：找出水发生了什么，并了解其他星际旅者在这里做了什么。大多数假设已经将两者联系起来，团队中的一些人认为这可能是“偷水贼”干的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1230`

### English

We have found signs of water all over this world. For all we know, it should still be here. The temperatures are within the normal range for liquid water to exist, and there is still an atmosphere, albeit tenuous. And yet, everything here is dry and what vegetation exists is parched.\n\nIn addition, scans have revealed traces of another spacefaring civilization on the planet. The archaeological team will have two goals: find out what happened to the water and understand what other spacefarers have been doing here. Most hypotheses are already linking the two and some in the team believe that this might have been the work of 'water thieves'.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1333`

## ancrel.8300.intro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.8300.intro&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的[Root.Owner.GetArchaeologistPlural]在[From.Planet.GetName]上进行的探险扰乱了一些当地野生动物腐烂的茧壳，它们现在似乎受到寄生真菌的影响。这些真菌僵尸立即开始袭击我们，显然是被我们营地和我们身体内部的水源所吸引。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1232`

### English

The expedition carried out by our [Root.Owner.GetArchaeologistPlural] on [From.Planet.GetName] has disturbed the decaying husks of some local wildlife, which seem now to be under the sway of parasitic fungi. These fungal zombies immediately began assaulting us, apparently drawn to sources of water in our camp and inside our very bodies.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1336`

## ancrel.8300.critical

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.8300.critical&lang=en)

### 中文

僵尸们在被我们的安全团队把它们烧光之前迅速地杀死了§H[dead_scientist.GetName]§!。干枯的遗骸就是他们遗留下来的全部了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1233`

### English

The zombies made short work of §H[dead_scientist.GetName]§! before they could be burned down by our security team. Their desiccated remains are all that is left of them. 

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1337`

## ancrel.8300.failure

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.8300.failure&lang=en)

### 中文

在被[Root.GetLeaderName]阻止之前，僵尸设法杀死了几个§H [Root.Owner.GetArchaeologistPlural]§!。当我们试图找回他们时，他们干枯的尸体已经变成碎片。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1234`

### English

The zombies managed to kill several [Root.Owner.GetArchaeologistPlural] before they could be stopped by §H[Root.GetLeaderName]§! Their desiccated corpses fell to pieces when we tried to recover them.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1338`

## ancrel.8300.success

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.8300.success&lang=en)

### 中文

幸运的是，§H[Root.GetLeaderName]§! 附近的保安护卫设法在僵尸对任何人造成严重伤害之前将其无害化。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1235`

### English

Luckily, §H[Root.GetLeaderName]§! and the nearby security escort managed to neutralize the zombies before they could seriously injure anyone.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1339`

## ancrel.8300.pay

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.8300.pay&lang=en)

### 中文

丧尸暂时被控制住了，但没有办法越过它们。也许我们可以用足够多的水把它们引出来。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1236`

### English

The zombies have been contained for now, but there is no way past them. Perhaps we can lure them out with a sufficiently vast amount of water.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1340`

## ancrel.8300.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.8300.name&lang=en)

### 中文

真菌僵尸

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1237`

### English

Fungal Zombies

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1341`

## ancrel.8300.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.8300.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$ancrel.8300.intro$\n\n$ancrel.8300.critical$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1238`
