## ancrel.13100.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.13100.name&lang=en)

### 中文

囤水者

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1245`

### English

Water Hoarders

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1351`

## ancrel.13100.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.13100.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在[From.Planet.GetName]上发现了充满蘑菇的巨大峡谷，这就是答案的开始。原来水在这些蘑菇里面。它们似乎扩张到了星球表面的很大一部分，并且都与同一个菌丝体相连，因此从技术上讲，它算是一个单一的有机体，也是我们见过的最大的有机体之一。该团队的一些成员将这种巨大的真菌称为“巨大蘑菇”。\n\n看起来蘑菇已经过基因改造，并完全接管了曾经存在于这里的生态系统。它们肆无忌惮地加速生长，以至于它们吸收了这里存在的大部分水。随后，它们进入了一种干枯的休眠状态，在这种状态下，大部分真菌仍然活着，水遍布整个生物体。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1246`

### English

The discovery on [From.Planet.GetName] of vast canyons filled with mushrooms provided us with the beginning of an answer. The water is inside the mushrooms. They seem to span a large part of the surface of the world, all connected to the same mycelium, which technically makes it a single organism, and one of the biggest we've ever seen. Some of the members of the team dubbed this humongous fungus the 'Fumongus'.\n\nIt looks like the mushrooms have been genetically altered and have completely taken over the ecosystem that once existed here. Their unbridled and accelerated growth was such that they absorbed most of the water that was present here. They subsequently entered a sort of dried-up dormant state in which most of the fungus is still alive, with water spread out across the whole organism. 

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1352`

## ancrel.13105.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.13105.name&lang=en)

### 中文

被摧垮的地堡

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1247`

### English

Overrun Bunker

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1353`

## ancrel.13105.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.13105.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在坐落在[From.Planet.GetName]峡谷中的菌丝森林的深处，我们发现了某种地堡，早已被蘑菇摧垮。该团队认为，它是由对这里的蘑菇进行基因改造的那个物种建造的。\n\n地堡的大部分已经倒塌，其中的许多设备可能已经损坏无法修复，但我们已经确定了一些可能装有可利用材料的密封房间。值得注意的是，这里没有发现外星人遗骸。也许他们放弃了这个地方，也或者全部被当地野生动物所吸收了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1248`

### English

Deep in the heart of the mycelial forests nestled in the canyons of [From.Planet.GetName] we discovered some sort of bunker, long overrun by mushrooms. The team believes that it was built by the same species that genetically altered the mushrooms here. \n\nLarge sections of the bunker have collapsed and a lot of the equipment within it is likely broken beyond repair, but we have identified some sealed rooms that might hold exploitable material. Notably, no alien remains have been found here. Maybe they abandoned the place, or else didn't leave behind any body parts that weren't absorbed by the local wildlife. 

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1354`

## ancrel.13110.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.13110.name&lang=en)

### 中文

真菌改造者

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1249`

### English

Fungal Tamperers

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1355`

## ancrel.13110.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.13110.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.Planet.GetName]上的地堡用于保护计算设备，其中一些似乎直接连接到菌丝体。值得庆幸的是，我们设法重新激活了几个终端和数据库。\n\n破解加密和解密所使用的语言需要时间，团队已要求派我们的几位顶级[Root.Owner.GetLinguists]来帮忙。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1250`

### English

The bunker on [From.Planet.GetName] was used to protect computing equipment, some of which appears to be directly connected to the mycelium. Thankfully, we managed to reactivate several terminals and databanks.\n\nBreaking the encryption and deciphering the language used will take time, and the team has requested that several of our top [Root.Owner.GetLinguists] be sent here to help.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1356`

## ancrel.13115.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.13115.name&lang=en)

### 中文

从蘑菇到代码

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1251`

### English

From Shroom to Code

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1357`

## ancrel.13115.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.13115.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们从[From.Planet.GetName]地堡中的数据库中找到了有价值的信息。数据库告诉我们，这是一种监听哨站。显然，外星人找到了一种方法来解释通过菌丝体传播的信号并将它们转化为可操作的情报。\n\n我们不明白这怎么实现的，但该团队现在将尝试从附近的冰小行星带运水过来再次“唤醒”巨大蘑菇，看看如果它有传输数据的话，传输的是什么样的数据。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1252`

### English

We have recovered valuable information from the databanks found in the bunker on [From.Planet.GetName]. The databanks tell us that this was a sort of listening post. Apparently, the aliens found a way to interpret the signals coursing through the mycelium and convert them into actionable intel.\n\nWe do not understand how this might have been possible, but the team will now try to bring water from nearby ice asteroids to 'awaken' the Fumongus again and see what kind data it transmits, if any.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1358`

## ancrel.13120.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.13120.name&lang=en)

### 中文

不再干燥

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1253`

### English

Dry No More

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1359`

## ancrel.13120.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.13120.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.Planet.GetName]上的蘑菇又开始繁茂了。在其他几艘飞船的帮助下，我们不得不将几颗冰小行星拖入轨道，然后将其中的大块降落到行星上的各个地方进行融化。这是一个漫长的过程，但它终于开始有结果了。\n\n有了这个，计算机开始吐出看起来非常随机的小块数据，直到我们从母星上分辨出信号。与[Root.Owner.Capital.GetName]的团队进行了一系列合作实验，唯一可能的结论是蘑菇对其他星球上发生的事情的有有限的了解。虽然我们检索到的数据尚不可利用，但预计继续尝试恢复蘑菇会给我们带来更多结果。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1254`

### English

The mushrooms on [From.Planet.GetName] are starting to flourish again. With the help of several other ships, we had to tow several icy asteroids into orbit before bringing down chunks of them to various places on the planet to melt. It has been long process, but it is finally beginning to bear fruit.\n\nWith this, the computers began to spit out small bits of data that seemed pretty random until we recognized activity from our home. In cooperation with teams on [Root.Owner.Capital.GetName], a series of experiments has been conducted and the only possible conclusion is that the mushroom has some limited awareness of what happens on other planets. While the data we have retrieved is not exploitable just yet, continued efforts to restore the mushrooms are expected to give us more results.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1360`

## ancrel.13135.intro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.13135.intro&lang=en)

### 中文

菌丝体监听阵列终于可以运行了。已经从轨道上带来了大量的水来满足一直很渴的蘑菇，并且对计算资源进行了重大升级。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1256`

### English

The Mycelium Listening Array is finally operational. A significant supply of water has been brought from orbit to sate the ever-thirsty mushroom, and major upgrades have been made to the computing resources.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1364`

## ancrel.13135.mid

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.13135.mid&lang=en)

### 中文

考虑到所传达的信息量，穿越网络的信号数量出人意料地低。最重要的理论假设巨大蘑菇存在于跨维度，在这些维度的距离与我们的习惯不同。\n在任何情况下，巨大蘑菇都可以与它跨越星系的后代接触，这些较小的殖民地能够传递数据，我们可以解析这些数据。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1257`

### English

The number of signals traversing the network proved surprisingly low, considering the quantity of information conveyed. The most prominent theory posits that the Fumongus exists across dimensions where distances are not what we are used to.\nIn any case, the Fumongus is in contact with its offspring across the galaxy, and these smaller colonies are able to relay data, which we can then interpret.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1365`

## ancrel.13135.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.13135.name&lang=en)

### 中文

巨大蘑菇又在倾听了

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1258`

### English

The Fumongus Listens Again

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1367`

## ancrel.13135.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.13135.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$ancrel.13135.intro$\n\n仍然存在一些谜团，毕竟我们仍然不确定蘑菇是如何设法从星系其他地方收集这么多数据的。$ancrel.13135.mid$\n\n随着网络再次运行，剩下的唯一决议就是是否将其用于监视。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1259`

### English

$ancrel.13135.intro$\n\nSome mysteries remain, as we are still not sure how the mushroom manages to collect that much data from the rest of the galaxy. $ancrel.13135.mid$\n\nWith the network working again, the only decision left to make is whether to use it for surveillance, or not.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1368`

## ancrel.13135.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.13135.a&lang=en)

### 中文

开始监视。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1260`

### English

Start monitoring.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1369`

## ancrel.13135.a.evopred

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.13135.a.evopred&lang=en)

### 中文

能监听还能汇报。也许我们也可以学学类似的事情。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1261`

### English

Monitor and report. Perhaps we can learn something similar.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1370`

## ancrel.13135.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.13135.b&lang=en)

### 中文

别管这个蘑菇了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1262`

### English

Let the mushrooms be.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1371`

## ancrel.13130.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.13130.name&lang=en)

### 中文

敬畏这个远古蘑菇

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1264`

### English

Fear the Old Shroom

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1375`

## ancrel.13130.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.13130.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$ancrel.13135.intro$\n\n然而，关于菌丝网络，我们仍然知之甚少。$ancrel.13135.mid$ 但是还有谁可以访问这个网络呢？有很多数据我们无法与这个星系中的恒星系相关联。我们更悲观的 [Root.Owner.GetScientistPlural] 认为我们可能不是唯一在倾听的人，通过重新激活网络，我们只是向使用它的所有人展示了我们自己。\n\n鉴于我们仍然不了解巨大蘑菇，以及之前接触过它的物种，最好不要管它。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1265`

### English

$ancrel.13135.intro$\n\nAnd yet, there is still so much we don't know about the mycelial network.  $ancrel.13135.mid$ But who else may have access to this network? There is a lot of data that we were not able to correlate to systems in this galaxy. Our more pessimistic [Root.Owner.GetScientistPlural] argue we might not be the only ones listening, and that by reactivating the network we just revealed ourselves to anyone using it.\n\nGiven all we still don't know about the Fumongus, or about the previous species that tapped into it, it might be best to just leave it alone..

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1376`

## ancrel.13130.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancrel.13130.a&lang=en)

### 中文

偏执的傻瓜。我们该使用它。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/ancient_relics_events_l_simp_chinese.yml:1266`

### English

Paranoid fools. Use it.

Source: `localisation/english/ancient_relics_events_l_english.yml:1377`
